0.2
+ + + + samājasya Mañjughoṣasya sādhanam |
grathitaṃ Jñānapādair yat tadvyākhyā samvidhīyate ||
1.0 [ 1a5--2a2] tatra tāvat sādhanaṃ cikīrṣur ayam ācāryo 'bhimatadevatāpūjayā puṇyavṛddhau pravartakasyāvighnenābhimatam icchaṃs tatpūjāpūrvakaṃ prekṣāvatpravṛttikā(*raṇam abhidheyādikam āha– śrīmantītyādi.)
*śrīmanty apāstasakalabhramanirmalāni
*tejāṃsy anantakulanirmitibhūṣaṇāni |
*prajñāṅgasaṅgarucirāṇi vibhānti yasya
*Mañjuśriyaṃ tam abhivandya tadarcanāya || 1
1.1 [ 2a2-3] (*iha bhagavataḥ pūjā stotreṇa praṇāmena ca. tatra) sadbhūtaguṇākhyānaṃ stotram. tac ca tejasāṃ viśeṣaṇadvāreṇa bhagavata eva kriyate, teṣāṃ tatsambandhitvāt, tattvatas tejastejasvinoś candracandrikayor iva bhedābhāvād vā.
1.2 tatra śrīḥ sāmānyena svaparārthasampadātmikā. parārthavāñchito + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + +
1.3 [ 2a3-4] (*athavā śrī)ḥ puṇyajñānasambhāraḥ. so 'sti yeṣāṃ tāni śrīmanti. anena svārthasampaddhetur uktaḥ, sarvākārajñatālakṣaṇasvārthasampadaḥ puṇyajñānahetukatvāt.
1.4 tayoś ca bhagavatsambandhinos tejasāṃ śrīmattvenopalakṣaṇāt phalāvasthāvyava + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + +
1.5 [ 2a4--2b1] (*a)pāstāḥ pradhvastāḥ sakalabhramā avidyālakṣaṇā yais tāni tathā. ata eva nirmalāni viśuddhāni savāsanasakalakleśādyāvṛtirahitānīty arthaḥ. anena tejasāṃ nirmalatvena nirmalasarvākārajñatāsūcanāt svārtha(*sampad uktā, śrāvakādibhyaś cātiśaya uktaḥ.)
1.6 + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + sūcayann āha– ananteti.
1.7 [ 2b1-2] anantānām ekakulādīnāṃ nirmitir vineyajanasantāneṣv āropaṇam. sā bhūṣaṇaṃ yeṣāṃ tāni tathā. etena parārthasampad uktā, tāyitvāt tadādhipatyena vā vineyānāṃ tadākārapratītisiddheḥ. + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + +
1.8 [ 2b2] (*prajñā niḥsvabhāvatālakṣaṇā vidyā.)
1.9 (*tasyāḥ prajñā)yā aṅgāni dānādayo viśuddhāḥ puṇyajñānasambhārarūpāḥ. yad uktam–
dānaṃ śīlaṃ ca puṇyasya prajñā jñānasya sambhṛtiḥ |
trayaṃ cānyaddvayasyārthe pañcāpi jñānasambhṛtiḥ ||
(Mahāyānasūtrālaṃkāra 18.39)
tathā–
imaṃ parikaraṃ sarvaṃ prajñārthaṃ hi munir jagau |
tasmād utpādayet prajñām
(Bodhicaryāvatāra 9.1abc)
iti. teṣāṃ saṅgaḥ svārthasampatteḥ kāraṇam. sambhārād dhi sarvākārajñatotpadyate. phalāvasthāyāṃ tv atra sambodhirūpaprajñāyā aṅgāni ṣoḍaśa śūnyatāprabhedāḥ.
1.10 [ 2b2-3] śleṣāt svābhavidyāvayavāḥ. teṣāṃ saṅga upāyena sahaikarasatā. satatānābhogavāhiniḥśeṣajagadupakārigambhīrodārarūpatety arthaḥ.
1.11 [ 2b3] tena rucirāṇy ujjvalāni spharaṇavanti ca . duḥkhād ?i ?ānir ata eva komalā madhurā śrīr asya. prajñāṅgasaṅgarucirāṇi.
1.12 [ 2b3-4] athavānena parārthasampaddhetur uktaḥ, parārthahetumahārāgopalakṣaṇatvāt. tataś ca Tattvasaṃgrahādyabhisambuddhebhyo viśeṣa uktaḥ.
1.13 [ 2b4] abhivandyety anena ca kāyādinā praṇāmapūjā kathitā.
1.14 [ 2b4-5] tato yasyaivambhūtāni tejāṃsi vibhānti bhāsante taṃ Mañjuśriyam abhivandya tasyaivārcanāya vidhiṃ vakṣya (see 2d) iti sambandhaḥ.
2.0 [ 2b5] asya vidheḥ prakarṣaṃ darśayitum āha– śrīmadityādi.
*śrīmatsamājanītyā
*sanmitraprārthanākṛtotsāhaḥ |
*sakalajagadarthasampan-
*nidānabhūtaṃ vidhiṃ vakṣye || 2
2.1 [ 2b5-6] śrīr lokasya laukikalokottarā sampattiḥ. sā kāryatayā yasyāsti sa śrīmāṃś ca Samājaś ceti, tasya nītir āmnāyas tayā.
2.2 [ 2b6-7] svaviśeṣaṇam āha– sanmitretyādi. sanmitraṃ guruḥ.
2.3 evaṃ hi śrūyate– gurubhiḥ Pālitapādaiḥ sādhanalikhanāya prārthanā kṛtā.
2.4 [ 3a2] tayā kṛtotsāho janitodyamo 'haṃ vakṣya iti. etenātmanaḥ sambhāvanayā samvāditvaṃ darśayati.
2.5 yad vā sanmitraṃ bhagavān eva. taṃ prārthayitum utsāhena kṛto grantha iti.
2.6 [ 3a2-5] sakaletyādinā vidhiṃ viśinaṣṭi. sakalā laukikalokottarā sambodhiparyantā jagato yārthasampat, tasyā nidānabhūtaṃ hetubhūtam iti. anena prekṣāvatpravṛttyaṅgaṃ prayojanādikaṃ darśitavān. tathā hy atra jagadarthasampat prakaraṇasyābhidheyaprayojanam. tannidānabhūto vidhir abhidheyaḥ.
tasyāvaiparītyena pratipādanaṃ prakaraṇakaraṇaśravaṇayoḥ prayojanam. tac cānena prakaraṇena sādhyata iti prakaraṇaprayojanayor upāyopeyalakṣaṇaḥ sambandho 'pi sāmarthyād ukta eveti.
3.0 [ 3a5-6] nanu bhagavadāgamād anyācāryopadeśād vā Mañjuśrīsādhanavidhijñānaṃ bhaviṣyati, tat kim aneneti śaṅkāyām asya sādhanasya viśeṣeṇa prayojanam āha– kṣaṇetyādi.
*kṣaṇasampad iyaṃ capalā
*sajjanasaṅgaḥ sudurlabho loke |
*prajñopāyapravaṇaṃ
*tac ceto 'daḥ paraṃ kartum || 3
3.1 [ 3a6--3b4] kṣaṇasyātmabhāvasya sampad aṣṭākṣaṇanirmuktatā. tatraite 'ṣṭāv akṣaṇāḥ– mithyādṛṣṭiḥ, narakeṣūpapattiḥ, preteṣūpapattiḥ, tiryakṣūpapattiḥ, buddhavacanāśravaṇam, pratyantajanapadeṣūpapattiḥ, indriyajaḍamūkatā, dīrghāyuṣkeṣu deveṣūpapattiḥ. seyaṃ dustyajāṣṭākṣaṇanirmokṣalakṣaṇā kṣaṇasampac capalā vināśinī.
sajjanānāṃ ca samyakSamājadeśakānāṃ saṅgaḥ suṣṭhu durlabho loke lokasya, ṣaṣṭhīsaptamyor abhedāt. uktaṃ ca–
sarvajñānāṃ kadā loke sambhavo jāyate na vā |
udumbarasyeva kusumaṃ kadācit karhicid bhavet ||
tato 'pi durlabhotpādo mantracaryānayasya hi |
yena sattvārtham atulaṃ kartuṃ śaktā hy anirvṛtāḥ ||
(Guhyasamājamaṇḍalavidhi 203–204)
iti. Samāje 'py uktaṃ mahāvajradhareṇa–
abhavyā bhagavantaḥ sattvā guhyapadārthasya tena samayena na bhāṣitam. (Guhyasamājatantra p. 109, ll. 6-7)
iti. tatraiva tadanantaraṃ tathāgatair apy uktam–
sāmānyākṣaraṃ kulaputrā yathā bhavadbhir na jñātaṃ na śrutaṃ tathāsmābhir api sarvatathāgataiḥ sarvabuddhabodhisattvaiś ca. (Guhyasamājatantra p. 109, ll. 18-19)
ityādi. anena mantranayotpādasya durlabhatvena taddeśakānām api durlabhatvaṃ darśitam.
3.2 [ 3b5-6] tad iti tasmāt, adaś capalakṣaṇasampadantargataṃ lokasya cetaḥ prajñopāyapravaṇaṃ gambhīrodārarūpaṃ śūnyatākaruṇāparavaśam iti yāvat, param iti śreṣṭham advaitarūpaṃ kartuṃ pūrvoktaṃ vidhiṃ (see 2d) vakṣya (see 2d) ity anenaiva sambadhyate.
3.3 [ 3b6-7] etad uktaṃ bhavati. yasmāt kṣaṇasampad iyaṃ sudurlabhā vinaśvarā ca gurūṇāṃ ca saṅgo durlabhaḥ, tasmād vistaragranthavyākhyānaśravaṇādau vighātaṃ sambhāvayan tanniṣedhena tac cetaḥ paraṃ prajñopāyapravaṇaṃ kartum etadanantaroktaṃ vidhiṃ vakṣye (see 2d). saṃkṣepeṇeti bhāvaḥ.
4.0 [ 3b7--4a2] idānīṃ sādhakasthānasādhyasampattiṣu vaktavyāsu siddhau sādhakasyaiva prādhānyāt prathamaṃ tasya siddhiyogyasya nirdeśaḥ. sādhyasya vāñchitatve 'pi hetupūrvakatvena parabhāvitvāt paścān nirdeśaḥ. tasmād yathā sādhako yogyo bhavati tathā tatretyādinā tāvad āryādvayenāha.
*tatra karuṇāprabhāvād
*utpāditabodhimānaso mantrī |
*tyāgādiguṇavibhūṣaṇa-
*gambhīranayādhimuktiś ca || 4
4.1 [ 4a2-3] tatreti vākyopanyāse. karuṇeti duḥkhād duḥkhahetor vā samuddharaṇakāmatālakṣaṇā gotrarūpaśokamūlalakṣaṇā, tasyāḥ prabhāvād iti guṇasambandhaḥ, tadākṣipto bodhicittādiguṇaḥ, tasmād utpāditaṃ bodhau buddhatve mānasaṃ cittaṃ yena sa tathā. mantrīti yogī prasādhayen Mañjughoṣākhyam (see 6d) iti sambandhaḥ.
4.2 [ 4a3-6] tathā cāvaśyam eva dayāvān janahitacikīrṣayā tadupāyānveṣaṇaparāyaṇo bhavati. tato 'yam uttamakaruṇo 'sarvajñatvena sarvākārato jagadarthaḥ kartuṃ na śakyata iti paśyan sarvākārajñatāyāṃ cittam utpādayati. sāpy upāyam antareṇāsambhāvinī. tatas tadupāyam abhyasyati. tato 'yam abhisambudhya dharmadeśanādinā janahitam āsādayatīti kramaḥ.
uktaṃ ca Prajñākaraguptena–
duḥkhahetos tathā duḥkhād viyogecchā pareṣu yā |
sā kṛpā tadvatas tena tadupāyārjane matiḥ ||
(Pramāṇavārttikālaṃkāra 377)
iti. ata eva
prāsaṅgikaṃ tasya tathāgatatvam
(Loc. class. untraced)
iti tatra tatroktam.
4.3 [ 4a6-7] tān evopāyān saṃkṣipya sādhakaviśeṣaṇatvenāha– tyāgetyādi. tyāgādayo dānapāramitādayaḥ, ta eva guṇyante 'bhyasyanta iti guṇāḥ, te vibhūṣaṇaṃ yasya sādhakasya sa tathoktaḥ. gambhīranaye mantranayayoge nairātmyarūpe 'dhimuktis tattvaniścayenābhilāṣo yasya sa tathā. tyāgādiguṇavibhūṣaṇaś ca gambhīranayādhimuktiś ceti samāsaḥ.
5.0 prastutalakṣaṇam āha – śrīmadityādi.
*śrīmatSamājamaṇḍala-
*vidhiśobhanasekasampadopetaḥ |
*vidhivallabdhānujño
*nijamantrārādhane dhīmān || 5
5.1 [ 4b1] śrīmāṃś ca kleśeṣv apramādatābhiprāyāt, Samājaś ca, tasyāmnāyena yo maṇḍalavidhis tena ye 'bhiṣekāḥ śobhanābhiṣekāḥ. śobhanatvaṃ ca sampūrṇavidhinā sadgurubhyo labdhatvāt. pūrṇabhāvanayā mārgāśrayaparāvṛttyartham uktam. teṣām sampadā, upeto yuktaḥ.
5.2 [ 4b1-2] vidhivad ity udakamukuṭādikrameṇa labdhā gurubhyo 'nujñā prajñājñānāt paraṃ vidyāvratādigrahaṇapuraḥsarī sarvānujñā yenāsau vidhivallabdhānujñaḥ.
5.3 [ 4b2-4] nijamantro mantradevatayor abhedāt sveṣṭadevatā. tasyārādhanaṃ sevādyupāyaiḥ prasādhanam. tatra dhīmān samayasamvarādinābhinnāṅgatāpratītivān. uktaṃ ca bhagavatā–
japen mantram abhinnāṅgaḥ
(Guhyasamājatantra 7.4c)
iti. athavā nijamantraḥ sveṣṭadevatāmantraḥ. tasyārādhanaṃ bhāvanādikramas tatra dhīmān acintyatādirūpeṇa mantradevatādāv abhedabuddhimān ity arthaḥ.
5.4 [ 4b4-6] iha tyāgādīnām ayogavyavacchedakatvena sādhakaviśeṣaṇatvād guhyajñatvayogatattvajñatvādikam api sādhakaviśeṣaṇam abhipretam. tad uktaṃ Bhadrapādaiḥ–
yogadhairyādiyukto 'pi proktair guṇair ananvitaḥ |
sattvārthe naiva bhavyo 'yaṃ sadbhis taiḥ syur guṇā hy amī ||
(Guhyasamājamaṇḍalavidhi 6)
iti.
6.0 [ 4b6-7] manorame hi sthāne sukhitasya cittaṃ samādhīyate, samāhitacitto yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti, yathābhūtaṃ prajānan vimucyata iti manasi niveśya manovilāsaviṣayābhyāsopalakṣaṇāya sthānaviśeṣaṇam āha– kvacid ityādi.
*kvacid amalasalilasampat-
*taruvararājīvirājitābhyāse |
*bhūbhṛti bhagavantam iti
*prasādhayen Mañjughoṣākhyam || 6
6.1 [ 4b7--5a1] kvacid ity anirdiṣṭaviśeṣe. amalasalilasampac ca taruvararājī ca tābhyāṃ virājitaḥ śobhito 'bhyāsaḥ saṃnihitaḥ pradeśo yasya sa tathā. tasmin bhūbhṛti parvate.
6.2 athavotsavatananopalakṣaṇam. anvarthas tu viśuddho (?) bhuvo bhūmijñānāni bibhartīti.
6.3 [ 5a1-4] bhagavantam iti–
bhañjanaṃ bhaga ity uktaṃ kleśamārādibhañjanāt |
prajñāvadhyās tu vai kleśās tasmāt prajñā bhagocyate ||
(Vajraśekhara 191b7)
iti vacanāt kleśaskandhamṛtyudevaputramārāṇāṃ bhañjanaṃ viśuddhaprajñāsvabhāvaṃ bhagas tadvān. athavā–
aiśvaryasya samagrasya rūpasya yaśasaḥ śriyaḥ |
jñānasyātha prayatnasya ṣaṇṇāṃ bhaga iti śrutiḥ ||
(Loc. class. untraced)
ity abhidhānam. aiśvaryarūpayaśaḥśrījñānaprayatnalakṣaṇaṣaḍartho bhagaḥ. sa yasyāsti tam.
6.4 [ 5a4-5] mañjur madhuraḥ sarvopatāpaharatvāt, ghoṣo vacanaṃ yasya tadākhyam itīha vakṣyamāṇakrameṇa prasādhayed iti prakarṣeṇa catuḥsandhyaṃ bhāvayed ity arthaḥ.
6.5 [ 5a5-6] etac ca Mañjuvajrayoginam adhikṛtya tatprasādhana(*m u)ktam. devatāntarayogināṃ tu svadevatāsādhanam ittham eva vijñeyam iti. Mañjuśrīmaṃkārādisthāne svasveṣṭadevatābījādiprakṣepa iti viśeṣaḥ. yad vakṣyati– cakraṃ tu tasya tat syād yas tanmadhyasthito bhūyād (see 157cd) iti.
7.0 [ 5a6] idānīṃ tām eva samādhitrayātmikāṃ bhāvanāṃ vaktum ādiyogārambhaṃ tāvad āha– prathamam ityādi.
*prathamaṃ hṛdīndumadhye
*prātaḥ sphuradamalaraśmitattvākhyam |
*maṃ vinyasya dayāvān
*kurvīta mukhādisaṃśuddhim || 7
7.1 [ 5a7--5b1] prathamam iti bhāvanārambhe, hṛdīti hṛdaye, indumadhya iti candramaṇḍalasyopari maṃkāraṃ vinyasya mukhādisaṃśuddhiṃ kurvīteti sambandhaḥ.
7.2 [ 5a6-7] prātar iti vyagrasya yoginaḥ prabhātasandhyāyām evāyaṃ viśeṣaḥ. bhāvanāmātravyāpārasya tu sādhakasya sandhyāntareṣv api sarvam etad dhyeyam iti. taṃ prati tu prātar iti, tadā prāyeṇa cittavaikalyābhāvāt.
7.3 [ 5a7--5b2] sphuranto 'malā raśmayo yasya maṃkārasya tad eva tattvākhyam aviparītarūpasaṃjñakaṃ mahārāgātmakamantranayasamutthatvāt sphuradamalaraśmitattvākhyaṃ mam iti gotrarūpamahākṛpādisvabhāvasatkuṅkumāruṇābhamaṃkāraṃ vinyasyety anena vinyāsasya dayāvastvahetutvam āha.
7.4 dayāvān iti bījaśaktijakaruṇātarumūlam āha. dayāyāś copalakṣaṇatvān maitryādayo 'py atra draṣṭavyāḥ.
7.5 etad uktaṃ bhavati. tato gotravaśād duḥkhadarśanādeś ca bodhicittasvabhāvaṃ mahākṛpādikaṃ saṃnihitaprajñāhetusamvalitaṃ ghanāyamānam āropitatathāgatācintyaśaktikaṃ bījabhāvam upagataṃ vajradharapraṇidhānādyākṣepataḥ sadgurusaṃnidhāne samupajātaśaktivaśān mahākṛpāṃ tathaiva janayatīti.
tathaiva ca maitrīṃ kṛpāpādapavṛddhyupāyabhūtām, duṣkaraviraheṇa sattveṣu sambuddhasatsukhasamyaksampādane ? jñā(?)kārāṃ tadāpyāyanād abhiṣekakalpām. suviśuddher bandho (?) viśuddhis tatropāyo viśeṣataś ca devatayeti vimarṣe sambhavam adhikṛtya harṣotpatter muditāṃ ca. tad uktam–
karuṇāseko maitrī
tadduḥkhe saukhyato vipulapuṣṭiḥ | śākhāvṛddhir viśadā
yonimanaskārato jñeyā ||
parṇatyāgādānaṃ
praṇidhīnāṃ saṃtater anucchedāt |
dvivi(*dhapratyayasiddheḥ
puṣpam abandhyaṃ phalaṃ cāsmāt ||
(Mahāyānasūtrālaṃkāra 17.39-40)
iti.) + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + (*pari)pākataḥ. sattvaparipākasya phalasyaiva lābhāt phalaṃ cāsmāt. iti karuṇātaruniṣpattiḥ. atra ca vyatirekaḥ–
mūlaṃ karuṇā na bhaved
duṣkaracaryāsahiṣṇutā na bhavet |
duḥkhākṣamaś ca dhīmān
sattvārthaṃ cintayen naiva ||
cintāvihīnabuddhiḥ
praṇidhānaṃ śuklajanmasu na (*kuryāt |
śubhajanmānanugacchan
sattvān paripācayen naiva ||
(Mahāyānasūtrālaṃkāra 17.37-38)
iti.) + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + kaś ca.
karuṇā kṣāntiś cintā
praṇidhānaṃ janma sattvaparipākaḥ |
(Mahāyānasūtrālaṃkāra 17.36ab)
mūlaṃ skandhaḥ śākhā pattraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ caiva.
karuṇātarur eṣa mahān
mūlādiḥ paścimāgraphalaḥ ||
(Mahāyānasūtrālaṃkāra 17.36cd)
kṛpayā sattvārthapravṛttasya duḥkhād api saukhyotpādād vipulapuṣṭir iti kṣāntilakṣaṇa + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + stāvaḥ. tad etad uttaratra yadīcchasītyādigīteś (see 46d passim) cāvasīyate, asatīnāṃ maraṇāsambhavāt. kṣāntilakṣaṇaskandhāder viśeṣaṇārthaṃ mukhādisaṃśuddhim ity āha.
7.5 [ 5b2-5] mukhaṃ trivimokṣamukhaṃ śūnyatānimittāpraṇihitalakṣaṇaṃ tad ādir yeṣāṃ kāyasmṛtyu(*pasthānādīnām) + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + (*yogī kurvīteti. tad atra bījanyāsānantaram uktā) viśuddhir Bhadrapādais tu pūrvāparabhāvāvirodham avadhārya sadopakāritvapratipādanāya

8.0 [ 5b5-6] tato gurubuddhabodhisattvānām agrataḥ pūjāpūrvakapāpadeśanādikaṃ vidheya(*m ity āha– saccandanetyādi.)

8.1 [ 5b6-7] + + + + + + + + + + (*saccandanādibhir digdhe pralipte sugandhikusu)mair abhikīrṇatvena parabhāgaḥ śobhā yatra dhyānālaye tasmin viniviṣṭaḥ. etac ca yathāsambhavaṃ samādhyanukūlasāmagryantaropalakṣaṇārthaṃ draṣṭavyam. sa evam upaviṣṭo matimān (see 9b) deśyadeśakādyabhedacittaḥ.

9.1 [ 6a2-5] ālambyeti manasā cintayi (*karmaṇo vā viśeṣaṇam.)

9.3 [ 6a5-6] kimarthaṃ vidadhīta? śubhābhivṛddhyartham. śubhasya puṇyasya vṛddhaye. tathā hi deśanayā pañcānantaryādikam api karma kṣīyate. uktaṃ ca–

10.0 [ 6a6–6b1] tato vyapagatasakalakalmaṣasya puṇyānumodanādinā puṇyaṃ vardhate. yathā saṃskṛte kṣetre bījam uptaṃ prarohati, sudhaute vastre kuṅkumarāgo jhaṭiti spaṣṭībhavati, tathā niṣpāpasya puṇyaṃ sādhayato nirvibandhaṃ śubhaṃ vardhate. ata evādau pāpadeśanām āha– (*yad ityādi.)
yad anādimati bhavaughe
samastasaṃkalpasaṃbhṛtaṃ kaluṣam |
tad deśayāmi vidhivan
mahākṛpāṇāṃ puraḥ sakalam || 10
10.1 [ 6b1-2] + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + ḥ sambhṛtam upārjitaṃ yat kaluṣaṃ pāpaṃ tat sakalaṃ mahākṛpāṇāṃ buddhānāṃ pura ity agrato deśayāmi.
10.2 vidhivad iti dṛḍhakalpanam api svakalpitatvenaivety arthaḥ.
11.0 [ 6b4] ādyaśabdasaṃgṛhīteṣv (see 9c) artheṣu puṇyānumodanāṃ tāvad āha– sambuddhetyādi.
sambuddhabodhisattvair
āryair anyaiś ca yat kṛtaṃ kuśalam |
anumodya tadavaśeṣaṃ
samyak pariṇāmayāmi sambodhau || 11
11.1 [ 6b4-6] sam(*buddhā Vairocanādayaḥ. bodhisattvāḥ Kṣitigarbhādayaḥ. āryā āryĀmṛtakuṇḍalyādayaḥ, teṣāṃ tantre buddhabodhi)sattvaśabdena cānirdeśāt, āryaśabdena ca vyapadeśāt. uktaṃ ca– āryĀmṛtakuṇḍalir vīra (see 89d) iti. anye VajrahūṃkāraHerukādayaḥ, teṣāṃ tribhir apy avyapadeśāt.
11.2 [ 6b6-7] atra buddhādibhedo 'pi na vāstavaḥ, teṣāṃ Vajradharasvabhāvatvāt. uktaṃ ca bhagavatā(*–
sa eva bhagavān pūrvārdhacandre niṣīdayām āsa. (untraced)
ityādi.) + + + + + + + + + + + + + + (*vyavahāramātreṇa) bhidyata iti, sarvavyavahārasiddher iti.
11.3 [ 6b7–7a2] athavā sambuddhāḥ samyaksambodhiprāptāḥ. bodhisattvāḥ pramuditādibhūmipraviṣṭāḥ, samyaksambodhyarthinaḥ. āryāḥ srotaāpannādiśrāvakapratyekabuddhāḥ. anye buddhādyabhiprasannāḥ, kuśalakarmakāriṇaḥ.
11.4 [ 7a2-4] tair yat kṛtaṃ kuśalaṃ tat sakalaṃ samyag iti parayā śraddhayānumodya tadavaśeṣam ity anumodanāsahagatakuśalam, anumodanājanitam iti yāvat. adhyāśayasampannasya hi samyakpuṇyānumodanayā puṇyaṃ pracīyate, cittapradhānatvāt puṇyapāpayoḥ.
tad etad anumodanayā sahagataṃ kuśalaṃ sambodhau pariṇāmayāmi siddhilakṣaṇasambodhinimittabhāvaṃ praṇidhānaśaktyānayāmīty arthaḥ.
12.0 [ 7a4-5] viśiṣṭaratnatrayaśaraṇagamanena puṇyasambhārasambhṛtaḥ sarvair adhṛṣyo bhavatīti prathamaṃ buddhaśaraṇagamanam āha– vilasadityādi.
vilasanmano'malendu-
prasādhitānantasatkṛpopāyān |
śaraṇaṃ prayāmi sugatān
ātmamanovartino nityam || 12
12.1 [ 7a5–7b2] vilasaditi samantabhadrasarvākāraprajñālakṣaṇaṃ manaḥ. tad eva jagadajñānatimiraharatvenāmalendusādṛśyād amalenduḥ, suviśuddhasvabhāvā prajñety arthaḥ. tena saṃśliṣṭatayā prasādhitātvena svacittābhinnasvabhāvān ity arthaḥ. nityam ity anavadhisaṃsāraṃ yāvat. na tu śrāvakayānikānām ivānupadhiśeṣe nirvāṇād iti.
13.0 [ 7b2] dharmaśaraṇagamanam āha– nirmuktetyādi.
nirmuktasakalakalpanam
aśeṣasatsattvasampadādhāram |
dharmaṃ prayāmi śaraṇaṃ
samastavastvekarasarūpam || 13
13.1 [ 7b2-4] nirmuktā nirastāḥ sakalakalpanā grāhyādilakṣaṇā yena taṃ dharmaṃ śaraṇaṃ prayāmīti sambandhaḥ. kīdṛśam? aśeṣetyādi. satsattvā bodhisattvāḥ, teṣām aśeṣā samyaksambodhiparyantā yā sampat, tasyā ādhārabhūtaṃ hetubhūtam. yato yathāvad dharmatattvabhāvanayā bodhisattvaiḥ sarvasampattayo labhyante.
13.2 tathā ca yathā hi yathā hi viśuddhaye dharmaṃ bhāvayanti bodhisattvās tathā tathā buddhaguṇodaya iti.
13.3 [ 7b4] samastavastvekarasarūpam iti svakalpanāmātrāvyatibhinnatvena sarvabhāvasamatāsvabhāvam.
14.0 [ 7b4-5] saṅghaśaraṇagamanam āha– samyagityādi.
samyaṅnirastabandhanam
uttamakaruṇāsamarpitaśrīkam |
muditādibhūpraviṣṭaṃ
gato 'smi śaraṇaṃ yatīśagaṇam || 14
14.1 [ 7b5–8a2] samyagiti bhūtārthapratipattyā svakalpanāmātratvenānyadho(?)kriyamāṇaśobhananirastaṃ bandhanaṃ rāgadveṣamohākhyaṃ yena, yena cottamakaruṇayā samarpitā sampāditā svapareṣāṃ śrīḥ sampattiḥ, yaś ca muditādiṣu pramuditādisādhumatīparyantāsu nava bhūmiṣu praviṣṭaḥ.
yato daśamyāṃ bodhisattvo buddha eveti tasya sambuddhasya ca samantaprabhāsādisambuddhabhūmilābhinaḥ pūrvasugataśabdenopādānaṃ (see 12c), tato daśamībhūmistho na gaṇe 'ntarbhāvyaḥ. uktaṃ ca–
daśamyāṃ bodhisattvo buddha eva na tu samyaksambuddhaḥ. (Pañcaviṃśatikā Prajñāpāramitā, p. xx)
iti. taṃ yatīśagaṇam iti yatayaḥ śrāvakapratyekabuddhādyāḥ, teṣām īśā bodhisattvāḥ, teṣāṃ gaṇaṃ śaraṇaṃ gato 'smīti sambandhaḥ. yad vā prāgvad buddhādīnām abhede 'pi sattvāśayānurodhamātreṇa buddhādibhedena śaraṇagamanam uktam.
15.0 [ 8a2] idānīṃ sambhārabhūmigataṃ bodhicittam āha– āśayetyādi.
āśayavipākaśuddhyā
sarvāvṛtivāsanāsamudghāti |
utpādayāmi bodhāv
adhimuktivibhūṣaṇaṃ cetaḥ || 15
15.1 [ 8a2-5] āśayaśuddhiḥ sarvasattvābhyuddharaṇabuddhiḥ. vipākaśuddhir uttaratrāpi nirmalakarmaphalānubandhaḥ. tayāśayavipākaśuddhyā sambhārasthiter āha– bodhāv iti. bodhau buddhatve cetaś cittam utpādayāmīti sambandhaḥ.
sarvā cāvṛtīnāṃ kleśādyāvaraṇādīnāṃ kleśādyākārajananaśaktilakṣaṇā vāsanā ca sā tathā, tāṃ samuddhartuṃ śīlaṃ yasya tat sarvāvṛtivāsanāsamudghāti cetaḥ.
15.2 adhimuktir mantranītāv adhimokṣita ācāraḥ, sā vibhūṣaṇaṃ viṣayatvena vedanād yasya,
15.3 [ 8a5] yad vādhimuktis tattvaniścayena tatrābhilāṣaḥ, saiva vibhūṣaṇaṃ yasya,
15.4 [ 8a5-6] tat tathābhūtaṃ ceta utpādayāmi. na yadṛcchā vicikitsādyupahatam, kiṃ tv adhimuktyaiva vibhūṣitam iti.
16.0 [ 8a6] yenedaṃ praṇihitaṃ sidhyati, taṃ sambhāram eva mārgarūpam āha– sasutetyādi.
sasutasugataikamārgaṃ
daśavidhadānādiśuklaguṇarūpam |
sambuddhātmasamasta-
svabhāvabuddhyā samāśrito 'smy adhunā || 16
16.1 [ 8a6–8b1] sasutā bodhisattvasamanvitā ye sugatāḥ, teṣāṃ ya ekaḥ sādhāraṇo mārgaḥ, daśavidhā ye dānādayo jñānapāramitāparyavasānāḥ, ta evopalambhamalarahitatvena śuklaguṇāḥ, te rūpaṃ svabhāvo yasya mārgasya sa tathā. taṃ samāśrito 'smīty arthaḥ.
16.2 aprastutim āśaṅkyāha– sambuddheti sambuddhātmakaḥ samasto hetuphalāvasthayoḥ svabhāvo yasya tasya buddhyā devatākāraśleṣadhiyety arthaḥ.
16.3 [ 8b1-2] athavā sambuddho Vajradharo 'cintyatātmakaḥ, sa evātmā yasya samastasvabhāvasya sarvātmakasya bhūtabhautikarūpatrailokyaviśuddhisvabhāvasyādhārādheyātmakamaṇḍalacakrasya tasya buddhyā tad eva maṇḍalacakraṃ mārgasatyam iti niścayena mārgam āśrito 'smīty arthaḥ.
16.4 [ 8b2-4] idaṃ ca mārgāśrayaṇacittaṃ tattvataḥ praṇidhibodhicittasvabhāvam eveti bhedenoktayoḥ praṇidhiprasthānacittayor madhye mārgacittasya nirdeśād antarbhāvajñāpanaṃ kṛtam ācāryeṇa.
tathāpi mārgacittaṃ pṛthag avaśyaṃ nirdeśyam iti cābhisaṃhitam asya, yato 'syaivābhiprāyasya prakaṭanāya Bhadrapādair mārgāśrayaṇam abhidhāya sāmānyabodhicittam uktam iti.
17.0 [ 8b4-5] kṛtapraṇidhāno mārgam āśritya yogī mantrasiddhau prasthito bhavatīti jñānasambhārārthaṃ prasthānabodhicittam āha– kṛpayetyādi.
kṛpayāvalambya sakalaṃ
lokam imaṃ dṛṣṭijālapariṇaddham |
sambodhicittam atulaṃ
vibhāvya vidhineti mantrī syāt || 17
17.1 [ 8b5–9a2] sarvatra kṛpaiva pradhānam iti jñāpanāya kathitāpi kṛpā prasthāne punar uktā. tayā lokam avalambya dṛṣṭijālapariṇaddham iti. dṛṣṭayaḥ satkāyadṛṣṭyādyāḥ, tāsāṃ jālaṃ samūhaḥ, tā eva vā jālaṃ tatsādharmyāt, tena pariṇaddham iti baddham. bhrāntimātreṇa saṃsāriṇam iti yāvat.
sambodhicittam iti samyaksambodhirūpaṃ prasthānabodhicittam, sarvadharmanairātmyajñānam iti yāvat, atulam iti prasthānarūpatvāt tulārahitam, itīti śūnyaṃ svabhāvavirahād (see 18a) ityādivakṣyamāṇavidhinā vibhāvya mantrī syād iti oṃ śūnyatetyādimantroccāraṇavān (see 18') bhavet.
athavā tadanu śaradindudhavalām (see 19a) ityādivakṣyamāṇakrameṇa mantradevatām ārādhayed ity arthaḥ.
18.0 [ 9a2] tatredānīṃ prasthānabodhicittabhāvanāvidhim āha– śūnyam ityādi.
śūnyaṃ svabhāvavirahād
dhetuviyogāt tathānimittaṃ tu |
ūhāpagamād akhilaṃ
vastu praṇidhānanirmuktam || 18
18.1 śūnyaṃ svo bhāvo yasya yad rūpaṃ prasiddhaṃ tasya lakṣaṇāyogād virahas tataḥ.
18.2 [ 9a2-4] athavā svabhāvasya grāhyagrāhakaikānekādirūpasya virahāc chūnyam akhilaṃ vastu. tathā hetor vicārasahasya viyogād animittam. vyāvahārikas tu hetur na niṣidhyata eveti tunā darśayati. ūho devamanuṣyādigatisukhādivitarkaḥ. tasyāpagamāt praṇidhānanirmuktam akhilaṃ vastu.
viśiṣṭagatibhogaiśvaryādyabhilāṣākāraṃ praṇidhānaṃ tena nirmuktam ity arthaḥ.
18.3 [ 9a4-7] etac ca trividham api vimokṣamukhaṃ sarvapadānāṃ paramārthataḥ śūnyatvena siddhaṃ grāhyagrāhakādirahitaṃ hi sarvam eva vastujātam. yad vakṣyaty atraiva– rūpādyākṛtimuktam (see 110a) ityādi, ekaṃ na cetyādinā (see 113a) vaikānekādisvabhāvarahitaṃ ca sarvam ity atraiva pratipādayiṣyati. uktaṃ ca Vārttikakṛtā–
bhāvā yena nirūpyante tad rūpaṃ nāsti tattvataḥ |
yasmād ekam anekaṃ vā rūpaṃ teṣāṃ na vidyate ||
(Pramāṇavārttika Pra° 360)
iti. tasmān niḥsvabhāvatvāc chūnyam.
18.4 [ 9a7–9b1] ata eva hetupratyayāyogenādyanutpannatvād animittam. yad uktaṃ pratyayaparīkṣāyām–
naivāsato naiva sataḥ pratyayo 'rthasya yujyate |
asataḥ pratyayaḥ kasya sataś ca pratyayena kim ||
(Mūlamadhyamakakārikā 1.6)
iti. ācāryaDharmakīrtināpy uktam–
niṣpatter aparādhīnam api kāryaṃ svahetunā | sambadhyate kalpanayā
(Pramāṇavārttika Pra° 26abc)
iti. tathā–
parāpekṣā hi sambandhaḥ so 'san katham apekṣate |
saṃś ca sarvanirāśaṃso bhāvaḥ katham apekṣate ||
(Sambandhaparīkṣā 3)
ityādi.
18.5 [ 9b2-4] tata evāpraṇihitam. na hi niḥsvabhāve hetuphalabhāvāyogini praṇidhānam upapadyate. tathā hy asato vandhyāsutasyeva na hetur yuktaḥ, sataś ca vyarthaḥ, hetuśūnyasya ca na sattvam upapadyate. tato māyopamam anādyantam avicāraramyaṃ pratibhāsamātraṃ sarvaṃ padārthajātaṃ taccam (?) iti sthitam.
etac copapattyā yathāvasaraṃ pratipādayiṣyāmaḥ.
18.6 atyantaśūnyatā cānupakāriṇīti tatparihāreṇa svalakṣaṇakarmalakṣaṇabhedalakṣaṇahetulakṣaṇair yathākramaṃ vikalpottrāsakausīdyavicikitsopaśamāya śūnyatābhāvanā vijñeyā.
tathā hy adhyāropāpavādapratipakṣataḥ svalakṣaṇena–
dvayābhāvo hy abhāvasya bhāvaḥ śūnyasya lakṣaṇam |
(Madhyāntavibhāga 1.13ab)
iti. tathā cātra Bhāṣyam–
dvayasya grāhyagrāhakasyābhāvaḥ, tasya cābhāvasya bhāvaḥ śūnyatāyā lakṣaṇam. ity abhāvabhāvalakṣaṇatvaṃ śūnyatāyāḥ paridīpitaṃ bhavati. yaś cāsau tadabhāvasvabhāvaḥ sa (Madhyāntavibhāgabhāṣya ad loc.)
na bhāvo nāpi cābhāvaḥ (Madhyāntavibhāga 1.13c)
kathaṃ na bhāvaḥ? yasmād dvayasyābhāvaḥ. kathaṃ nābhāvaḥ? yasmād dvayābhāvasya bhāvaḥ. etac ca śūnyatālakṣaṇam. tasmād abhūtakalpān (Madhyāntavibhāgabhāṣya ad loc.)
na pṛthaktvaikalakṣaṇam ||
(Madhyāntavibhāga 1.13d)
pṛthaktve sati dharmād anyā dharmateti na yujyate, anityatāduḥkhatāvat. ekatve sati viśuddhyālambanaṃ na syāt, sāmānyalakṣaṇaṃ ca. etena tattvānyatvavinirmuktalakṣaṇaṃparidīpitaṃ bhavati. (Madhyāntavibhāgabhāṣya ad loc.)
iti. karmalakṣaṇena paryāyatadarthābhyām–
tathatā bhūtakoṭiś cānimittaṃ paramārthatā | dharmadhātuś ca paryāyāḥ śūnyatāyāḥ samāsataḥ ||
ananyathāviparyāsatannirodhāryagocaraiḥ |
hetutvāc cāryadharmāṇāṃ paryāyārtho yathākramam ||
(Madhyāntavibhāga 1.14-15)
iti. tathā cātraiva Bhāṣyam–
kathaṃ paryāyārtho vijñeyaḥ? ananyathetyādi. ananyathārthena tathatā, nityaṃ tathaiveti kṛtvā. aviparyāsārthena bhūtakoṭiḥ, viparyāsāvastutvāt. nimittanirodhārthenānimittam, sarvanimittābhāvāt. āryajñānagocaratvāt paramārthaḥ, paramajñānaviṣayatvāt. āryadharmahetutvād dharmadhātuḥ, āryadharmāṇāṃ tadālambanaprabhavatvāt.
hetvartho hy atra dhātvarthaḥ. (Madhyāntavibhāgabhāṣya ad loc.)
iti. bhedalakṣaṇena–
saṃkliṣṭā ca viśuddhā ca samalā nirmalā ca sā |
abdhātukanakākāśaśuddhivac chuddhir iṣyate ||
(Madhyāntavibhāga 1.16)
iti. atra Bhāṣyam–
kathaṃ śūnyatāyāḥ prabhedo vijñeyaḥ? saṃkliṣṭā ca viśuddhā cety asyāḥ prabhedaḥ. kasyām avasthāyāṃ saṃkliṣṭā? kasyāṃ viśuddhā? samalā nirmalā ca sā. yadā saha malena vartate tadā saṃkliṣṭā. yadā prahīṇamalā tadā viśuddhā.
yadi samalā bhūtvā nirmalā bhavati, kathaṃ vikāradharmitvād anityā na bhavati? yasmād asyāḥ– abdhātukanakākāśaśuddhivac chuddhir iṣyate. āgantukamalāpagamān na tu tasyāḥ svabhāvānyathātvaṃ bhavati. (Madhyāntavibhāgabhāṣya ad loc.)
iti. vicikitsāhānaye hetulakṣaṇena–
saṃkliṣṭā ced bhaven nāsau muktāḥ syuḥ sarvadehinaḥ | viśuddhā ced bhaven nāsau vyāyāmo niṣphalo bhavet ||
(Madhyāntavibhāga 1.21)
iti. tathā cātraiva Bhāṣyam–
kathaṃ sādhanaṃ vijñeyam? saṃkliṣṭetyādi. yadi sarvadharmāṇāṃ śūnyatāgantukair upakleśair anutpanne 'pi pratipakṣe na kliṣṭā bhavet, saṃkleśābhāvād ayatnata eva muktāḥ sarvasattvā bhaveyuḥ. athotpanne 'pi pratipakṣe na viśuddhā bhavet, mokṣārtham ārambho vyarthaḥ syāt. evaṃ ca kṛtvā (Madhyāntavibhāgabhāṣya ad loc.)
na kliṣṭā nāpi cākliṣṭā śuddhāśuddhā na caiva sā |
(Madhyāntavibhāga 1.22ab)
kathaṃ na kliṣṭā nāśuddhā? prakṛtyaiva. (Madhyāntavibhāgabhāṣya ad loc.)
prabhāsvaratvāc cittasya
(Madhyāntavibhāga 1.22c)
kathaṃ ca nākliṣṭā na śuddhā? (Madhyāntavibhāgabhāṣya ad loc.)
kleśasyāgantukatvataḥ ||
(Madhyāntavibhāga 1.22d)
iti. bhāvābhāvobhayapṛthaktvāpṛthaktvagrāhātmako vikalpaḥ. śūnyatālakṣaṇagrahaṇānadhimuktānāṃ tūttrāsaḥ svalakṣaṇakarmalakṣaṇatuṣṭānām alasānāṃ kausīdyaṃ syāt.
ādhārabhedāt kleśaviśuddhibhedo dehinaḥ. (untraced)
iti vacanāt. kathaṃ prabhāsvaratvam āgantukatvaṃ ceti vicikitsā. ity evaṃ Muktibindukathitaprakārair api śūnyatābhāvanā draṣṭavyā.
18.7 ayam aparaḥ prabhedaḥ. pudgaladharmavivekaikalakṣaṇāpi ṣoḍaśavidhā śūnyatā. tathā hy
ādhyātmikānāṃ cakṣurādīnām adhyātmaśūnyatā. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya §7a, 298b4, 146b24)
tad uktam ācāryeṇa–
adhyātmaviparyāsapratikūlatayādhyātmaśūnyatā. (Ātmasādhanāvatāra 57b2)
iti.
bāhyānāṃ rūpādīnām upalambhāpagamād bahirdhāśūnyatā. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya §7b, 298b4-5, 146b24-25; Ātmasādhanāvatāra 57b2)
cakṣurādirūpāditajjñānānāṃ cābhigamasyādhyātmabahirdhāśūnyatā. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya §7c, 298b5, 146b25-26)
tad uktam–
tadāśrayaśarīrānupalabdhisvabhāvatayādhyātmabahirdhāśūnyatā. (Ātmasādhanāvatāra 57b2-3)
iti.
digādyavayavabhāsabhedena mahato bhājanalokasya mahāśūnyatā. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya §7d, 298b5, 146b26-27)
tad uktam ācāryaVasubandhunā–
pratiṣṭhāvastu bhājanalokas tasya vistīrṇatvāt tacchūnyatā mahāśūnyatety ucyate. (Madhyāntavibhāgabhāṣya ad 1.17)
yathāparikalpaṃ grāhyādiviviktatvāc chūnyatāyām api śūnyatā śūnyatāśūnyatā. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya §7e, 298b5-6, 146b27-28)
tad uktam Ātmasādhanāvatāre–
śūnyatājñāne 'py animittatvāc chūnyatāśūnyatā. (Ātmasādhanāvatāra 57b5)
iti. asyāyam arthaḥ. yena jñānenādhyātmādicaturvidhaśūnyatā jñāyate tatrāpy animittatvād avikalpanāc chūnyatāyāḥ śūnyatādhyavasāyasya śūnyatā śūnyatāśūnyatā.
ācāryaVasubandhunāpy uktam–
tac cādhyātmikāyatanādi yena śūnyaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ śūnyatājñānena tasya śūnyatā. (Madhyāntavibhāgabhāṣya ad 1.17)
iti.
paramārthasyāpi paramārthaśūnyatā. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya §7f, 298b6, 146b28-29)
yad uktam–
yathā ca dṛṣṭaṃ paramārthākāreṇa tasya śūnyatā. (Madhyāntavibhāgabhāṣya ad 1.17)
iti. uktaṃ cācāryeṇaiva–
paramārthārthanimittāparāmarśāt paramārthaśūnyatā. (Ātmasādhanāvatāra 57b5)
iti. asyāyam arthaḥ. paramo 'kṛtrimo 'rtho 'syeti paramārtho yasya dṛṣṭārtho dharmadhātuḥ. sa eva nimittaṃ yasya tadadhyavasāyasya tasyādhyavaseyānadhyavasāyarūpatayātmanas tattvato bhedāt parāmarśāt paramārtha iti vikalpenāpi śūnyatvāt paramārthaśūnyatā.
saṃskṛtasyāpy abhisamayasya dānādeḥ saṃskṛtaśūnyatā. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya §7g, 298b6, 146b29–c1)
tad uktaṃ Bhāṣyakṛtā–
yadarthaṃ (1.17d) ca bodhisattvaḥ pratipadyate tasya śūnyatā (1.17d). kimarthaṃ ca pratipadyate? śubhadvayasya prāptyarthaṃ (1.18a) kuśalasya saṃskṛtāsaṃskṛtasya. (Madhyāntavibhāgabhāṣya ad 1.17-18)
iti. Ātmasādhanāvatāre 'py uktam–
saṃskṛtapuṇyasambhāre nimittāparigrahāt saṃskṛtaśūnyatā. (Ātmasādhanāvatāra 57b5-6)
iti. ayam arthaḥ. saṃskṛtaṃ dānādibhir aśeṣasattvārthakaraṇena sarvatathāgatapūjayā ca cittasya saṃskaraṇāt, pavitrīkaraṇena ca puṇyaṃ, santatyā bhāvanām āgamya bhūyo bhūyaḥ śubhasya ya āhāraḥ samutpādaḥ sa sambhāraḥ. tad uktam–
santatyā bhāvanām etya bhūyo bhūyaḥ śubhasya hi |
āhāro yaḥ sa sambhāro dhīre sarvārthasādhake || (Mahāyānasūtrālaṃkāra 18.40)
iti. saṃskṛtapuṇyasambhāre niṣpādyamāne nimittasya tatsaṃskāravikalpasyātmano bhedenāparigrahāt saṃskṛtaśūnyatā.
asaṃskṛtasyāpy anābhogānimittāvāhino 'saṃskṛtaśūnyatā. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya §7h, 298b6-7, 146c1)
tad uktam Ātmasādhanāvatāre–
asaṃskṛte 'pi bodhisambhāre nimittānudgrahād asaṃskṛtaśūnyatā. (Ātmasādhanāvatāra 57b6)
iti. asaṃskṛte 'pīti prakṛtipariśuddhatvāj jñānasya vibandhamalānām āgantukatvena nirākaraṇe jñānasya samvedanarūpatvāt. nirabhragaganamaṇḍale dinakarakaranikara iva sakalajñeyavyāpijñānālokasya pravṛtter yathāvat sarvadharmāvabodho bodhiḥ. tatsambhāro jñānasambhāraḥ. tad uktam–
nāpaneyam ataḥ kiṃcit prakṣeptavyaṃ na kiṃcana |
draṣṭavyaṃ bhūtato bhūtaṃ bhūtadarśī vimucyate ||
(Abhisamayālaṃkāra 5.21)
iti. tatra nimittam asaṃskṛtam iti vikalpas tasyānudgrahād uddhṛtya bhedenāgrahād asaṃskṛtavāsanā śūnyāsaṃskṛtaśūnyateti.
atyantasattvārthapraṇidhānānabhiveśād atyantaśūnyatā. (Ātmasādhanāvatāra 58a1)
iti. ayam arthaḥ. antam atikrāntāḥ sattvās teṣām arthaḥ sambodhiparyavasānaḥ, tatra praṇidhānāni, teṣv aviparyastatvenānabhiniveśād atyantaśūnyatā. tad uktam–
sadā sattvahitāya ca |
(Madhyāntavibhāga 1.18b)
iti. Bhāṣyaṃ cātra–
atyantasattvahitārtham (Madhyāntavibhāgabhāṣya ad loc.)
iti.
anavarāgro mayā saṃsāro na parityaktavya iti praṇidhānānavagrahād anavarāgraśūnyatā. (Ātmasādhanāvatāra 58a1-2)
ayam arthaḥ. na vidyate 'varam ādir agram anto yasya saṃsārasya sa tathā. sattvān adhikṛtyāpratiṣṭhitanirvāṇatayā na parityaktavya iti praṇidhānasyānavagrahād anabhiniveśād anavarāgraśūnyatā. tad uktam–
saṃsārātyajanārthaṃ ca
(Madhyāntavibhāga 1.18c)
iti. tathā cātra Bhāṣyam–
anavarāgrasya hi saṃsārasya śūnyatām apaśyan khinnaḥ san saṃsāraṃ parityajet. (Madhyāntavibhāgabhāṣya ad loc.)
iti.
nirupadhiśeṣe nirvāṇe mayā kuśalaṃ nāvakīraṇīyam ity anabhiniveśād anavakāraśūnyatā. (Ātmasādhanāvatāra 58a2-3)
ayam artho 'sya. upadhayaḥ skandhāḥ kleśāś ca, teṣāṃ śeṣo vāsanā, tadrahite savāsanakleśādyāvaraṇavimukte nirvānti sakalavikalpā asminn iti nirvāṇe tāthāgatadharmadhātau, mayā kutsitāt pāpāc chalati dūrībhavatīti kuśalam aparimitajanaparivarjanacittotpādādiyāvatsambodhiprāpaṇalakṣaṇaṃ nāvakīraṇīyaṃ na vikṣeptavyam ity anabhiniveśād
anavakāraśūnyatā. tad uktam–
kuśalasyākṣayāya ca ||
(Madhyāntavibhāga 1.18d)
tathā cāsya Bhāṣyam–
nirupadhiśeṣe nirvāṇe yan nāvakirati notsṛjati. (Madhyāntavibhāgabhāṣya ad loc.)
iti.
prakṛtilakṣaṇaṃ vā gotraṃ mayā śodhanīyam iti prārthanāvirahāt prakṛtiśūnyatā. (Ātmasādhanāvatāra 58a3)
ayam arthaḥ. pūrvajanmaparamparāpratibaddhaṃ pratyutpanne śubhaṃ prakṛtir dharmadhātus tallakṣaṇaṃ sambuddhagotram evānyasyātrānupayogāt. vā samuccaye. pariniṣpannasya śodhyatvān mayā śodhanīyam iti prārthanāyāḥ praṇidhānasya tattvato virahāt. tad uktam–
gotrasya ca viśuddhyartham
(Madhyāntavibhāga 1.19a)
iti. uktaṃ ca Bhāṣyakṛtā–
gotraṃ hi prakṛtiḥ svābhāvikatvāt. (Madhyāntavibhāgabhāṣya ad loc.)
iti.
lakṣaṇavyañjanātmani rūpakāye 'nabhisaṃskārāl lakṣaṇaśūnyatā. (Ātmasādhanāvatāra 58a5)
lakṣaṇavyañjanātmani sāmbhogikakāye paramārthato 'nabhisaṃskārāl lakṣaṇaśūnyatā. lakṣaṇasya copalakṣaṇatvenānuvyañjanasya ca parigraha iti. tad uktam–
lakṣaṇavyañjanāptaye |
(Madhyāntavibhāga 1.19b)
iti. Bhāṣyaṃ ca–
mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇānāṃ sānuvyañjanānāṃ prāptaye. (Madhyāntavibhāgabhāṣya ad loc.)
iti.
balavaiśāradyādisvabhāvadharmānabhisaṃskaraṇāt sarvadharmaśūnyatā. (Ātmasādhanāvatāra 58a5-6)
balavaiśāradyādīti smṛtyupasthānam ārabhya yāvad aṣṭādaśāveṇikasvabhāvabuddhadharmāṇāṃ tathānabhisaṃskaraṇāt sarvadharmaśūnyatā. tad uktam–
śuddhaye buddhadharmāṇāṃ bodhisattvaḥ prapadyate ||
(Madhyāntavibhāga 1.19cd)
iti. Bhāṣyaṃ ca–
balavaiśāradyādīnām iti. evaṃ tāvac caturdaśānāṃ śūnyatānāṃ vyavasthānaṃ veditavyam. (Madhyāntavibhāga ad loc.)
pudgaladharmābhāvas tasya śūnyatābhāvaśūnyatā. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya §7p, 299a2-3, 146c8-9 (?))
tad uktam Ātmasādhanāvatāre–
pudgaladharmāsamāropaṇād abhāvaśūnyatā. (Ātmasādhanāvatāra 58a6)
pudgalo Vaibhāṣikakalpitaḥ. upalakṣaṇād ātmajīvādeś ca parigrahaḥ. dharmo rūpādiḥ. tayoḥ kūrmaromalakṣaṇayor atattvadṛśāṃ bhrameṇāropasambhave tadviparyayeṇāsamāropād abhāvaśūnyatā.
sa cāyam abhāvo na skandhādibhyo 'nyo 'pi tu ta eva saṃkalpapratibhāsalakṣaṇā grāhakādiviviktā ity abhāvasvabhāvaśūnyatā. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya §7r, 299a3-4, 146c10-12)
tad uktam ācāryeṇaiva–
yathoktaśūnyatālakṣaṇānapavādād anabhisaṃskaraṇāc cābhāvasvabhāvaśūnyatā. (Ātmasādhanāvatāra 58a6-7)
yathoktety adhyātmādiśūnyatāḥ, tāsāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ samāropitadharmavivekaḥ, tasyāpy apavādāc cakṣurāder abhāve tacchūnyatāpi nāsty eveti śūnyatānirāsāt pudgalādeḥ pūrvavad bhāvaprasaṅga ity āśayaḥ. abhāvasya caivaṃbhūtasya dharmadhātoḥ sadbhāvo 'rvāgdṛśām aviṣayaḥ.
sthitaivaiṣā dharmāṇāṃ dharmatā. (Loc. class. untraced)
ity āgamāt. prakṛtipariśuddhatayā ca saṃskaraṇābhāvāt tadātmikābhāvasvabhāvaśūnyatā.
kā punar atra śūnyatā? (Madhyāntavibhāgabhāṣya intro to 1.20)
iti śaṅkāṃ vyudasyatā bhagavatā Maitreyeṇa dayāvatābhāvaśūnyatā kathitā. tacchūnyatāpavādaparihārārthaṃ ca–
tadabhāvasya sadbhāvas tasmin sā śūnyatāparā ||
(Madhyāntavibhāga 1.20cd)
iti. anenābhāvasvabhāvaśūnyatā nirdiṣṭā. tathā cātra Bhāṣyam–
pudgaladharmābhāvaḥ śūnyatā, tadabhāvasya ca sadbhāvas tasmin yathokte bhoktrādau sānyā śūnyateti śūnyatālakṣaṇakhyāpanārthaṃ dvividhām ante śūnyatāṃ vyavasthāpayati, abhāvaśūnyatām abhāvasvabhāvaśūnyatāṃ ca, pudgaladharmāropasya tacchūnyatāpavādasya ca parihārārthaṃ yathākramam. evaṃ śūnyatāyāḥ prabhedo vijñeyaḥ. (Madhyāntavibhāgabhāṣya ad loc.)
sā ceyaṃ tattadvikalpahānaye rūpādisambandhenābhinirhārabhedād anantaprakārā skandhādiṣu grāhakādivivekaikalakṣaṇatvenādvayā ca. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya §7o', 299a4, 146c12-14)
uktaṃ ca–
bhoktṛbhojanataddehapratiṣṭhāvastuśūnyatā |
yac ca yena yathā dṛṣṭaṃ yadarthaṃ tasya śūnyatā || śubhadvayasya prāptyarthaṃ sadā sattvahitāya ca |
saṃsārātyajanārthaṃ ca kuśalasyākṣayāya ca ||
gotrasya ca viśuddhyarthaṃ lakṣaṇavyañjanāptaye |
śuddhaye buddhadharmāṇāṃ bodhisattvaḥ prapadyate ||
pudgalasyātha dharmāṇām abhāvaḥ śūnyatātra hi |
tadabhāvasya sadbhāvas tasmin sā śūnyatāparā ||
(Madhyāntavibhāga 1.17-20)
iti. tatra bhoktṛṇy adhyātmaṃ cakṣurādīni. bhojanaṃ bahirdhā rūpādīni. tayor adhiṣṭhānaṃ deho 'dhyātmabahirdhā. pratiṣṭhāvastu mahān bhājanalokaḥ. dṛṣṭaṃ śūnyatājñāneneti tacchūnyatā. yatheti paramārthākāreṇa. yadarthallakṣaṇabhramaniṣedhārtham ante śūnyatādvayam.
tathānimittaṃ teṣām eva hetubhūtānāṃ tā eva śūnyatāḥ. apraṇihitaṃ ca phalabhūtānām.
kiṃ ca śūnyatā kalpitayor dharmapudgalayor hānaye, tadabhiniveśasya ca paratantrabhūtasyāpraṇihitam, animittaṃ tu pariniṣpannasākṣātkriyāyai. iti praṇidhīnāṃ santater anucchedaḥ.
18'.0 [ 9b4] idaṃ ca sarvavastutattvālambanaprasthānabodhicittaṃ draḍhayituṃ mantrādhiṣṭhānam āha– om ityādi.
oṃ śūnyatājñānavajrasvabhāvātmako 'ham ||
18'.1 [ 9b4-6] acintyākāradevatābhyupagama oṃkāraḥ. śūnyatā sarvadharmanairātmyarūpā, saiva jñānam, tad evābhedyatvād vajram, tadātmako 'ham iti. uktaṃ ca bhagavatā–
na rūpād anyā śūnyatā na śūnyatāyā anyad rūpam. (Prajñāpāramitāhṛdaya Ed. p. 150, 145a)
iti.
18'.2 anena ca rucijapamantreṇa svabhāvātmaka ity ekapadaṃ kṛtvā ṣaṭpadātmatayā bodhicittapaṭaloktaṣaḍvidhabodhicittam abhihitaṃ boddhavyam.
19.0 [ 9b6] samprati devatāsādhanavidhim āha– tadanv ityādi.
*tadanu śaradindudhavalām
*anantadikcakravarticitrābhām |
*ūrdhvasthitāṃ trikoṇām
*antarga)ganasvarūpavajradharām || 19
19.1 [ 9b6–10a3] tadanu prasthānabodhicittādhiṣṭhānānantaraṃ dharmodayākhyamudrāṃ (see 20a) dhyāyād (see 20d) iti sambandhaḥ. śaradindudhavalām iti jagadāhlādanasnigdhasitākārām. anantadikcakravarticitrābhām ity anantānāṃ diśāṃ cakraṃ samūhas tadvartinīti tadvisarpiṇī citrā nānārūpābhā prabhā yasyāḥ sā tathā tām.
ūrdhvasthitām ity apatitarūpām ity arthaḥ. trikoṇām ity atrāmnāyena koṇadvayam upari draṣṭavyam. antar abhyantare gaganasvarūpā ca vajrāṃś ca sphuradrūpatathāgatān dhārayatīti vajradharā cety antargaganasvarūpavajradharā tām.
dharmodayākhyamudrāṃ
madhyasthasarojaviśvakuliśāḍhyām |
hūṃvajrīkṛtabhūmau
samantabhadrodbhavāṃ dhyāyāt || 20
20.1 [ 10a6–10b1] dharmāṇāṃ daśabalavaiśāradyādīnām uda(*mudrām. kutra dhyāyād ity āha– hūṃvajrītyādi. sūryastha)hūṃkārajajvaladvajravisarair vajramayyāṃ bhūmau.
20.2 [ 10b2-4] tatrāyaṃ rakṣācakrabhāvanākramaḥ. oṃ śūnyatetyādimantroccāraṇānantaraṃ kāyādimātrasvabhāvasya ca jagataḥ kalpanāmātratāvasāyād icchāmātrādhīnatvena svaparakāyavākcittam oṃ āḥ hūṃ ity akṣaratrayarūpaṃ hoḥkāradvayagarbhitaṃ mantriṇo bodhicittaṃ pratisandhya + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + parāvṛttibījam asaṅgapratipatter ākāśasthaṃ
candre vidhāya purato vighnacakranigrahārthaṃ sūryasthanīlahūṃkāranirgatajvaladvajravisaraiḥ sīmābandhaṃ digbandhaṃ vajraprākāraṃ vajrapañjaraṃ vajramayīṃ bhuvaṃ vibhāvya tanmadhye nirvighnapratibhāsena dṛṣṭavajradharapariṇatām uktalakṣaṇāṃ dharmodayākhyamudrāṃ dhyāyād iti.
20.3 etac ca mṛ(*ducittasyoktam. adhimātracittasya tu) + + + + + + kārā draṣṭavyās tair vajrīkṛtā bhūmir yatreti tasmiṃś cakre sati bhāvita ity arthaḥ.
20.4 tathā hi bhramaṇena bhūmivāsanānām api chedane 'sādhāraṇāś catvāraḥ samatādhṛta(?)pādānaparyantā ādarśādinā traidhātukātikrāntyā śuddhau tathāgatabhūmir vajropamagocare ākṣiptā.
21.0 tatas tasminn antarbhūte vighnākṛtyavagatau ma + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + m vighnābhāvāt trividhatathatāparivṛttyātmake 'pratiṣṭhitanirvāṇe spaṣṭato bhavaśamavikalpaviśuddhirūpayoś ca padmavajrayor bhāvanāyāṃ mārgāṅgaviśuddhirūpaṃ pariṇamya Vairocanajaṃ kūṭāgāraṃ vaktum āha– bhrūṃkāretyādi.
bhrūṃkāracakrasambhava-
savidyaVairocanāṅgasambhūtam |
bhāsvanmunīndramaṇḍala-
citrāṃśuvyāptasarvadikcakram || 21
21.1 mārgāṅgāni punar etāni. samyagdṛṣṭir laukikī yathāsvaṃ dṛṣṭasyaivaṃ mayedam adhiga(*di) + + + + + + + + + + + + (*samyaksmṛ)t(*i)ḥ. layādidoṣarahitaḥ samādhiḥ samyaksamādhir iti.
21.2 ācāryas tv evam āha–
jñeyāvaraṇahānaye bhāvanāmārgaprabhāvitāny aṣṭau mārgāṅgāni. tadyathā– samyagdṛṣṭiḥ sarvadharmanairātmyalakṣaṇāyāṃ samatāyāṃ tadvibhāge ca duḥkhādike yāviparītā susūkṣmākārā prajñā. samyaksaṃkalpo heyo + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + (*. samyaksmṛ)tiḥ smṛtyupasthānādiprabhāvitā maitryādyupastambhasvapraṇidhānānuyāyiny asaṃmoṣadharmatā.
samyaksamādhiś ca kāyādikarmānugataṃ vaiśeṣikaguṇābhinirhārāśrayam avikalpajñānapratiṣṭhāpanam amitaprathamādidhyānalakṣaṇaṃ samādhānam. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya §8g, 305a4–300b2, 146a26–147b10)
iti.
21.3 [ 10b6–11a3] bhrūṃkāras tajjacakraṃ ca tataḥ sambhava utpattir yasya savidyasya svā(*bhaprajñā) + + + + + + + + + + + + + (*Vai)rocanas tasyāṅgaṃ tena sambhūtaṃ niṣpannam. tatra (see 28d) dharmodayāntaḥsthaviśvābjavajre yathoktaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭaṃ kūṭāgāraṃ prabhāvayed (see 28d) iti sambandhaḥ.
bhāsvatāṃ munīndrāṇāṃ maṇḍalaṃ samūhaḥ, tasya ye citrā nānāvarṇā aṃśavo raśmayaḥ, tair vyāptaṃ sarvāsāṃ diśāṃ cakraṃ samūho yena tat tathā.
21.4 athavā bhāsvanti munīndramaṇḍalāni + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + sya.
caturaśram avaiṣamyāc
caturbhir agraṃ smṛtīndriyadvāraiḥ | dhyānacatuṣṭayatoraṇa-
samādhivedīpariṣkṛtaṃ paritaḥ || 22
22.1 [ 11a3-4] avaiṣamyād iti dharmatayā buddhābuddhādīnāṃ samatvāc caturaśram. uktaṃ ca Bhadrapādaiḥ–
caturaśram avaiṣamyād buddhābuddhasamatvataḥ |
(Guhyasamājamaṇḍalavidhi 327cd)
iti.
22.2
pramuditādidaśabhūmiviśuddhirūpadaśārarakṣācakrabhāvanākṣiptapāramitābhir ekāgrīkṛtāyām uktalakṣaṇaśūnyatāyāṃ vipañcitākāravipaśyanāvatārāya saptatriṃśad bodhipākṣikā (*dharmāḥ.) (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya §7'-§8', 299a4-5, 146c14-17)
22.3 [ 11a4-5] + + + + + + + + + + + + + + (*catu)rbhir agraṃ smṛtīndriyadvārair iti. smṛtayaḥ kāyādismṛtyupasthānāni. etābhiś ca catvāri samyakprahāṇāni catvāraś ca rddhipādā lakṣitā grāhyāḥ. tāsv indriyāṇi śraddhādīni. smṛtyādīni smṛtyādiviṣayāṇīty arthaḥ. tāni cātrānāsravāṇy āropitāni catvāri dvārāṇi tair agraṃ śreṣṭham.
22.4 [ 11a5-6] ayam arthaḥ. kāya(*vedanācittadharma) + + + + + + + + + ḥ smṛtyupasthānākhyāḥ. śraddhānusāriṇaḥ śrutacintābhāvanāmayā dharmānusāriṇas tu parāmṛṣabhāvāḥ. nānātvādyayogataḥ prajñotpannā smṛtyā kīlavad upatiṣṭhate. smṛtir vopatiṣṭhate 'nayeti.
22.5 tad uktam–
tadyathā kāyasmṛtyupasthānaṃ nānāvayavasaṃniveśamātram ayaṃ kāyo grāhyādiviviktalakṣaṇaś ca. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya §8a', 299a5-6, 146c18-20)
iti.
(*evaṃ vedanāsmṛtyupasthānam, cittasmṛtyupasthāna)m, dharmasmṛtyupasthānam. tac cādhyātmikabāhyobhayakāyādibhedāt pratyekaṃ trividham. vīryasamādhismṛtisamprajanyayogād viśeṣato bodhisattvānāṃ sambodhicittadānādikuśalamanaskārayogenānekadharmātmakam api smṛtiprādhānyāt smṛtyupasthānam ity ucyate. evam anyatrāpi draṣṭavyam.
(Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya §8a' cont'd, 299a6-7, 146c20-25)
iti. pramāṇā + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + ṇaṃ
22.6 [ 11a6–11b1] dharmapraveśahetutvena pūrvaṅgamatvāc chraddhāmātrakateti. tatra yā śraddhā tadviśuddhyā prāgdvāram. uktaṃ ca Bhadrapādaiḥ–
kāyaviccittadharmāṇāṃ nānaikatvādyayogataḥ |
tatsmṛtis tatra yā śraddhā prāgdvāraṃ bodhaye matam ||
(Guhyasamājamaṇḍalavidhi 328)
iti.
22.7 [ 12a3-5] tathā dharmasmṛtyupasthānena vipakṣapratipakṣabhūtadharmayor avabodheḥ. (?) utpannavipakṣasvabhāvaviparyāsanāśāya bhāviviparyāsānutpādāyānutpannapratipakṣarūpatathyavāsanotpādāyābhūtatathyavāsanāsthitaye śraddadhānasya tadākṣiptam advayākāreṇa yac caturvīryaṃ samyakprahāṇākhyaṃ kuśalākuśalavṛddhiprahāṇotsāharūpaṃ
tadviśuddhisvabhāvaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ dvāram. uktaṃ ca–
bhūtabhāviviparyāsahānyanutpattaye tataḥ |
abhūtotpannatathyasya cotpattisthitaye punaḥ ||
arvāgdvāraṃ caturvīryam
(Guhyasamājamaṇḍalavidhi 329-330a)
iti.
22.8
evaṃ samyakprahāṇeṣu satsu samyaksambodhau chando vīryaṃ cittaṃ mīmāṃsā cādhikyena pravartanta ity avagamya yogācārasyānimittaphalaprāptāv āśvāsapratilambhaprabhāvitāś chandādyālambanāḥ samādhayaḥ. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya §8c', 299b2-3, 147a1-3)
kausīdyāvavādasampramoṣalayauddhatyānabhisaṃskārābhisaṃskāralakṣaṇapañcasamādhidoṣaprahāyakacchandavīryaśraddhāprasrabdhismṛtisamprajanyacetanopekṣārūpāṣṭavidhaprahāṇasaṃskāraviparicārāḥ.
kāyacittavivekam āśritya vipakṣavirāganirodhasaṃniśrayā nirabhisaṃskāravṛttiparyavasānāś catvāra ṛddhipādāḥ samvṛtiparamārthamukhena. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya §8c' cont'd, 299b3, 147a3-4)
22.9 [ 12a6–12b1] ṛddher ākāśagamanādilakṣaṇāyāḥ pādā aṅgāni hetava iti yāvat. teṣu chandādiṣu viṣayabhūteṣv asampramoṣalakṣaṇā smṛtir ālabdhavīryasyopatiṣṭhate. tasyāś ca smṛter dharmapravicayotthāpitāyā viśuddhyā paścimaṃ dvāram. tad uktam–
chandotsāhasthitir matiḥ |
paścimam ṛddhipādās tu dvāraṃ tatsmṛtir atra tu ||
(Guhyasamājamaṇḍalavidhi 330bcd)
iti.
22.10 chandādibhyaś ca
sarvākārajñatākhye phale śraddhāvīryasmṛtisamādhiprajñāś cādhipatyena vartanta itīndriyāṇi pañca. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya §8d-e, 299b3-4, 147a5-7)
etatprabhāvitaṃ tu nirvedhabhāgīyadvayam, ūṣmagataṃ smṛtyupasthānapradhānaṃ nirvedhabhāgīyaṃ mūrdhagataṃ ca samyakprahāṇapradhānam iti. etāny eva śraddhādīni svavipakṣakṣatāv aurjityaprāptāni pañca balāni. tatprabhāvitam aparaṃ nirvedhabhāgīyadvayam. kṣāntaya ṛddhipādapradhānāḥ. agradharmāś cendriyabalapradhānā bhavanti.
(Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya §8S', 299b4-6, 147a8-12)
iti draṣṭavyam.
22.11 [ 12b2-3] tadā pūrvoditeṣu pradhvastasakalavikalpeṣu dharmeṣu cittasyābhiprasannatotsāhālambanaḥ smṛtiparicārito dharmapravicayam āmukhayaṃs tadviparītāśraddhāder antaṃ kurvann upasthitasmṛtiś cittaikāgratālakṣaṇaḥ samādhir āmukhībhavatīti tadviśuddhirūpam uttaraṃ dvāram. uktaṃ caitat–
śraddhāvīryasmṛtidhyānaprajñendriyabalātulam | samādhir uttaraṃ tv evam
(Guhyasamājamaṇḍalavidhi 331abc)
iti.
22.12 dhyānacatuṣṭayatoraṇam iti pūrvoditeṣv asaṃgatiprabhāviteṣu caturṣu śūnyatācatuṣkeṣu vaśitāsamādhānākaraṇaprabhāvitāni yathākramaṃ prāgvat smṛtyupasthānādipradhānāny ūṣmagatamūrdhagatakṣāntyagradharmalakṣaṇāni catvāri nirvedhabhāgīyāni kuśalamūlāni mantrā(?)kṣiptāni tebhyaś ca kāmavivekādilakṣaṇaṃ vipulaṃ prathamaṃ dhyānam.
tadvaipulyaṃ ca saṃkṣepeṇa caturvidham– āśayavaipulyaṃ sarvasattvārthaṃ sambodhicittaprabhāvitatvāt, prayogavaipulyam animittadānādiprayogapariniṣpannatvāt, prativedhavaipulyaṃ sarvatragadharmadhātuprativedhānukūlatvāt, vihāravaipulyaṃ cāpratiṣṭhitanirvāṇaparyantaṃ sarvasamādhānādiguṇāśrayatvenādhiṣṭhānāt.
idaṃ caturvidhaṃ vaipulyaṃ kāmāt kuśalavivekajaprītisukhasamādhitadāśrayaśraddhādibhedāt pāramitādibhedāc ca bahuprakāram.
atra ca dhyāne divyacakṣuḥ | pūrvanivāsānusmṛtikrameṇa cirābhyastasambodhinimittapraveśe saty antarbahiḥsarvadharmeṣu sthirībhūteṣu skandhādīnāṃ yathāparikalpitādirūpeṇānupalabdhinirvikalpaḥ sarvasattvārthasambodhipraṇidhānamayaś ca sam(*ya)ksaṃkalpo darśanamārgaḥ.
dānapāramitāviśuddhyadhiṣṭhānabhūtaś cittotpādaḥ prathamā bhūmiḥ.
śrāvakādimanaskāravarjitaḥ sarvasattvasambodhaye ca daśakuśalakarmapathaprayogādhiṣṭhānaḥ sarvākārajñatāniyato dvitīyā bhūmiḥ.
tato vitarkavicāradoṣapraśamāya śūnyatāśūnyatādicatuṣkagocarasamatājñānādhiṣṭhānaṃ dvitīyaṃ dhyānaṃ puraskṛtam. tatpuraskāraś ca pūrvavad āśayaprayogapra(*ti)yogena puraskaraṇāt. samādhipuraskāraḥ– yathoktasamādhe rātrindivānunivṛttyā samastāvaraṇapratipakṣabhāvena puraskaraṇāt.
vipaśyanāpuraskāraś ca– yathoktavipaśyanāyāḥ sarvākārajñatārthaṃ yathoktaskandhānutpannadharmanidhyānakṣāntirūpāyāḥ saśrutasambhārāyāḥ sattvārthaṃ prati puraskaraṇāt. iyaṃ ca tṛtīyā bhūmiḥ.
vīryātiśayayogena caturvaiśāradyasamatājñānāvāptau śūnyatāśūnyatādigocarabodhipakṣadharmayogād dvitīyadhyānāśrayāc caturthī bhūmiḥ.
tataḥ prīteś cittamārdavatvena prajñātiśayasammukhībhāvaviguṇatvena tadvairāgyasambhavaṃ tṛtīyaṃ dhyānam. tac cāpramāṇaniyatam. maitrīkaruṇāmuditopekṣālakṣaṇāny apramāṇāni sarvadikṣv apramāṇasarvasattvagocaratvāt. tanniyamo 'pratiṣṭhitanirvāṇaparyantaṃ niruttarajñāmatayā pūrvoktenāśayavaipulyādinā samādhivyavasthāpanāt. iyaṃ pañcamī bhūmiḥ.
dhyānapāramitāprabhāvitavipaśyanāpramāṇaniyamaḥ– vimūḍheṣu sattveṣu bhūmyantarānukūladṛṣṭiṣu ca pṛthagjanaśrāvakaparyāpanneṣv aupalambhikabodhisattveṣu ca maitryādibhiḥ sarvaśūnyatāprabhedagocarāyāḥ prādhānyenānavakāraśūnyatādicatuṣkālambanāyāḥ prajñāpāramitāyāḥ sarvākārajñatābhimukhībhāvaniyamāt. tata eva ceyaṃ ṣaṣṭhī bodhisattvabhūmiḥ.
tataḥ sukhasamvitter uttarottarābhisaṃskāravirahe samantabhadrabhāvānukūlatvāt tatprahāṇena caturthaṃ dhyānam. tac cāsādhāraṇaṃ śrāvakādibhiḥ. tad aṣṭavidham. sukhaprahāṇāsādhāraṇam– laukikaśrāvakādisukhaprahāṇasya sāvidyanirvāṇaparatvāt. bodhisattvasukhaprahāṇaṃ tu lakṣaṇaśūnyatāpratipatteḥ.
sarvākārajñatāniṣpattyarthaṃ sābhogānimittapuruṣakārayogena nānopāyakauśalyāśraya(?) dūraṅgamā bhūmiḥ.
duḥkhaprahāṇāsādhāraṇam– pudgalāntaraduḥkhaprahāṇasya punarbhavanirvāṇānukūlatvāt. bodhisattvaduḥkhaprahāṇaṃ tu yathoktavaipulyādiyogena sarvasattvārthaṃ sambodhiprāptau. anābhogānimittapraṇidhānayogenāṣṭamī bhūmiḥ.
saumanasyaprahāṇāsādhāraṇam– tadanyaprahāṇasya pūrvavat. bodhisattvasya tv animittapratipattibalādhiṣṭhānena sarvaśrāvakapratyekabuddhatvasvasambhārabhūmipraveśaparokṣābalayogāt, sarvaparṣatsu samastasaṃśayaprahāṇasamprayogāc ca navamī bhūmiḥ.
daurmanasyaprahāṇāsādhāraṇam– pudgalāntarasambandhino 'sya pūrvavat. bodhisattvasya tu sarvatra yathoktavaipulyādiyogena viśeṣato 'syām avasthāyāṃ sarvadharmaśūnyatopasaṃhāre saty ātyantikasvaparārthādhikāreṇa sarvatathāgataraśmyabhiṣekāśrayatvena niruttaraprabhāvadharmopadeśāśrayā sambuddhabhūmipraveśe vajropamasamādhibhūmiparyantā daśamī bhūmiḥ.
tadanūpekṣāsādhāraṇam– asyā abhāvaśūnyatā, pratipattimukhena svaparayoḥ prajñāpāramitājnānasambhogadṛḍhībhāvāśrayatvāt.
vipaśyanāsādhāraṇaṃ ca– asyā vipaśyanāyā mahāmaitrīmahākaruṇāyogena sarvalokadhātuṣu samastasattvakṛtyānuṣṭhānajñānasvabhāvatvāt, yathoktādarśādiṣu vaśitvayogenāveṇikadharmaprayogeṇa ca, yathoktaskandhādidharmadhātuviśuddhijñānasvabhāvatvāc cety etadāropeṇa ca catvāri toraṇāni yasya tad dhyānacatuṣṭayatoraṇam.
22.13 [ 12b6-7] samādhayo viśeṣeṇa sarvākārajñatābhinirhārayogināṃ pariśuddhaikāgratāprabhāvitā yathāsvanāmānusāreṇāparimitaprabhedās te 'pīha saṃkṣepaprasiddheḥ śūraṅgamagaganagañjanirmalasiṃhavikrīḍitākhyāś catvāras ta eva vedyaḥ. tābhiḥ pariṣkṛtaṃ maṇḍitaṃ paritaḥ samantataḥ. dhyānacatuṣṭayatoraṇaṃ ca samādhivedīpariṣkṛtaṃ ceti karmadhārayaḥ.
tadvedikāsamāśrita-
satpūjāvyagradhāraṇīnivaham | sarvābharaṇavicitraṃ
sarvāśāpūraṇaṃ yato jagataḥ || 23
23.1 [ 12b7–13a2] tadvedi sarvopakaraṇādyabhilāṣapūrakaṃ tatas tadviśuddhipradarśanāya sarvair ābharaṇair vicitraṃ tat kūṭāgāram (see 28d).
vinayoddhūtanavāṅga-
pravacanasaddharmasarvagaṃ yad ataḥ |
pavanoddhatadhvajālī-
ghaṇṭādhvanimukharasarvadigvadanam || 24
24.1 [ 13a3-6] vinayetyādi– vinayaḥ sattvavinayanam. tannimittam uddhūtaṃ vistāritaṃ navāṅgaṃ– sūtraṃ pāramitāpratisaṃyuktādyarthasūtraṇāt, geyaṃ ca sālaṃkārapāṭhaṃ Bhadracaryādipāṭhavat, vyākaraṇaṃ Maitreyavyākaraṇādikam, gāthaikagāthādikā, udānaṃ
kāma jānāmi te mūlaṃ saṃkalpāt kila jāyase
(Mahāvastu III.190)
ityādi vinayaprasiddham, avadānaṃ Śūkarikāvadānādikam, jātakaṃ Vyāghrījātakādi, itivṛttakaṃ kathāprabandhaḥ, vaipulyādbhutadharmo buddhe dharme 'vajñetyādipratipādakaḥ. etadrūpaṃ pravacanam, tad eva saddharmaḥ, taṃ sarvaṃ gacchaty ākṣipaty antarbhāvayatīti tat tathoktam.
pavanenoddhatā ca dhvajānām aindryādidiganugatasitapaṭolakṛṣṇābhadhūmrāruṇaśabalapītaharitānām ālī ca tasyā ghaṇṭādhvanibhir mukharāṇi saśabdāni sarvāsāṃ diśāṃ ca vadanāni yasya tat tathoktam. yad yasmād vinayoddhūtanavāṅgapravacanasaddharmasarvagaṃ tat kūṭāgāram (see 28d), atas tadviśuddhyā pavanoddhatadhvajālīghaṇṭādhvanimukharasarvadigvadanam.
jñānādarśasadāmala-
bodhyaṅgāmodipuṣpasampattyā |
hārārdhahāradarpaṇa-
kusumasrakcitrabhūṣaṇāpratimam || 25
25.1 [ 13a6-7] jñānādarśetyādi– jñānāny evādarśā jñānādarśāḥ. ādarśādipañcajñānāni jñānaśabdena gṛhyante, ādarśaśabdasyopalakṣaṇārthatvāt.
25.2
bodhyaṅgāni sapta. smṛtisambodhyaṅgaṃ sattvārthāya sambodhipraṇidhiśrutādhigamāsampramoṣalakṣaṇam. dharmapravicayasambodhyaṅgaṃ tathaiva vyatirekapradhānaṃ prakṛtyanātmadharmatāparicchedalakṣaṇam. prītisambodhyaṅgaṃ prajñātadvinayanānukramapāramitādiguṇāsambhedasamatāvatārahetur anāgatagatiprabhāvitam.
etāni ca darśanamārgaparyāpannāni dharmadhātugocarāṇi kleśāvaraṇapratipakṣabhūtāni sambodher aṅgāny avayavabhūtānīti sambodhyaṅgāni. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya §8f, 299b6–300a4, 147a14-26)
25.3 [ 13b1-2] sadāmalāni ca sattvārthetyādiviśeṣaṇayogāntāni bodhyaṅgāni ca. tāny eva prītikaratvād āmodipuṣpāṇi. jñānādarśāś ca sadāmalabodhyaṅgāmodipuṣpāṇi ceti samāsaḥ. teṣāṃ sampattyā sadbhāvena hetunā tadviśuddhirūpair hārādibhir apratimam anupamam asamaṃ tat kūṭāgāram (see 28d).
aṣṭavimokṣaviśuddher
aṣṭastambhopaśobhitaṃ divyam |
vajranayadharmacakra-
pravartanād vajrasūtrasaṃyuktam || 26
26.1 aṣṭavimokṣetyādi–
vimokṣā alobhākāraprabhāvitāḥ sarvālambaneṣv āsaṅgavyāvṛttiparā yathābhimatābhāsaniṣpattyarthaṃ niryāṇamārgasvabhāvāḥ. te cāṣṭau. nirastarūpasaṃjño vigatalobho rūpī rūpāṇi paśyatīti prathamaḥ. tiraskṛtarūpasaṃjñatvād adhyātmam arūpī bahirdhā rūpāṇi paśyatīti dvitīyaḥ. śubhādhimukto 'pi rūpādiṣv alobho virahatīty ayaṃ tṛtīyaḥ.
(Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya 301b1-4, 147c28–148a6)
apramāṇair jagadanugrahapravaṇatām āpādite cetasi dhyānāvaropicirābhyastenāpramāṇacittadharmatāpratipattyarthaṃ nirātmadharmatāsukhāvabodhāyāpramāṇāvatāreṇa sarvasamāpattipattiḥ. tathā nāsti kiṃcid ity akiṃcanyāyatanasamāpattiḥ. evaṃ naivasaṃjñānāsaṃjñāyatanasamāpattiś ca. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya 301a5–301b1, 147c16-27)
nirodhasamāpattiś ceti, sā ca naivasaṃjñānāsaṃjñāyatanasamāpattipūrvakacittābhisaṃskāranirodhaḥ, pragāḍhasvaprakṛtyavarodho bhavaśamāvaraṇapratirodhalakṣaṇaś ca. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya 301b4-5, ...)
26.2 tad uktam–
rūpī rūpaṃ paśyati śūnyam. adhyātmam arūpī bahirdhā rūpaṃ paśyati śūnyam. śubhāśubhadṛṣṭikṛtaṃ paśyati śūnyam. ākāśānantyāyatanaṃ paśyati śūnyam. vijñānānantyāyatanaṃ paśyati śūnyam. akiṃcanyāyatanaṃ paśyati śūnyam. naivasaṃjñānāsaṃjñāyatanaṃ paśyati śūnyam. saṃjñāvedayitanirodhaṃ paśyati śūnyam. (untraced)
26.3 śūnyam iti vaimukhyasya śūnyatālambanād ata eva kleśānutpādasūcanād abhittvāyatanasaṃgraho draṣṭavyaḥ. adhyātmam iti rāgaviśuddhatvād ato dvitīyam. śubhetyādi śubhādhimuktāv apy alobhapravṛddheḥ. anantam ākāśaṃ vijñānaṃ na kiṃcid astīti manasikaraṇena pravṛttes trīṇi mṛdusaṃjñatvāc caturtham.
saṃjñāgaṇḍa (?) ityādi– asaṃjñakasaṃmoha iti ca pravṛttes tatpūrvakacittābhisaṃskārādinirodhaś cāṣṭama iti.
26.4 [ 13b7–14a2] evam eṣām aṣṭānāṃ vimokṣāṇāṃ viśuddher aṣṭastambhopaśobhitam. aṣṭau cakrādistambhās tair upaśobhitaṃ maṇḍitam. svargopamatvād divyam. vajranayo mantranayaḥ. taddvāreṇa dharmacakrapravartanād vajrasūtreṇa vajrāvalyā samāyuktam.
rūpādiviṣayaśuddhyā
niruttarānantapañcakāmaguṇam |
pañcopahāramaṇḍita-
viśuddhasaddharmadhātuniṣyandam || 27
27.1 [ 14a2-5] rūpādītyādi– ādigrahaṇāc chabdagandharasasparśā gṛhyante. ta eva viṣayā arvāgdṛgdarśanāpekṣayā, tattvato viṣayabhāvānupapatteḥ. teṣāṃ viśuddhiḥ svabhāvaratraṃ prajñāpāramitācittaṃ tasya niḥsyandaṃ phalarūpaṃ daśabalādiniḥśeṣaguṇaviśuddhisvabhāvam iti yāvat.
viśvadalakamalasaṃsthita-
sūryendusanāthadevatāsthānam |
niḥśeṣaratnaviracita-
kūṭāgāraṃ prabhāvayet tatra || 28
28.1 [ 14a5-7] viśvetyādi– viśvadale nānāvarṇadale kamale yathāyogaṃ saṃsthitā ye sūryendavas taiḥ sanāthāny adhiṣṭhitāni devatānām ūnaviṃśatisaṃkhyātānāṃ sthānāni yasmin tat tathā. niḥśeṣair akhilair indranīlādibhir yathāśobhaṃ racitaṃ kūṭāgāraṃ prabhāvayec cintayed ity arthaḥ.
ṣoḍaśakalākalāpa-
prabhavaṃ pūrṇendumaṇḍalaṃ madhye |
madhyāsanasya vidhivad
dhyāyād amitaprabhānikaram || 29
29.1 [ 14a7–14b2] ṣoḍaśetyādi– ṣoḍaśa kalā akārādayo varṇāḥ, kalāpāḥ kakārādayaḥ, tābhyas tebhyaḥ prabhava utpattir yasya pūrṇendumaṇḍalasya tat tathoktam. jātāv ekavacanam. yathākramaṃ prajñopāyasūcakaṃ candradvayam ity arthaḥ. tad etac candradvayam amitaprabhānikaraṃ madhyāsanasya madhye dhyāyāt.
29.2 [ 14b2-5] vidhivad ity asyāyam arthaḥ. yokībhūtair yathākramam adhaūrdhvasthitair niṣpannaṃ dvātriṃśanmahāpuruṣalakṣaṇāśītyanuvyañjanaviśuddhirūpaṃ praṇidhiprasthānabodhicittasvabhāvaṃ candradvayam iti.
29.3 [ 14b6-7] tad etac candradvayaṃ pratighamalarahitatvena svacchatvād ādarśaṃ dyotayati. ekībhavan samatām iti. tad anena pañcākārābhisambodher ākāradvayam uktam.
30.0 [ 14b7] aparāṃs trīn ākārān āha– paramādyetyādi.
paramādyahṛdayasambhavam
aruṇamaṇipratimam atra sadvajram |
sphuradamitamunimayūkha-
prasādhitānantadiṅmukhaṃ paritaḥ || 30
30.1 [ 14b7–15a2] atreti candradvayasthāne tatpariṇāmeneti yāvat. tadupari vā. anantanirmāṇair (see 31b) vihitajagaduttamārthaś (see 31c) ca san tadahaṃkṛtimāñ (see 31b) jinajanakatanuṃ paśyed (see 34d) iti sambandhaḥ.
paramaś ca tathāgatābhisambodhirūpatvenādyaś ca sarveṣāṃ hetubhūtatvena paramādyo Vajrasattvas tasya hṛdayaṃ bījaṃ hūṃkāras tasmāt sambhavo janma yasya tat tathā. aruṇamaṇipratimam iti padmarāgasadṛśam. sphuratām amitānām asaṃkhyātānāṃ munīnāṃ mayūkhaiḥ prasādhitānantadiṅmukhaṃ paritaḥ.
dhyātvādyahṛtsanāthaṃ
tadahaṃkṛtimān anantanirmāṇaiḥ |
vihitajagaduttamārtho
munivaraparamāṇuviracitāpratimām || 31
31.1 [ 15a3–15b1] ādyahṛd Vajrasattvabījaṃ tena sanātham adhiṣṭhitaṃ hūṃkārādhiṣṭhitavaraṭakam iti yāvat. tad īdṛśaṃ vajraṃ (see 30b) dhyātvā tadahaṃkṛtimān ity oṃ vajrātmako 'ham iti vajrāhaṃkāravān mantrī. etena pratyavekṣaṇā darśitā.
anantanirmāṇair Vairocanādyākārāspharaṇair vihito jagatām uttamo buddhatvalakṣaṇo 'rtho yena sa tathā san tadahaṃkṛtimān ity oṃ yathā sarvatathāgatās tathāham ityahaṃkāravān. anenaitad uktam– hūṃkārajatathāgatais tathāgatīkṛtya sattvān antarbhāve oṃ yathā sarvatathāgatās tathāham iti cāhaṃkāre kṛtyānuṣṭhānam. ayaṃ cārthaḥ
kṛtajinābhadehyarthas tadahaṃkṛtimān (Guhyasamājamaṇḍalavidhi 25cd')
ity anena spaṣṭīkṛtaḥ. tayor adiparamāṇuracitatvena sāmyābhāvāt.
nīlāruṇābhadakṣiṇa-
vāmānanapūrṇacandradhavalābhām |
bhāsvadvicitrabhūṣaṇa-
bhujadvayāśliṣṭasadvidyām || 32
32.1 [ 15b1-3] nīlāruṇā ca dakṣiṇavāmānanā cāsau pūrṇacandradhavalābhāsā ceti samāsaḥ. Vajrasattvasya hi kṛṣṇarakte dakṣiṇetaravadane mūlamukhaṃ sarvaśarīraṃ śuklam ity arthaḥ. tāṃ ca bhāsvadvicitrabhūṣaṇena bhujadvayenāśliṣṭasadvidyāṃ
kuliśāsikamalamaṇibhṛt-
pariśiṣṭabhujadviyugmāḍhyām |
trailokyodaramaṇḍana-
vividhāṃśuvyūhanirgatamunīndrām || 33
33.1 [ 15b3-4] kuliśāsikamalamaṇibhṛtā pariśiṣṭabhujadviyugmenāḍhyāṃ dakṣiṇetarapariśiṣṭabhujacatuṣṭayena yathākramaṃ raktavajrakhaḍgamaṇikamalasampannām ity arthaḥ. trailokyodarasya nabhaso maṇḍanā ye vividhā aṃśuvyūhās tebhyo nirgatamunīndrāṃ gaganodaravyāpijinanivahaspharaṇavatīm ity arthaḥ.
trivimokṣamukhaviśuddhyā
prajñopāyātmikāṃ kṛpāparamām |
sakalajagadekatattvāṃ
jinajanakatanuṃ nijāṃ paśyet || 34
34.1 [ 15b4-5] trivimokṣamukheti trīṇi śūnyatānimittāpraṇihitarūpāṇi.
34.2 tāni ca vimokṣā vikalpajālād vimuktihetavas ta eva mukhāni dvārāṇi bauddhadharmadhātupraveśāyeti trivimokṣamukhāni. tad uktam ācāryeṇaiva–
abhijñāpāramitāniṣpattaye prajñāprabhāvitāni sarvadharmābhisaṃkṣepalakṣaṇāni trīṇi vimokṣamukhāni– śūnyatāvimokṣamukhaṃ, tac ca dharmapudgalabhedena skandhādiṣu draṣṭavyavivekalakṣaṇam. tataś ca skandhādiṣuśubhāśubhādiparikalpanimittābhāvāc chūnyatāyām api grāhakādinimittavivekād adhyātmam akliṣṭājñānapratipakṣabhūtam animittavimokṣamukham.
animittīkṛteṣu skandhādiṣu bahirdhākliṣṭājñānapratipakṣabhūtam asaṅgaprabhāvitam apraṇihitavimokṣamukham. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya §15, 302a1-3, 148a20-b1)
iti.
34.3 [ 15b5-7] etadrūpayā viśuddhyā hetubhūtayā prajñopāyātmikāṃ vastutaḥ prajñāsvabhāvatve 'py upāyapradhānām, sakalajagadekatattvām iti sarvaṃ jagad advaitarūpeṇa svapratibhāsābhinnatvād ekatattvaṃ yasyās tāṃ jinajanakatanum iti buddhātmasakaladevatācakrotpattihetuṃ śrīVajrasattvam ity arthaḥ.
nijām iti yogipratibhāsābhinnarūpāṃ paśyed iti bhāvayet. anena suviśuddhadharmadhātujñānaṃ pradarśitam.
35.0 [ 15b7–16a1] Vajrasattvasya pañcākārābhisambodhim uktvā vineyasattvaviśuddhyartham abhiṣekaṃ dātuṃ padmāntarmaṇḍalaracanāya rāgavineyavinayamukhapravṛttes taddvāreṇaiva prajñopāyayoḥ saṃtoṣaṇaṃ tāvat parametyādināha.
paramānurāgasampad-
vineyam avalambya sarvalokaṃ tu |
ratimāyādhvanināntar
niveśayet sakalajinavṛndam || 35
35.1 [ 16a1-2] paramānurāgasya mahāsukhasya sampadā vineyaṃ sarvalokam avalambya dṛṣṭvā ratyābhāsamāyayā nirmitā (?) sattvārthaṃ tatra ca māyākāra eva dhvaniḥ kamalakuliśāsphālanena svaraḥ, tena dhvanināntaḥ śarīre romakūpair niveśayet sakalajinavṛndam. anena prajñātoṣaṇaṃ kathitam.
36.0 [ 16a2-3] atha tad ityādinā dvitīyatoṣaṇaṃ devīrūpajinanirgamasyopāyatoṣaṇārthatvena viśeṣavidhānāt.
tal Locanādividyā-

nijavidyāntargatīkuryāt || 36
36.1 [ 16a3–16b2] Locanādividyeti– Locanādividyādevyaś catasraḥ, rūpādisamastaviṣayā rūpaśabdagandharasasparśaviśuddhyā Rūpavajrādayaḥ pañca, teṣāṃ bhāvena tadrūpeṇa tad iti jinavṛndaṃ romakūpair utsṛjya tā eva Locanādirūpāpannaniḥsṛjajjinavṛndasvabhāvāḥ sarvavidyā iti navadhā devīr nijavidyāntargatīkuryād iti nijabhujayugadhṛtayuvatitanau
niveśayet. etena copāyatoṣaṇam uktam. tathā coktaṃ Samāje–
atha te sarvatathāgatā bhagavataḥ sarvatathāgatakāyavākcittavajrādhipateḥ paritoṣaṇārthaṃ svabimbāni strībimbāny abhinirmāya (Guhyasamājatantra p. 5, ll. 2-3)
ityādi. svasvakulatathāgatapariṇāmenaiva ca devīnāṃ spharaṇaṃ darśayituṃ tatraivoktam–

iti. buddhā Vairocanās tanniṣpannaLocanākāreṇa. tathā–
SamayaTārākāreṇa (Guhyasamājatantra p. 5, l. 5)
iti. samayā Amoghās tanniṣpannaTārākāreṇa. evam itarāsv apy upalakṣaṇārthatvena svakulapariṇāmo draṣṭavyaḥ. Dharmadhātuvajrāyās tu spharaṇaṃ noktam, dharmadhātoḥ sarvagatatvāt, Vajradhareṇāliṅganād vā, sambodhau sparśajasyaiva mahāsukhasya prādhānyajñāpanārthaṃ nidānapaṭale Sparśavajrāparyantānāṃ navadhādevīnāṃ nirgamapraveśayor upadarśanād vā.

37.1 [ 16b3-4] tatkamaletyādi– tasyāḥ svavidyāyā yat kamalaṃ tasya yā karṇikā varaṭakarūpā tasyā antar abhyantare, dharmasaṃketād amalendur bodhicittaṃ sa eva sarvājñānatamovināśakatvād raśmis tatsambhūtaṃ sakalabuddhādyadhipatiVajrasattvasamanvitaṃ maṇḍalaṃ dhyātvā tatra maṇḍale samastaṃ janakāyaṃ dhiyā praveśayet.

44.0 [ 17a5-6] Mañjuvajrotthānāyākṣaratrayapraveśam āha– om ityādi.

44.1 [ 17a6–17b1] cittam iti pūrvoktadharmanairātmyālambanam antarābhavacittaṃ bhāsvaddhoḥkāragarbhitam iti sphuratkiraṇābhyām akṣaratrayaṃ mātāpitṛVajradhararañjanābhyāṃ raktahoḥkārābhyāṃ madhyekṛtaṃ vidhivad ity āsyena praveśya vajramārgeṇa svavidyākamalodarasthīkṛtatvāt kamalodarasthaṃ kāyādirūparahitatvād atulaṃ tad akṣaratrayaṃ prajñārāgeṇa
