gītidhvanicodanāṃ dhyāyāt || 45
45.1 [ 17b1-5] tasyetyādi– tasyety anantaroktākṣarākāracittasya dravībhūtasya prabhāyā raktavarṇāyāḥ prabhāvād dvāv api vidyāVajrasattvau candradravīkṛtau kalalīkṛtau paśyet.
bhagavatā hi kāyakīṃ deśanāṃ datvenotpāditāḥ kṛpādicittaviśeṣā yathākramam indudravaraśmijaLocanādibhāvam āpannāḥ svasvakulādhipatirūpeṇa Vajradharaṃ stuvatyaś codayantīti draṣṭavyam.
46.0 [ 17b5-6] atra cāryāmātrānurodhāl Locanādīty uktāv api tantre tu cittasya prādhānyakhyāpanāya Māmakīcodanaiva prathamam ukteti tām eva tāvad ādau tvaṃ vajracittetyādinā darśayati.
tvaṃ vajracitta bhuvaneśvara sattvadhāto trāyahi māṃ ratimanojñamahārthakāmaiḥ | kāmāhi māṃ janakasattva mahāgrabandho
yadīcchase jīvitu mahya nātha || 46
(Guhyasamājatantra 17.72)
46.1 tatra Māmakī maitryādiviśuddhirūpā svakulādhipatiCittavajrarūpeṇa Vajradharaṃ stuvatī codayatīti draṣṭavyam.
46.2 [ 17b6–18a3] vajracittaś Cittavajro Vajrasattvaḥ suviśuddhadharmadhātujñānātmakatvāt. bhuvane cakra īśvaraḥ. sattvānāṃ dhātus teṣām arthakaraṇāt. sato bhāvaḥ sattvaṃ buddhatvaṃ tasya janako janakasattvaḥ. athavā Mañjuvajrādyutpattinimittatvāj janakasattvaḥ. ata eva mahāgrabandhuḥ.
evaṃ stuvatī sambodhya Vajradharam āha– trāyahi mām iti māṃ trayasva. katham ity ārthaḥ.
47.0 [ 18a3] adhunā Locanā karuṇādiviśuddhirūpā svakulādhipatiKāyavajrarūpeṇa bhagavantaṃ stuvatī codayati– tvaṃ vajrakāyetyādi.
tvaṃ vajrakāya bahusattvapriyājñacakra
buddhārthabodhiparamārthahitānudarśī |
rāgeṇa rāgasamayāṃ mama kāmayasva
yadīcchase jīvitu mahya nātha || 47
(Guhyasamājatantra 17.73)
47.1 [ 18a3-7] ādarśajñānākāraKāyavajrasvabhāvatvād vajrakāyaḥ Kāyavajro bhagavān evācintyākāratvāt. bahavaś ca sattvāś ca te priyā asyeti bahusattvapriyaḥ. ājñācakravartitvād ājñācakraḥ. buddhānām arthaḥ prayojanaṃ sattvahitam, upāyād bodhiparamāṃ bodhiparyavasānam anudarśayatīti buddhārthabodhiparamārthahitānudarśī.
itthaṃ sambodhya Vajradharam āha– rāgeṇeti mahāsukhena, rāgo jagadabhyuddharaṇecchā, sa eva samayo 'nullaṅghanīyo yasyās tāṃ rāgasamayāṃ kṛpātmikām iti yāvat, māṃ kāmayasva. śeṣaṃ pūrvavat.
48.0 [ 18a7] tataḥ Pāṇḍarā muditādiviśuddhirūpā svādhipaVāgvajrarūpeṇa Vajrasattvaṃ stuvatī codayati– tvaṃ vajravācetyādi.
tvaṃ vajravāca sakalasya hitānukampi
lokārthakāryakaraṇe sadā sampravṛttaḥ |
kāmāhi māṃ suratacarya samantabhadra
yadīcchase jīvitu mahya nātha || 48
(Guhyasamājatantra 17.74)
48.1 [ 18a7–18b3] t pūrvavat.
49.0 [ 18b3] idānīm upekṣādiviśuddhirūpā Tārā svādhipatyAmoghasiddhirūpeṇa bhagavantaṃ stuvatī codayati– tvaṃ vajrakāmetyādi.
tvaṃ vajrakāma samayāgra mahāhitārtha
sambuddhavaṃśatilakaḥ samatānukampī | kāmāhi māṃ guṇanidhiṃ bahuratnabhūtāṃ
yadīcchase jīvitu mahya nātha || 49
(Guhyasamājatantra 17.75)
49.1 [ 18b3-6] vajrarūpād agrajñānātmadevatātmakāḥ kāmā rūpādayo 'syeti vajrakāmaḥ. samayo 'moghaḥ sa evāgraś ca kṛtyānuṣṭhānasvabhāvatvena sattvārthakaraṇāt samayāgraḥ. mahāhita āyatipathyo 'rtho vineyānām asmād bhavatīti mahāhitārthaḥ. sambodher vaṃśo gotraṃ tatra tilakaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ. samatayā śūnyatayānukampata iti samatānukampī.
īdṛśaṃ Vajrasattvam āha– kāmāhi mām iti jinādhāratvena guṇanidhitvād eva bahuratnabhūtāṃ māṃ kāmayasva yad evaṃ matsevayā sakalasvaparārthasiddhir bhavatīti. vipattyavasthāṃ vihāya Samantabhadrākāraṃ madrūpābhedaṃ bhajamāno mām anugṛhāṇety arthaḥ. śeṣaṃ pūrvavad vyākhyeyam.
50.0 [ 18b6] samprati bījacihnakrameṇa Mañjuvajrotthānam āha– iti gītiketyādi.
iti gītikānurodhān
māyopamasarvavastusamvittyā |
bhāsvanmayūkhamaṇḍala-
nijamantratanuḥ svayaṃ bhūyāt || 50
50.1 [ 18b6-7] māyopamasarvavastupratipanmantrī dravajabhāsvanmaṃkārajakṛpāṇamuṣṭimadhyacandrasthaspharanmayūkhamaṇḍalamaṃkārākṛtiḥ svayaṃ bhūyāt.
tasya prabhābhir akhilān svadevatākāranirmitamunīndrān |
antar niveśya manasā
śrīmān iti Mañjughoṣaḥ syāt || 51
51.1 [ 18b7–19a1] tasya maṃkārasya prabhābhir akhilān, svadevatā Mañjuśrīs tadākāratvena nirmitān munīndrān tatraiva maṃkāre 'ntar niveśya manasā śrīmān iti vakṣyamāṇaviśeṣaṇo Mañjughoṣaḥ syāt. iti Mañjuvajram ātmānaṃ paśyed ity arthaḥ.
52.0 [ 19a1-2] itiśabdasūcitaviśeṣaṇāny āryābhyām āha– satkuṅkumetyādi.
satkuṅkumāruṇābhaḥ
savyetaravadananīlasitavarṇaḥ |
nānābharaṇavibhūṣita-
satprajñāśliṣṭabhujayugmaḥ || 52
bhāsvatkṛpāṇabāṇī
nīlotpalacāpavān anantābhaḥ |
sphuradakhilakulavibhūṣita-
sakalanabhomaṇḍalas tāyī || 53
53.1 [ 19a2-3] sphuradbhir akhilais tathāgatādikulair vibhūṣitaṃ sakalaṃ nabhomaṇḍalaṃ yena sa tathā. tāyaḥ svadṛṣṭamārgoktiḥ. sa yasyāsti sa tāyī. jagattāraṇaśīlo vā tāyī.
53.2 tad anena samayasattvo niṣpanno draṣṭavyaḥ.
54.0 varṇitarūpasya samayayuktitvāt prādhānyāyoga upalakṣaṇatvam evety āha– grāhyetyādi.
54.1 [ 19a3-4] anye tv anyathā. iha–
bhavasaṅgād bhavo 'nantaḥ śamasaṅgo vipattibhāk |
māyayā kṛtasaṃsevo dharmadhātvātmako bhavet ||
(Guhyasamājamaṇḍalavidhi 48)
iti manyamāno dharmadhātusvabhāvatām asya bhagavataḥ pratipādayitum āha– grāhyetyādi.
grāhyagrāhakavirahāt
samastavastvasvabhāvatāṃ matvā |
vyavadānābhiniveśa-
kṣatāv adhiṣṭhānam iti kuryāt || 54
54.2 [ 19a4-6] paramārthato grāhyagrāhakayor virahāt samastavastūnām asvabhāvatā, grāhyagrāhakarūpatvāt svabhāvasya. tad uktam–
na grāhyagrāhakākāraṃ bāhyam asti ca lakṣaṇam |
tato lakṣaṇaśūnyatvān niḥsvabhāvāḥ prakāśitāḥ ||
(Pramāṇavārttika Pra° 215)
iti. tatas tām asvabhāvatāṃ matvā vyavadānaṃ ca nirvāṇam abhiniveśaś ca saṃsāras tayoḥ kṣatau kṣayārtham oṃ dharmadhātusvabhāvātmako 'ham (see 54') ity adhiṣṭhānaṃ kuryāt.
oṃ dharmadhātusvabhāvātmako 'ham ||
54'.1 [ 19a6-7] oṃkārādibhiḥ svabhāvātmaka iti padadvayaṃ kṛtvā ṣaḍbhiḥ padaiḥ kāyādiṣaḍvidhatattvacodanam. dharmāṇāṃ daśabalavaiśāradyādīnāṃ dhātur ādhāras tatsādhakatvāt.
54'.2 tathā hi sattvāśayānurūpavarṇādibhedakalpanayā śāntyādikapalakṣaṇatvād ākārasya tatraiva caritārthatvāt.
54'.3 anye tv anyathā vyācakṣate. asvabhāvatāṃ matvā (see 54b) vyavadāne nirvāṇe yo 'bhiniveśas (see 54c) tasya kṣatau (see 54d) kṣayārtham oṃ dharmadhātusvabhāvātmako 'ham iti mantrādhiṣṭhānaṃ kuryāt (see 54d). bhavanirvāṇāpratiṣṭhitadharmadhātusvabhāvātmako 'ham ity arthaḥ.
54'.4 [ 19b1-4] samastavastuniḥsvabhāvatājñāne hi bhavābhiniveśaḥ kṣīṇa eva. nirvāṇāsaṅgaḥ syāt. tatas tasyāpi prahāṇārthaṃ dharmadhātusvarūpādhiṣṭhānaṃ kāryam, yato nirvāṇe 'py abhiniveśo niḥśreyasanāśanimittam. tad uktaṃ bandhamokṣaparīkṣāyām–
nirvāsyāmy anupādāno nirvāṇaṃ me bhaviṣyati |
iti yeṣāṃ grahas teṣām upādāne mahāgrahaḥ ||
na nirvāṇe samāropo na saṃsārāpakarṣaṇam | yatra kas tatra saṃsāro nirvāṇaṃ kiṃ vikalpyate ||
(Mūlamadhyamakakārikā 16.9-10)
iti. nirvāṇaparīkṣāyām apy uktam–
nirvāṇasya ca yā koṭiḥ koṭiḥ saṃsaraṇasya ca |
na tayor antaraṃ kiṃcit susūkṣmam api vidyate ||
(Mūlamadhyamakakārikā 25.20)
iti.
54'.5 Ratnāvalyām apy uktam–
na cābhāvo 'pi nirvāṇaṃ kuta evāsya bhāvatā |
bhāvābhāvaparāmarṣakṣayo nirvāṇam ucyate ||
(Ratnāvalī 1.42)
iti. bhagavatāpy uktam–
anirvāṇaṃ hi nirvāṇaṃ lokanāthena deśitam |
ākāśena kṛto granthir ākāśenaiva mocitaḥ ||
( Avaivartikacakrasūtra 240b27-28)
iti.
54'.6 [ 19b5] tad anena sevā nāma prathamam aṅgaṃ hetusadṛśadevatākhyamṛduniḥsyandaphalam uktam.
55.0 [ 19b5] dvitīyam aṅgam āha–
tadanu nijabījabhābhi(*r
*yathāsvam āpūrya cakṣurādīni |
*Kṣitigarbhādibhir amala-/amale
*svahṛdindau vajravaraṭakāntaḥstham || 55
55.1 [ 19b6–20a2] tadanv iti sevānantaram. nijabījabhābhir iti Kṣitigarbhādīnāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ bījair yathākramaṃ kṣiṃkārādibhiś cakṣurādisthāneṣu svasvacihnavaraṭakasthais teṣām eva bhābhiś ca tair eva svasvaraśmibhiḥ Kṣitigarbhādyākārīkṛtānītatathāgatair ity arthaḥ.
tad evaṃ nijabījabhābhir hetubhir niṣpannā ye Kṣitigarbhādayaḥ ṣaṭ, taiś cakṣurādīny āpūrya dhyāyād (see 56c) iti pare Sarvanivaraṇaviṣkambhiṇaṃ kṛṣṇasitadakṣiṇetaravadanam, nābher upari sitasaṃvajrajaṃ Samantabhadraṃ kṛṣṇaraktadakṣiṇetaravadanaṃ cintayed ity arthaḥ.
55.2 ete ca Kṣitigarbhādayaḥ svabījavarṇamūlamukhacihnaśarīrāḥ. dakṣiṇe khaḍginaḥ. Viṣkambhiṇaś cakraṃ ca. vāme maṇikamalavantaḥ. Lokeśvarasya cakramaṇī. Khagarbhasya cakrakamale. śaśiprabhāmaṇḍalāsanā vajraparyaṅkiṇaḥ sarvābharaṇā ratnamukuṭā āliṅgitasvābhavidyā draṣṭavyāḥ.
55.3 [ 20a2-4] anenopasādhanam aṅgaṃ sarvajñatālakṣaṇamṛduvipākaphalam uktam, cakṣurādīnāṃ Kṣitigarbhādidevatākāratvāt, pratyekaṃ śrotrādisarvendriyasadbhāvena śabdādisarvaviṣayaparijñānasambhavāt, viṣayāṇāṃ sūkṣmādiviśeṣaparijñānāc ca.
55.4 [ 20a4] tṛtīyam aṅgam āha– amaletyādi. amale svahṛdindau vajravaraṭakāntaḥstham iti kṛṣṇahūṃkārajavajravaraṭakamadhyasthaṃ
*hūṃkāraCittavajraṃ
*vadanaśiraḥkamalacakranābhau ca |
*āḥpraṇavābhyāṃ dhyāyād
*Vāgvajraṃ Kāyavajraṃ ca || 56
56.1 [ 20a4-7] hūṃkāraCittavajram iti kṛṣṇahūṃkārajavajraṃ tanmadhyasthahūṃkāraṃ ca pariṇamya Cittavajraṃ Vajrapāṇinibhaṃ dhyāyād ity arthaḥ. evaṃ bījacihnapariṇāmena niṣpattir VāgvajraKāyavajrayor draṣṭavyā. vadanaśiraḥkamalacakranābhau ceti jihvāmūle raktāḥkārajapadmamadhye śirasi sitoṃkārajacakranābhau ca.
āḥpraṇavābhyām iti raktāḥkāreṇa Vāgvajraṃ Lokeśvaranibham, sitoṃkāreṇa Kāyavajraṃ Kṣitigarbhasadṛśaṃ dhyāyād iti sambandhaḥ.
*taddhṛdayaraśminivahaiḥ
*satprajñāsaṅgabhāsibhiḥ paritaḥ |
*sampūjya jinendrāṃs tair
*ity adhitiṣṭhet svaCittavajrādīn || 57
57.1 [ 20a7–20b3] teṣāṃ Cittavajrādīnāṃ hṛdayāni taddhṛdayāni teṣāṃ raśminivahair iti Cittavajrādihṛdayasthacandrāsanavajrapadmacakrāntaḥsthacandrasitahūṃkārāḥkāroṃkāraraśmibhir ity arthaḥ.
bhāvena viśinaṣṭi– satprajñāsaṅgabhāsibhir iti mayūkhamukhanirgatadaśavidhaRūpavajrādipūjādevīrūpaiḥ parito daśadigavasthitamunīndrahṛdayādisthaCittavajrādīn vidhinā sampūjya tair eva pūjitair jinendraiś Cittavajrādibhiḥ satprajñāsaṅgabhāsibhiḥ paścād itīti Cittavajradharaḥ śrīmān (see 58a) ityādikrameṇa svaCittavajrādīn adhitiṣṭhet.
Cittavajradharaḥ śrīmāṃs trivajrābhedyabhāvitaḥ |
adhiṣṭhānapadaṃ me 'dya karotu cittavajriṇaḥ || 58
(Guhyasamājatantra 12.74)
58.1 [ 20b3] trivajrābhedyabhāvita iti Kāyavajrādīnām abhedena bhāvitas tatsvabhāva ity arthaḥ.
daśadiksaṃsthitā buddhās trivajrābhedyabhāvitāḥ |
adhiṣṭhānapadaṃ me 'dya kurvantu cittavajriṇaḥ || 59
(Guhyasamājatantra 12.75)
oṃ sarvatathāgatacittavajrasvabhāvātmako 'ham ||
dharmo vai vākpathaḥ śrīmāṃs trivajrābhedyabhāvitaḥ|
adhiṣṭhānapadaṃ me 'dya karotu vāgvajriṇaḥ || 60
(Guhyasamājatantra 12.72)
60.1 [ 20b3-4] vākpatha iti jihvāmūle niṣpannaḥ. itarat prasiddham.
daśadiksaṃsthitā buddhās trivajrābhedyabhāvitāḥ |
adhiṣṭhānapadaṃ me 'dya kurvantu vāgvajriṇaḥ || 61
(Guhyasamājatantra 12.73)
oṃ sarvatathāgatavāgvajrasvabhāvātmako 'ham ||
kāyavajradharaḥ śrīmāṃs trivajrābhedyabhāvitaḥ |
adhiṣṭhānapadaṃ me 'dya karotu kāyavajriṇaḥ || 62
(Guhyasamājatantra 12.70)
daśadiksaṃsthitā buddhās trivajrābhedyabhāvitāḥ |
adhiṣṭhānapadaṃ me 'dya kurvantu kāyavajriṇaḥ || 63
(Guhyasamājatantra 12.71)
oṃ sarvatathāgatakāyavajrasvabhāvātmako 'ham ||
64.0 [ 20b4] tato gāthābhir abhyarthanayā praviṣṭasyom ityādimantrādhiṣṭhitasya trivajrasyācintyatāṃ darśayitum āha– gaganopametyādi.
*gaganopamasvabhāvaṃ
*lakṣaṇarahitaṃ trivajram ālambya |
*saṃcodya hṛtprabhābhiḥ
*sakalāśāvalayavartimunivṛndam || 64
64.1 [ 20b4-5] bhedalakṣaṇarahitatvād eva gaganopamasvabhāvam acintyarūpam ity arthaḥ. tad īdṛśaṃ trivajram ālambya svakuleśodbhāsimauliḥ syād (see 65d) iti pareṇa sambandhaḥ.
64.2 [ 20b5-7] anena sādhanam aṅgaṃ dharmasambhoganirmāṇalakṣaṇakāyatrayasvabhāvacittavākkāyavajrākhyaṃ mṛdupuruṣakāraphalam uktam. tad uktam–
dharmasambhoganirmāṇavāhinī jagadarthatā | cittaguhyādyadhiṣṭhānaṃ sādhanārtham ato bhavet ||
(Guhyasamājamaṇḍalavidhi 51)
iti.
64.3 ayam arthaḥ. tatra dharmakāyo dvividhaḥ, saṃskṛto 'saṃskṛtaś ca. tatra saṃskṛto yaḥ sambuddhakārakasvabhāvo 'nāsravaḥ pariśuddhe 'kaniṣṭhabhavane 'bhisambuddho yathā–
bodhipakṣyāpramāṇāni vimokṣā anupūrvaśaḥ |
navātmikā samāpattiḥ kṛtsnaṃ daśavidhātmakam ||
abhibhvāyatanāny aṣṭaprakārāṇi prabhedataḥ |
araṇā praṇidhijñānam abhijñāḥ pratisamvidaḥ ||
sarvākārāś catasro 'tha śuddhayo vaśitā daśa | balāni daśa catvāri vaiśāradyā nirakṣaṇam || trividhaṃ smṛtyupasthānaṃ tridhāsaṃmoṣadharmatā | vāsanāyāḥ samuddhāto mahatī karuṇā jane || āveṇikā muner eva dharmā ye 'ṣṭādaśeritāḥ | sarvākārajñatā ceti dharmakāyo 'bhidhīyate ||
(Abhisamayālaṃkāra 8.2-6)
teṣāṃ dharmāṇāṃ pratilakṣaṇās tv asaṃskṛtaḥ. tadavyatirikto 'pi vyatirikta iva lokottaramārgeṇa labhyate notpadyata iti. akṛtrimatvāt svābhāvikaḥ. yad uktam–
sarvākārāṃ viśuddhiṃ ye dharmāḥ prāptā anāsravāḥ | svābhāviko muneḥ kāyas teṣāṃ prakṛtilakṣaṇaḥ ||
(Abhisamayālaṃkāra 8.1)
iti. āśrayanivṛttilakṣaṇo 'sau dharmatātmakaḥ kāyo dharmakāya iti. ayaṃ ca sāmbhogikakāyaśliṣṭaḥ samaḥ sarvatathāgatānāṃ sūkṣmaś ca yathecchaṃ sambhogadarśanāya ca sambhogacittatve hetutvaṃ ca. tathoktam–
samaḥ sūkṣmaś ca tacchliṣṭaḥ kāyaḥ svābhāviko mataḥ |
sambhogavibhutāhetur yathecchaṃ bhogadarśane ||
(Mahāyānasūtrālaṃkāra 9.62)
iti. dvividho 'py asau viśuddhacittacaittarūpo bhāvitaCittavajrasvabhāvas tatra darśanacitratvapratilambhaviśeṣaḥ sarvākārajñeye pratyakṣībhāvato 'vagantavyaḥ. yena tu kāyena bhūmipraviṣṭānāṃ bodhisattvānāṃ dharmaṃ deśayati sa lakṣaṇādyātmā sāmbhogikaḥ, parṣanmaṇḍalabuddhakṣetranāmaśarīrasambhogakriyābhir bhinnaḥ. yad uktam–
sarvadhātuṣu sāmbhogyo bhinno gaṇaparigrahaiḥ | kṣetraiś ca nāmabhiḥ kāyair dharmasambhogaceṣṭitaiḥ ||
(Mahāyānasūtrālaṃkāra 9.61)
iti. sa cāsamāhitaprajñāpūrvako bhāvitaVāgvajralakṣaṇaḥ. tatra deśanāvibhutvapratilambhaviśeṣo gāmbhīryodāryavicitrānekamukhāprameyadeśanāto jñeyaḥ. yena punaḥ kāyena tadanyeṣāṃ sattvānāṃ vinayanaṃ karoti sa nairmāṇikaḥ, ā saṃsāraṃ nānājagadarthakaraṇād avicchinnaḥ. yad uktam–
karoti yena citrāṇi hitāni jagataḥ samam |
ā bhavāt so 'nupacchinnaḥ kāyo nairmāṇiko muneḥ ||
(Abhisamayālaṃkāra 8.33)
iti. punaś ca–
sadā śilpamahābodhijanmanirvāṇadarśanaiḥ |
buddhanirmāṇakāyo 'yaṃ mahopāyo vimocane ||
(Mahāyānasūtrālaṃkāra 9.64)
iti. mahopāyatvāt parārthasampattir eva mukhyā. sa ca bhāvitaKāyavajrātmako 'tas tatra vibhutvapratilambhaviśeṣaḥ sattvakṛtyānuṣṭhānāśrayo 'prameyāvyāhatābhijñāguṇasaṃśrayād draṣṭavyaḥ. anayoś ca sāmbhogikanairmāṇikakāyayoḥ svaparārthasampattiḥ sarvākārā pratiṣṭhitā. yad uktam–
dvayor dvayārthā sampattiḥ sarvākārā pratiṣṭhitā |
(Mahāyānasūtrālaṃkāra 9.63cd)
iti. yadā tu svābhāvikaḥ kāyaḥ pṛthag abhipretas tadā viviktāvyatirekajñānarūpaṃ durbodham atas tatpratirūpaṃ spaṣṭam apy aspaṣṭam iti sūcanāya svābhāvikakāyasvabhāvo jñānasattvo vakṣyamāṇakrameṇa pṛthag boddhavyaḥ. na hi lokottaramārgagamyam anyaviṣayas tathānyo 'pi tathāgatakāya iti.
64.4 tad anena catasro mudrā vyākhyātā draṣṭavyāḥ. tatra Cittavajrasvabhāvā mahāmudrā, jñānasattvasvabhāvā samayamudrā, Vāgvajrarūpā dharmamudrā, Kāyavajrasvabhāvā karmamudreti.
64.5 [ 20b7–21a1] saṃcodyetyādinā caturtham aṅgam āha. tadvad (see 66a) yo jñānasattvo dṛṣṭaḥ, athavātra Cittavajrādinivartanakāla eva VāgvajraCittavajrayor antarāle śaśisthakaravālamuṣṭimadhyasthitendau niṣaṇṇaḥ samayasadṛśo jñānasattvo vijñeyaḥ, tasya hṛtprabhābhiḥ sakalāśāvalayavartimunivṛndaṃ saṃcodya
*munivaramarīcinirgata-
*vidyāgaṇakarasamuddhṛtaiḥ kalaśaiḥ |
*jñānāmbunābhiṣiktaḥ
*svakuleśodbhāsimauliḥ syāt || 65
65.1 [ 21a1-3] teṣām eva saṃcoditānāṃ munivarāṇāṃ marīcibhyo nirgatā ye vidyāgaṇā Locanādayaḥ, teṣāṃ karaiḥ samuddhṛtā ye jñānāmbupūrṇāḥ kalaśās tair jñānāmbunābhiṣiktaḥ san Mañjuvajrayogī svakuleśodbhāsimauliḥ syād ity Akṣobhyeṇoddīptamukuṭo bhavet.
abhiṣekānantaram abhiṣekadevīpūjājñānāmbubhiḥ pariniṣpanno 'kṣobhyo Mañjuvajrasya mukuṭe draṣṭavya ity arthaḥ.
65.2 [ 21a4-5] etena mahāsādhanam aṅgam abhiṣekarūpamahāvaimalyaphalam uktam, jñānodakābhiṣekeṇa vaimalyotpādanāt. tad evaṃ sevādyaṅgacatuṣṭayam adhipatigatatvenottamaṃ niḥśeṣavipakṣakṣayākṣamatvenāpaṭutvān mṛduśabdavācyaṃ mṛduniḥsyandaphalaṃ coktam iti.
66.0 [ 21a5-6] sampraty aparaṃ sevādyaṅgacatuṣṭayaṃ niḥsyandādiphalasahitaṃ prajñāviśodhanaṃ mṛdvadhimātrayoś caturaṅgayor madhyavartitvena madhyaśabdaviśeṣaṇaṃ vaktum āha– tadvad iti.
*tadvat svadevatābhāṃ
*svahṛdā saṃvīkṣya nirmitāṃ vidyām |
*āpūrṇacakṣurādyāṃ
*tryakṣaravinyāsabhāsvaraśarīrām || 66
66.1 [ 21a6-7] Mañjuvajravat sevādikrameṇa vidyāṃ saṃvīkṣya tattvajño (see 67a?) mantrī sugatān anurāgayāmīty adhimokṣeṇa (see 69?) tayā saha ratim ārabheteti (see 69?) sambandhaḥ. tad eva sevādikaṃ darśayan vidyāṃ viśinaṣṭi– svahṛdeti.
66.2 [ 21a7–21b2] asyāyam arthaḥ. tathaiva kākandyāgatapuraḥsthitacandrasthasvapratibhāsacoditākārādirūpatathāgatāntarbhāvādinobhayasaṃtoṣasattvābhiṣekarahitena vidhinā svahṛdā vajradharadravajaMañjuvajrabījena nirmitāṃ niṣpāditāṃ svadevatābhām iti Mañjuvajrākārām. anenaitasyāḥ sevā madhyaniḥsyandaphalā sūcitā.
66.3 [ 21b2-3] āpūrṇacakṣurādyām iti prāgvat svasvabījaniṣpannaKṣitigarbhādyadhiṣṭhitacakṣurādiṣaṭkam. anenopasādhanaṃ madhyavipākaphalaṃ sūcitam.
66.4 [ 21b3-4] tryakṣareti tryakṣarāṇi hūṃ-āḥ-oṃ-kārā eṣāṃ prāgvac Cittavajrādirūpeṇa pūrvoktasthāneṣu dhyānaṃ vinyāsaḥ. tad anena sādhanaṃ madhyapuruṣakāraphalaṃ sūcitam.
66.5 [ 21b4-5] tataḥ prāgvad eva jñānāmbunābhiṣekādinā bhāsvaraṃ nirmalaṃ śarīraṃ yasyāḥ sā tryakṣaravinyāsabhāsvaraśarīrā. anena mahāsādhanaṃ madhyavaimalyaphalaṃ sūcitam.
67.0 [ 21b5] tām eva pañcakulakalāpinītvena viśeṣayann āha– śirasītyādi.
*śirasi hṛdi [ca tattvajño]
*nābhau guhye tathoruyugmādau |
*oṃ hūṃ svā āḥ hā iti
*kāyamanovajrasatkulādyaiś ca || 67
67.1 [ 21b5-6] śirasi hṛdi nābhau guhya ūruyugme cādigrahaṇena yathāsvabhāgaparamāṇuṣu ca yathākramaṃ oṃ-hūṃ-svā-āḥ-hākhyaiḥ kāyamanovajrasatkulādyair iti kāyādipañcakulasvabhāvair mantradevatayor abhedād akṣarākārair eva
*pañcabhir adhiṣṭhitāṅgīṃ
*jagattrayavyāpisatprabhāvalayām |
*āḥkṛtavicitrakarṇika-
*hūṃkāravinirmitāṣṭadalakamalām || 68
68.1 [ 21b7–22a2] pañcabhir adhiṣṭhitāṅgīm. tatra Kāyavajrākhyaṃ satkulaṃ mohakulam. Manovajrasatkulaṃ dveṣakulam. ādyagrahaṇād ratnakulaṃ sā tathā,
69.0 [ 22a2-3] tāṃ evaṃbhūtām upadeśād bījaraśmicodanāt pañcāvasthāṃ prāpyāntarbhūtāṃ ca samvīkṣya (see 66b) kiṃbhūto ratim ārabhetety (see 69?) āha– sadityādi.
*satprajñāniḥsyanda-
*svabhāvasopāyabhūtanijadehaḥ |
*[ratim ārabheta mantrī
*sugatān anurāgayāmīti ||] 69
69.1 satī ca mahākṛpābhisandhinā sarvākāratvāt prajñā ca niruttaramokṣarūpatvenātaḥ prakarṣāntarābhāvād iti satprajñā. athavā navavidhaparipākadyotakanavadevīrūpatayā. ata eva sopāyabhāvaḥ.
69.2 [ 22a3-4] sā ca satprajñā hetuVajrasattvavidyā tanniḥsyandasvabhāvaḥ sopāyabhūto nijadeho yasya mantriṇaḥ sa tathā.
69.3 īdṛśyaiva sakalasvaparārthasampadāśrayabhūtagambhīrodārākārapratipattyā sarve tathāgatā anurajyanta iti
69'.0 [ 22a4-5] tadanurāgaṇayogādhiṣṭhānamantram āha– oṃ ityādi.
oṃ sarvatathāgatānurāgaṇavajrasvabhāvātmako 'ham ||
69'.1 [ 22a5] iyatā granthenādiyogo nāma prathamaḥ samādhir uktaḥ.
70.0 [ 22a5-6] sampraty aparaṃ sevādyaṅgacatuṣṭayaṃ niḥsyandādiphalasahitaṃ sarvadevatāgatatvena sāmānyaṃ jñānaviśeṣavaśād adhimātraviśeṣaṇaṃ vaktuṃ maṇḍalarājāgrisamādhim utpattikrameṇāha– hṛccandretyādi.
*hṛccandramantrabhābhiḥ
*praveśya niḥśeṣabuddhasaṃdoham |
*hūṃvajranirgatam amalam
*ambhoruhavarti sanmanorūpam || 70
70.1 [ 22a6-7] hṛccandramantro jñānasattvahṛdayendusthacihnamadhyasthitacandrasthamaṃkāras tasya bhābhir niḥśeṣabuddhasandoham ātmani mukhena praveśya samastanijamāṇḍaleyarūpeṇa (see 71b) svahṛtprasūtaṃ dhyātvā (see 71a) lokahitahetoḥ (see 71d) sthiracittaḥ (see 71c) samutsṛjed (see 71d) iti sambandhaḥ. hūṃkāreṇa vajraṃ hūṃvajram.
tanmārgeṇa nirgataṃ ca tad amalaṃ ca sanmanorūpam iti praveśitabuddhasandohapariṇāmajaṃ bodhicittam. ambhoruhavartīti devīkamalodaravarti.
*dhyātvā svahṛtprasūtaṃ
*samastanijamāṇḍaleyarūpeṇa |
*iti vidhinā sthiracittaḥ
*samutsṛjel lokahitahetoḥ || 71
71.1 [ 22a7–22b2] dhyātvā svahṛtprasūtam iti svahṛd yathokto maṃkāras tatpariṇatikrameṇa samastanijamāṇḍaleyarūpeṇotpannaṃ tad eva bodhicittam īdṛśaṃ dhyātvā, iti vakṣyamāṇena vidhinā samutsṛjet. kūṭāgāre sthāpayituṃ devīkamalodaramaṇḍalān niścārayed ity arthaḥ.
72.0 [ 22b2] tam evotsargavidhim āha– saṃcodyetyādi.
*saṃcodya Cittavajraṃ
*vajradhṛg iti [paramam arthakṛj jagatām |]
*sitasavyetararaktaṃ
*[sadindranīlamaṇivarṇam] || 72
72.1 [ 22b2] Cittavajram Akṣobhyaṃ yathānirdiṣṭaviśeṣaṇayuktaṃ paramādyavad bhujaiḥ kuliśādidharair anvitam (see 73a), vajradhṛg ity anena saṃcodyārthataḥ sambodhya saṃhṛtyātmani (see 73c) niveśayed (see 73d) iti.
72.2 arthas tv ayam. svarūpaniṣṭham anālambanamano vajradhṛg ucyate. tad uktam–
pañca hetiś ca vetiś ca vajram ity abhidhīyate |
dhāraṇaṃ dhṛg iti khyātaṃ vijñānavajradhṛṅ manaḥ ||
(Guhyasamājatantra 18.40)
iti. asya cārthaḥ– pariṇāmābhisaṃdhyādīnāṃ sāṃkleśikavaiyavadānikavyavasthayor yathāsambhavaṃ hetutāyojanāt pañcarūpādyavasthākalpanāyāṃ pañca hetavo dharmasaṃketakāt pañca hetir ity ucyate. tadapekṣayā phalarūpayojanāt phalāni pañcādarśādīni vetisaṃjñakāni.
tac caitat sarvaṃ jñānarūpatāparyavasitam iti prakāśātmatayāpratihatisādharmyād vajram abhedyaṃ grāhyādyābhāsaṃ vijñānam. taddhāraṇatvena ca taddhṛk. mano nisvanaṃ tattvataḥ svabhāvaniṣṭhaṃ vedanam iti.
72.3 athavā hetir iti prāgvad dhetuḥ. vetir iti phalam. vajrasya pañca śūlāni pañcabuddhātmakāni. tāny evādharottarabhāvena hetuphalaviśuddhirūpāṇi. vajraṃ tad dhārayatīti vajradhṛg dharmadhātuviśuddhirūpas tad eva ca manaḥ.
*Paramādyavadbhujānvitam
*... | *saṃhṛtyātmani dhīmān
*niveśayet sarvabhāvena || 73
73.1 [ 22b2-3] saṃhṛtyeti vipañcitaspharaṇakārasaṃharaṇenānīya sarvātmanātmani Mañjuśrīrūpe niveśayet. dhīmān ākārapūraṇābhiprāyayuktaḥ. athavā niveśyaniveśakagraharahitaḥ.
*jinajig iti Kāyavajraṃ
*saṃcodya ... | *pūrvakamalendumadhye
*dhyāyāc cakrādicihnitaṃ tadvat || 74
74.1 [ 22b3-4] Kāyavajraṃ Vairocanaṃ yathoktaviśeṣaṇam. cakrādicihnitam iti cakrakhaḍgapadmamaṇibhir lakṣitam. tadvad iti Cittavajravaj jinajig ity utsargamantreṇa saṃcodya pūrvakamalendumadhye dhyāyāt.
74.2 atrāpi saṃcodanam arthataḥ sambodhanam. arthaḥ punar ayam. tattvato 'nālambanam eva mano grāhyādijayāj jig ucyate, sadādicatuḥkoṭivinirmuktatvenācintyatvāj jinaś ceti. tad uktam–
sadasanmadhyamātītaṃ bhūtabhautikasambhavam |
vigrahaḥ sarvasattvānāṃ jinajig jananaṃ jinaḥ ||
(Guhyasamājatantra 18.41)
sat sattvaṃ bhāvas tadabhāvo 'sat, tayor bhāvābhāvau madhyamaṃ tebhyo 'tītam acintyatvāt. kiṃ tad upāttamahābhūtabhautikākāratayā sattvasaṃkhyātaṃ pradhānyād viśeṣaṇagraharūpaṃ mohāsiktatayā jaḍarūpaṃ ca. bhittyādisamānatvenānupāttam api yat tat sambhavaṃ tena rūpeṇa sambhavad vigrahaśabdavācyam.
tattvato 'nālambanam eva mano grāhyādijayanāj jig jinaś cācintyatvād iti.
74.3 athavā sadasanmadhyamātītaṃ sadādivikalparahitaṃ bhūtabhautikākārādarśaviśuddhirūpam. tad eva ca vigraha ity ālayavijñānaṃ sarvasattvānām. tad idaṃ jinajig ucyate. jinaśabdasyaivārtham āha– jananaṃ jina iti. jinaśabdena janmocyate. taj jayatīti jinajik. kalpitātmanā notpadyata ity arthaḥ.
*dakṣiṇato Ratneśaṃ
*ratnadhṛg ity ānanaiḥ Kumārābham |
*taptatapanīyasaṃnibha-
*marakataratnādiramyakaraṣaṭkam || 75
75.1 [ 22b4-5] ānanaiḥ Kumārābham iti dakṣiṇavāmamadhyānanair nīlasitapītair Mañjuśrītulyaṃ taptatapanīyasaṃnibhamarakataratnādiramyakaraṣaṭkam iti viśuddhasuvarṇasadṛśavaktraṃ haritamaṇikhaḍgapadmacakraprajñāliṅganaiḥ śobhitakaraṣaṭkaṃ ca Ratneśaṃ Ratnasambhavaṃ ratnadhṛg iti saṃcodya Mañjuvajrasya dakṣiṇato dhyāyād (see 74d) iti sambandhaḥ.
75.2 atrāpi tathaiva sambodhanam. arthas tu tad evāvikalpakaṃ mano vakṣyamāṇaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭaṃ ratnadhṛg ucyate. tad uktam–
cittaṃ ratnam iti khyātam arthaiḥ sarvaiḥ samudbhavam |
vedakena vinā vedyaṃ vedanā ratnadhṛṅ manaḥ || (Guhyasamājatantra 18.42)
iti. tathā cittaṃ sarvair arthākāraiḥ samudbhavaṃ yat tat sarvaṃ pūrvavac cittam eva, tad eva prādhānyād anarghatvād ratnam. ātmādivedako 'paro 'stīty āśaṅkyāha– vedakenāyogād vinā. ata eva vedanāmātram eva ratnam. tāṃ cāvasthāṃ ratnam acintyopāyatvena dhārayatīti taddhṛk. prakārāntareṇa mana eva svarūpaniṣṭham.
75.3 athavārthaiḥ sarvair iti sarvārthair yā samatā tatsambhūtaṃ tadviśuddhaṃ cittam eva ratnam. tac ca vedakena svasamvedanavedyam anyathāyogād vedanāviśuddhaṃ mano ratnadhṛg ity uktam.
*ārolig iti saroruha-
*rāgāruṇam ānanadvayaṃ tadvat |
*paścimata ujjvalaprabham
*Amitābhaṃ paṅkajādicihnakaram || 76
76.1 [ 22b5-6] saroruharāgāruṇam iti padmarāgaratnaval lohitam. ānanadvayaṃ tadvad iti kumārasyevāsyāpi dakṣiṇavāmānane nīlasite. paṅkajādicihnakaram iti raktapadmakhaḍgacakramaṇiviśiṣṭakaram. Amitābham ujjvalaprabham ārolig iti saṃcodya Mañjuvajrasya paścimato dhyāyād (see 74d) ity arthaḥ.
76.2 saṃcodanam atrāpi prāgvat. arthas tv ayam– tad evāvikalpakaṃ mano vakṣyamāṇaviśeṣaṇavad ārolig ucyate. uktaṃ ca–
lakṣyalakṣaṇabhāvais tu sarvaṃ sarveṇa sarvataḥ |
ramaṇaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ lakṣyam ārolig iti kathyate ||
(Guhyasamājatantra 18.43)
tathā lakṣyam abhiniveśaviṣayas taccihnaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ tadbhāvais tadrūpatayā tuśabdo 'nukarmaṇārthaḥ. sarvaṃ vastu sarveṇākāreṇa sarvataḥ sarvatrātītādau vyavasthitam ato lakṣyaṇopalakṣyamāṇaṃ lakṣaṇam apy abhiniveśād eveti. lakṣyākāratayā vā ?ādabhāvena mana eva tadākāram ārolig ity ucyata iti.
76.3 athavā lakṣyalakṣaṇabhāvais tv iti– lakṣyaṃ nīlapītādi, lakṣaṇaṃ tasya nirūpaṇaṃ, bhāvaḥ ṣaṣṭhaṃ vikalparūpaṃ manaḥ. tena sarvathā yad ramaṇaṃ sattvārthapratyavekṣaṇaṃ, tadviśuddham eva yal lakṣaṇaṃ lakṣyam anantaroktaṃ tad ārolig iti bhaṇyate.
*marakatamaṇiprakāśaṃ
*prāg iva vadanādisampadopetam |
*uttarato 'moghākhyaṃ *dhyāyāt prajñādhṛg iti kṛpāṇādyaiḥ || 77
77.1 [ 22b7–23a1] marakatamaṇiprakāśam iti haritaratnasadṛśavarṇam. prāg iva vadanādisampadopetam iti nīlasitadakṣiṇavāmamukhanānāvidhakiraṇaspharaṇādisampattyā pūrvavad anvitam. kṛpāṇādyair iti khaḍgacakrapadmaratnair anvitam. Amoghasiddhiṃ prajñādhṛg iti saṃcodya Mañjughoṣasyottarato dhyāyāt (see 74d).
77.2 saṃcodanam atrāpi tathaiva. prakārāntareṇa tad eva manaḥ prajñādhṛg ucyate. tad uktam–
prakarṣakṛtavijñānaṃ yat tat prajñeti bhaṇyate |
saṃskāracetanādhāryaṃ prajñādhṛg iti kathyate ||
(Guhyasamājatantra 18.44)
pratibhāsamātratayā viṣayāsāmyāt prathamotpattim apekṣya dvitīyādi paraṃ vijñānaṃ prakarṣakṛtam ucyate. atas tādṛkprabandhajasaṃskāracetanayā dhāryamāṇaṃ tad eva vijñānaṃ prajñādhṛg ity ucyata iti.
77.3 athavā prakarṣakṛtavijñānam iti maitrīkaruṇādyabhyāsaprakarṣeṇa kṛtaṃ yad vijñānaṃ kṛtyānuṣṭhānarūpaṃ tat prajñeti bhaṇyate. tāṃ dhārayatīti saṃskāracetanāviśuddhaṃ manaḥ prajñādhṛg ucyate.
77.4 ete ca buddhā viśuddhā arthā (?) boddhavyāḥ.
78.0 [ 23a1] sarveṣām eva samānāni viśeṣaṇāny āha– ruciretyādi.
*rucirajaṭāmukuṭottama-
*nānāratnaprabhojjvalābharaṇāḥ |
*vidyāsanāthavapuṣaḥ
*sarve ravimaṇḍale dhyeyāḥ || 78
78.1 [ 23a1-3] rucirajaṭāmukuṭāś cottamanānāratnaprabhābhir ujjvalābharaṇāś ceti tathoktāḥ. vidyāsanāthavapuṣa iti vidyayāliṅgitasvābhaprajñayā sanātham anugṛhītaṃ vapur yeṣāṃ te tathā. sarva ity anuktāsanāḥ ravimaṇḍale dhyeyāḥ. Vairocanasya tu candrāsanitvaṃ viśeṣeṇa vihitam iti na tasya sūryāsanitvaprasaṅgaḥ.
tathā ca śabdavitsamayaḥ– sāmānyavihitā vidhayo viśeṣavihitair vidhibhir abhibhāvyanta iti.
*āgneyadikpradeśe
*syān moharatīti Locanā devī |
*pūrṇendumaṇḍalasthā
*paramā śṛṅgāraKāyavajrābhā || 79
79.1 [ 23a3-4] parameti kṛpākṣityupāyadūraṅgamāsvabhāvatvenotkṛṣṭā. śṛṅgāraKāyavajrābheti śṛṅgārarasabhūṣitā mukhādibhir Vairocananibhā pūrṇacandrasthā Locanā devī moharatīti mantreṇa saṃcodanād āgneyadikpradeśe syāt.
79.2 saṃcodanam atrāpi prāgvat. dvayābhāsādirūpajñānayogājñānāhaṃkāro mohaḥ. tad uktam–
advayajñānadharmeṣv ahaṃkāro moha ucyate |
(Guhyasamājatantra 18.50ab)
iti.
79.3 athavā dharmatayādvayajñānarūpeṣu yo 'haṃkāraḥ svarūpādhimokṣaḥ, sa moha ucyate 'prādeśikarūpaḥ.
79.4 mohādiṣu mohādirūpeṇāpi bhedāyogād aikyarūpā prajñāsvabhāvā ratiḥ. uktaṃ ca–
ratir atyantasambhogaḥ
(Guhyasamājatantra 18.51c)
iti. atyantam abhilaṣitakaruṇādirūpatāpatteḥ samyag bhogo 'viparītatvāt.
*nairṛtakoṇe lāsyā
*Citteśavad ... | *dveṣaratīti mahārata- *paramotsukaMāmakī dhyeyā || 80
80.1 [ 23a4-5] mahārate sattvārtharāge parama utsuko yasyāḥ sā tathā. maitrīpraṇidhijalācalāsvabhāvā Māmakī dveṣaratīti coditā nairṛtakoṇe dhyeyety arthaḥ.
80.2 codanaṃ pūrvavat. dveṣo hi grāhyagrāhakavidveṣaḥ. tad uktam–
anyo'nyaghaṭṭanaṃ tatra dveṣa ity abhidhīyate || (Guhyasamājatantra 18.50cd)
jñānāyoge ceyaṃ yuktiḥ, yato dvayasya ghaṭṭane sphoṭane dveṣo 'nyo'nyam apekṣya. tathā hi vijñānam apekṣya grāhyanirākṛtis tadabhāvam apekṣya vijñānasyeti.
80.3 athavā yac ca parasparasaṃgharṣaṇam advayarūpam, tad eva dveṣaḥ.
80.4 ratiś ca prāgvat.
*Vāgīśāmaladehā
*rāgaratīti prabhañjanāśāyām |
*paramānu Pāṇḍarākhyā
*[dhyeyā tāvad amitaguṇaratnā] || 81
81.1 [ 23a5-6] Vāgīśasyevāmalo deho yasyāḥ sā tathā. raktavarṇādinirmalaśarīrety arthaḥ. paramānv iti muditātejobalasādhumatīviśuddhirūpatvenottamā ca Pāṇḍarākhyā yathoktarūpā rāgaratīti saṃcoditā prabhañjanāśāyāṃ dhyeyā.
81.2 codanam atrāpi tathā. abhiniveśākāro rāgaḥ. tad uktam–
lakṣaṇaṃ rāgam āsaktir
(Guhyasamājatantra 18.51a)
iti. lakṣaṇam abhiniveśākāraṃ rāga ucyate. asyaiva paryāyāntaram āsaktir iti.
81.3 athavā yac ca lakṣaṇam āsaktisvabhāvam advayākāram, tad eva rāgaḥ.
81.4 atrāpi ratiḥ pūrvavat.
*Ratneśavatsvarūpā
*vajraratīti pracoditā Tārā |
*aiśānyadikpradeśe
*... draṣṭavyā || 82
82.1 [ 23a6-7] Ratneśavatsvarūpeti varṇādibhī Ratnasambhavatulyā, upekṣāvāyujñānapāramitādharmameghāviśuddhirūpā Tārā yathoktaviśeṣaṇā vajraratīti pracoditā, aiśānyadikpradeśe draṣṭavyā.
82.2 codanaṃ pūrvavat. mohādiviśeṣaṇarahitajñānam apratighātād vajram ity ucyate.
82.3 athavā yac cānantaroktam evābhedyarūpaṃ jñānaṃ tad vajram ucyate.
82.4 mohādidvitīyasyākhyāne caturṇām eṣāṃ mohādīnāṃ
ratir atyantasambhogaḥ
(Guhyasamājatantra 18.51c)
ekatrāpattir iti draṣṭavyam.
82.5 atrāpi ratiḥ pūrvavat.
83.0 [ 23a7] āsāṃ pradhānacihnāny āha– saccakretyādi.
*saccakra[raktanalinī-
*paṅkajapītotpalāni cihnāni]
*[kramaniyamaḥ] śiṣṭāni
*svādhipavat [diṣṭāni] || 83
83.1 [ 23a7–23b1] śiṣṭānīti Locanāyāḥ khaḍgapadmamaṇayaḥ pariśiṣṭāni cihnāni. evaṃ Māmakyāḥ. Pāṇḍarāyās tu khaḍgacakramaṇayaḥ, Tārāyāḥ khaḍgapadmamaṇaya ity arthaḥ.
*maṇḍalakoṇacatuṣke
*mūladvāradvipārśvayoś ca syuḥ |
*Rūpādivajradevyaḥ
*Kāyādinibhās tritattvena || 84
84.1 [ 23b1-2] maṇḍaletyādi– ā. tāś ca Rūpādiṣaḍdevyo yathākramaṃ Kāyavajrādinibhāḥ.
84.2 [ 23b2-3] tritattveneti– oṃ āḥ hūṃ iti mantreṇotsṛjya yathāvidhi vakṣyamāṇadarpaṇādipradhānacihnā dhyātavyā ity arthaḥ.
84.3 tatra kāyeśo Vairocanas tatsadṛśī darpaṇacihnā khaḍgapadmamaṇipariśiṣṭacihnā ca rūpāyatanadānapāramitāpramuditāviśuddhisvabhāvā Rūpavajrā. Śabdavajrā śabdāyatanaśīlapāracihnā ca.
84.4 etāś ca sarvā devyo viśuddhisvabhāvā boddhavyāḥ.
85.0 adhunāstitvaguṇavattvaśakyatvahetukarmapratyayaprasādābhilāṣalakṣaṇaśraddhāvato yogino yuktyāgamāśritayā prajñayā sakalaprapañcaviviktajñānānupraveśanimittaṃ mokṣabhāgīyaśraddhādikuśalamūlāvaropaṇaṃ catuḥkoṭivinirmuktaṃ kāyādismṛtyupasthānādiviṣayam abhyāsād dṛḍhatarāyamāṇam
aśraddhākausīdyavismṛtivikṣepadauḥprajñadṛṣṭimārāsravāvaraṇanivārakaṃ mahāmohādirūpaṃ cetyādinā sattvārthānurūpārthena (?) rakṣākarṣādividhāyakaṃ Yamāryādidvārapālacatuṣṭayam āha– pūrvetyādi.
*pūrvadvāre khyātaḥ
*sarvāśāvartyanantabhīmābhaḥ |
*bhāsvati yamāntakṛd iti
*kṣayograghanamaṇḍalacchāyaḥ || 85
85.1 [ 23b3-4] sarvāśāvartinyo 'nantā bhīmā ābhā asyeti tathā. kṣayasya pralayakālasya ya ugraghanas tasya maṇḍalaṃ samūhas tasyaiva chāyāsyeti tathā.
*Cittādhirājasamatanu-
*viniḥsṛtānekaraudrakulabhābhiḥ |
*Vaivasvatāntakārī
*krūrabhujaṅgāṅgabhūṣaṇaḥ śrīmān || 86
86.1 [ 23b4--24a1] Cittādhirājo 'kṣobhyas tena mukhādibhiḥ samā yā tanur Yamāntakavigrahas tato viniḥsṛtānām anekaraudrakulānāṃ bhābhir lakṣitaḥ. krūrāḥ kruddhā bhujaṅgā aṅgabhūṣaṇam asyeti tathā. śrīmān sarvārthalābhī. sa evaṃbhūto Vaivasvatasya Yamasyāntakārī, yamāntakṛd ity (see 85c) evaṃ saṃcoditaḥ san pūrvadvāre (see 85a) dhyeya ity arthaḥ.
86.2 saṃcodanam atrāpi prāgvat. Yamaḥ sattvārthaniyatatvenopāyātmakaḥ, antakṛc ca kleśavināśakatvena prajñātmaka ity arthaḥ. tad uktam–
avināśātmakā dharmā anutpādasvabhāvataḥ |
samayaḥ sarvabhāvānāṃ tenaivāntakṛd yamaḥ ||
(Guhyasamājatantra 18.53)
iti. ayam arthaḥ. śraddhāvataḥ parārthakriyābhyāsasamayasambhavasamprajanyena niḥśeṣabudhyamāneṣu krameṇa smṛtyupasthāneṣu viṣayata ghanībhūteṣu viśuddhirūpeṣu dharmāṇāṃ niḥsvabhāvatvenānutpannatayā ṃ dharmatājñānaṃ tattvato 'jñānasvabhāvam iti prajñopāyātmakam. grahasya pratipakṣarūpatvena kruddham.
sattvārthapraguṇatāyāṃ ghanībhāvena dārḍhyāc ca devatākāram. Yamaḥ sattvārthaniyamāt. antakṛc ca kleśavināśakatvāt.
Yamasya niyatirūpasya vāntakṛn māyopamarūpatvād iti.
athavā Yamāntakṛd iti dveṣadveṣṭṛsaṃyojanaviśuddhirūpĀnantādyaṣṭanāgālaṃkṛto 'vidyāsaṃyojanasvarūpamohaviśuddhisvabhāvaḥ pradhānībhūtaḥ krodhātmā Yamaḥ, sa eva ca pratipakṣākāratayā prākṛtamohasyāntakṛc ceti. tad uktam–
moho mohopabhogena kṣayamoho yamāntakṛt |
kāyāntakṛd bhavet tena tena jñeyāntakṛd bhavet ||
(Guhyasamājatantra 18.58)
iti. ayam arthaḥ. vicārājñeyābhāvena jñānāyogād ajñānaṃ tattvajñānarūpaṃ mohalakṣaṇayogān mohaḥ. vikalpasya caivaṃ pratipakṣa iti krodharūpaḥ. aparāṇy api mohamūlāni.
moho 'ṣṭau saṃyojanāni. vividhena duḥkhena saṃyojayantīti saṃyojanāni. tadyathā– pratighasaṃyojanam, anunayasaṃyojanam, avidyāsaṃyojanam, mānasaṃyojanam, īrṣyāsaṃyojanam, mātsaryasaṃyojanam, dṛṣṭisaṃyojanam, parāmarṣasaṃyojanam, vicikitsāsaṃyojanam.
tatra pratighasaṃyojanam– pratihanyate 'neneti pratighaḥ, sa eva saṃyojanam iti viśeṣaṇasamāsaḥ. anunayasaṃyojanādiṣv apy ayam eva samāso boddhavyaḥ.
anunayasaṃyojanam– anunīyate 'nenety anunayaḥ. traidhātuke saktilakṣaṇo rāgaḥ.
avidyāsaṃyojanam– ajñānam avidyā tamastimitir moha iti paryāyāḥ. tad uktam–
avidyā satyaratnakarmaphaleṣv asampratipattiḥ, anātmabhūteṣu ca skandhādiṣv ātmopalabdhiḥ, rāgādayaś ca. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya §4a, 297b2, 146a4-6)
iti.
mānasaṃyojanam– manyate 'neneti mānaḥ. sa ca saptavidhaḥ. tadyathā– mānaḥ, atimānaḥ, mānātimānaḥ, asmimānaḥ, abhimānaḥ, ūnamānaḥ, mithyāmānaś ca. tatra māno yātibhogas (?) tatra rūpādiguṇair hīnād asmi śreyān, sadṛśena cāsadṛśa iti tān guṇaparikalpitān hetūn kṛta (?) yo māno mānanaṃ mānāpiti saptavidho māno mānasaṃyojanam ity ucyate.
īrṣyāsaṃyojanaṃ yaś cetaso vyāroṣaḥ, parasampadasahanapūrvako manastāpaḥ.
mātsaryasaṃyojanaṃ yaś cetasa āgrahaḥ. tad uktam–
idaṃ vastu mā matto 'nyatra saratv iti matsaraḥ puruṣas tasya bhāvo mātsaryam. (untraced)
dṛṣṭisaṃyojanam– tisro dṛṣṭayaḥ. tadyathā– satkāyadṛṣṭiḥ, antagrāhadṛṣṭiḥ, mithyādṛṣṭiś ca. tatra pādāya yā kṣāntir ityādi pūrvavat. mithyādṛṣṭiḥ– samudayasatyaṃ vāpavadataḥ, duḥkhasatyaṃ vā, mārgasatyaṃ vā, nirvāṇaṃ vā na śeṣatas tad upādāya yā kṣāntir ityādi pūrvavat. iti tisro dṛṣṭayo dṛṣṭisaṃyojanam.
parāmarṣasaṃyojanam– dvau parāmarṣau. tadyathā– dṛṣṭiparāmarśaḥ śīlavrataparāmarśaś ca. tatra dṛṣṭīḥ parāmṛṣatīti dṛṣṭiparāmarśaḥ. pañcopādānaskandhān agrataḥ śreṣṭhato viśiṣṭataḥ paramataś ca samanupaśyatas tad upādāya yā kṣāntir ityādi pūrvavat. śīlaṃ vrataṃ ca parāmṛśatīti śīlavrataparāmarśaḥ.
pañcopādānaskandhān śuddhito muktito nairyāṇikataś ca samanupaśyatas tad upādāya yā kṣāntir ityādi pūrvavat.
vicikitsāsaṃyojanam– vicikitsanaṃ vicikitsā yā satyeṣu vimatiḥ. tad uktam–
pratighānunayāvidyā mānerṣyāmatsarās tathā |
dṛṣṭir dṛṣṭiparāmarśo vicikitsety amī nava ||
(untraced)
iti.
eṣāṃ ca saṃyojanānām upabhogo vicārato 'yogāt prakṛtinirīkṣaṇam, parārthatvenārthasaṃcārato yojanāt.
arthasaṃcāras tu sattvānantatayānantakālamahākaruṇāprajñābalenānantasattvasarvakleśajñeyādiviparyāsadoṣadveṣād anantaguṇajñānasamāropāc ca dveṣo 'nantaḥ. ata eva kṛṣṇaḥ.
aśeṣajagadarthānurāgalakṣaṇamahārāgo 'nunayaḥ. sa eva ca Takṣakaḥ. ato raktaḥ.
avidyāntaḥ prāgvat tattvajñānarūpo Yamāntakaḥ. sa ca vyākhyātaḥ. viparyāsāsahanadvāreṇa tu kṛṣṇaḥ.
samatayā sarvasattveṣūnnatir ahaṃkāro mānaḥ. sa eva ca nirmalajñānabījatvena mahāprajñatayā sitaMahāpadmaḥ.
īrṣyānto lokeṣu lokottaravirodhilaukikaguṇāsahanenānanyasādhāraṇānuttarajñānaguṇāropānuṣṭhānamukhena gambhīrakṛtyatayā haritaKārkoṭakaḥ.
matsaraḥ– mā viśve mattaḥ sakāśād viparyāsahetor ātmanaḥ paraparikalpitād doṣāḥ sarantu gacchantv iti viśvakuleṣu viśvasya viśvasampatsampādanakatvena ca viśvavarṇaḥ Kulikaḥ.
anantaraparārthaḥ sann asti sambhogakāye nirmāṇakāye cāsminn iti dṛṣṭiḥ satkāyadṛṣṭiḥ. antau bhāvābhāvau tannirākaraṇenācintyopāyaiḥ sukhasampadaṃ sattvān grāhayatīti. antayor vā vināśakatvena grāha iva graha ity etadātmikā dṛṣṭir antagrāhadṛṣṭiḥ. nāsty ātmasattvādir grāhyagrāhakotpādādir veti tadākārā dṛṣṭir mithyādṛṣṭiḥ.
etac ca dṛṣṭitrayaṃ duḥkhahetvātmādimaloddhāradvāreṇa mahāmaitryā jñānaguṇapuṣṭiniṣpādanenāhlāditvāt pītaŚaṅkhapālaḥ.
yena kenacic chuddhim icchatāṃ gotrānurūpayānatrayānyantyā yogisamvṛttyā svasantānābhyāsapratibaddhatayā sambodhir amalasukhasambhogapramodena satataṃ jagaddhitasaṃyojanāc chīlavrataparāmarśaḥ. etac ca saṃyojanadvayaṃ sitaVāsukiḥ.
satyaratnādiṣu samyaktattvānavabodhāt saṃśayitān prati saṃśayacchetṛtayā pratighādivivekena vividhadoṣavyādhipratipakṣajñānāmṛtabhaiṣajyacikitsācaraṇād vicikitsā sitaPadmaḥ.
ata eva krūrabhujaṅgāṅgabhūṣaṇaḥ.
prākṛtamohakṣayaś caivam ity āha– kṣayamoha iti. iti sadṛśabhavāsravasya prākṛtamoharūpasya vicāreṇa nimittavirahe vināśāyuktau kṣayo bhāvaḥ.
kāyāntakṛd iti kāyasya satkāyadarśanasya cayanaṃ kāya ity upalakṣaṇāt, skandhamārasya ca pratipakṣākāratayā vicāreṇa nairātmyadarśanarūpatvāt.
jñeyāvaraṇasya prayogādeś ca devatākāratvena śubhādyāśrayatvād antakṛd iti.
kiṃ tad āvaraṇam? ke śubhādayaḥ? (Madhyāntavibhāgabhāṣya ad loc.)
aprayogo 'nāyatane 'yonivihitaś ca yaḥ | notpattir amanaskāraḥ sambhārasyāprapūrṇatā ||
gotramitrasya vaidhuryaṃ cittasya parikheditā |
pratipatteś ca vaidhuryaṃ kuduṣṭajanavāsatā ||
svadauḥṣṭhulyaṃ cāvaśiṣṭatvaṃ trayāt prajñāvipakvatā | prakṛtyā caiva dauṣṭhulyaṃ kausīdyaṃ ca pramāditā ||
saktir bhave ca bhoge ca līnacittatvam eva ca |
aśraddhānadhimuktiś ca yathārutavicāraṇā ||
saddharme 'gauravaṃ lābhe gurutākṛpatā tathā |
śrutavyasanam alpatvaṃ samādhyaparikarmitā ||
(Madhyāntavibhāga 2.4-8)
etad āvaraṇam. ke śubhādayaḥ? (Madhyāntavibhāgabhāṣya ad loc.)
śubhaṃ bodhiḥ samādānaṃ dhīmattābhrāntyanāvṛtī |
natyatrāsāmatsaritvaṃ vaśitvaṃ ca śubhādayaḥ ||
(Madhyāntavibhāga 2.9)
eṣāṃ śubhādīnāṃ kasya kāny āvaraṇāni jñeyānīty āha– (Madhyāntavibhāgabhāṣya ad loc.)
trīṇi trīṇi ca eteṣāṃ jñeyāny āvaraṇāni hi |
(Madhyāntavibhāga 2.10ab)
kuśalasya sasya trīṇi– asambhāvanā pudgale, anadhimuktir dharme, yathārutavicāraṇā cārthe. amātsaryasya trīṇi– saddharme 'gauravam, lābhasatkārapūjāyāṃ gauravam, sattveṣv akāruṇyaṃ ca. vaśitvasya trīṇi, yair vibhutvaṃ na labhate– śrutavyasanaṃ dharmavyasanasamvartanīyakarmapravasanāt, alpaśrutatvam, samādher aparikarmitatvaṃ ca.
tat punar etad āvaraṇaṃ śubhādau yatrārthe daśa kāriṇatikāraṇam, tadyathā suvarṇakārādayaḥ suvarṇādīnāṃ kaṭakādibhāvena pariṇatau. sampratyayakāraṇam, tadyathā dhūmādayo 'gnyādīnām. sampratyāyanakāraṇam, tadyathā hetuḥ pratijñāyāḥ. prāptikāraṇam, tadyathā mārgādayo nirvāṇādīnām.
evam utpattikāraṇaṃ śubhe draṣṭavyam, tasyotpādanīyatvāt. sthitikāraṇaṃ bodhau, tasyā akopyatvāt. dhṛtikāraṇaṃ samādāne, bodhicittasyādhārabhūtatvāt. tve, tasya vibhutvaprāptilakṣaṇatvāt.
kāraṇaṃ daśadhotpattau sthitau dhṛtyāṃ
[prakāśane | vikāraviśleṣānyathātvapratyayaprayaṇāptiṣu || cakṣurāhārabhūdīpavahnyādis tadudāhṛtiḥ |
dātraśilpajñatādhūmahetumārgādayo 'pare ||
bodhiṃ prāptukāmenādita eva tāvat kuśalamūlam utpādayitavyam. tataḥ kuśalamūlabalādhānena bodhiḥ prāptavyā. tasya punaḥ kuśalotpādasya bodhicittaṃ pratiṣṭhā. tasya bodhicittasya botrastamānasena dharmeṣu guṇadarśinā pareṣāṃ te dharmā vistareṇa samprakāśayitavyāḥ.
tataḥ sa bodhisattva evaṃ vicitraguṇabalādhānaprāptaḥ kṣipram anuttarāṃ samyaksambodhim anuprāptavān sarvadharmavaśitām anuprāpnotīti. eṣo 'nukramaḥ śubhādīnām. (Madhyāntavibhāgabhāṣya ad loc.)
etadāropadārḍhyarūpatvena devatāyāḥ śubhādyāśrayatvaṃ pratipattavyam. asya tu jñeyāvaraṇasya śuddhiḥ prathamam abhidhīyate, yatas tadviśuddhyā yagrahaṇaṃ dharma iti kleśāvaraṇahānipūrvakaṃ jñeyāvaraṇaprahāṇam, tathā karmaṇo janmeti karmāvaraṇaprahāṇipūrvakaṃ janmāvaraṇaprahāṇam iti.
86.3 bhāsvatīti (see 85c) sūryamaṇḍale pūrvadvārasthe khyātaḥ (see 85a) kathitaḥ. tatra draṣṭavya ity arthaḥ.
*dakṣiṇadigdvārasthe
*prajñāntakṛd ity anuttarajyotiḥ |
*Kāyeśavadbhujādyair
*[ugraḥ aparājitaḥ prasabhaḥ] || 87
87.1 dakṣiṇetyādi.
87.2 [ 24a1] jaganmohatimirahāritvād anuttarajyotiḥ.
87.3 Kāyeśo Vairocanas tadvad bhujamukhavarṇair ugraḥ.
87.4 [ 24a1-2] kleśamārair durdharṣatvād aparājitaḥ. prasabha iti pracaṇḍarūpaḥ.
87.5 [ 24a2-4] ayam arthaḥ. bhṛkuṭīmukho daṃṣṭrākarālavadano vivṛtasaroruhasitavadano bṛhatkaṭhinapīnodaroruvakṣasthalo lalajjihvaḥ kharvapīvarāṅgāvayavo jvalatpiṅgalaśmaśrūrdhvakeśo jvalaraśmisūryābhacaladvartulanetra iti. idaṃ ca viśeṣaṇaṃ vikaṭakrodhopalakṣaṇatvena Yamāntakādiṣv api yojyam.
87.6 [ 24a4] sa evaṃbhūtaḥ prajñāntakṛd iti saṃcoditaḥ san dakṣiṇadigdvārasthabhāsvati draṣṭavyaḥ.
87.7 saṃcodanam atrāpi pūrvavat. arthas tu kathyate. prajñā ca prakṛṣṭajñānarūpatvāt, antakṛc ca saṃkleśasasyāt (?). tad uktam–
avijñānātmakā dharmāḥ paramākṣaraśuddhitaḥ | samayaḥ sarvacittānāṃ tena prajñāntakṛj jinaḥ ||
(Guhyasamājatantra 18.54)
iti. astitvādinimittasampratyayādirūpaśraddhākṣiptaṃ samyakprahāṇaviṣayaṃ mokṣabhāgīyaṃ dṛḍhatarāyamāṇam ālambyālambakadvāreṇa dvāradvārirūpaṃ satatasatkṛtyābhiyogād bhavanadhanam ityādi. śeṣaṃ pūrvavat. prajñā ca prakarṣāntakṛc ca saṃkleśasya jitasamastavipakṣatvena ca jinaḥ. ata uktam aparājita iti.
athavā dveṣaviśuddhirūpatvena prākṛtadveṣasya prāgvan nāgābharaṇatvena taditarasaṃyojanānāṃ pratipakṣarūpatvena kāmāsravāntagrāhadṛṣṭikleśānāṃ cāntakṛt prajñāntakṛd ucyate. tad uktam–
doṣo doṣopabhogena kṣayadoṣaḥ prajñāntakṛt |
cittāntakṛd bhavet tena tathā kleśāntakṛd bhavet ||
(Guhyasamājatantra 18.59)
iti. ayam arthaḥ. jñānajñeyayor aniṣṭer aprītir doṣo vicāreṇāyogāt. kleśeṣu vā sattvāpakāriṣu sa eva doṣaḥ krodhaḥ prāgvad aparāṇy api tadupalakṣitāni kāyādidūṣakatvena vā doṣo mohādy aṣṭau saṃyojanāni. teṣām upabhogo vicārata ityādi pūrvavat. tatra saṅgarūpaḥ kāmāsravaḥ. cittatvāc cittam.
śātaḥ sa ca dveṣo 'yaṃ bhogena bhavati mahādveṣātmako 'kṣobhyaḥ. pariśuddhādveṣābhyāse vajrāyata ity arthaḥ. kṣayadoṣa iti– tasyaivĀkṣobhyasya bahirmukhadveṣapraveśasambhavakṣayakartā dvārādhikārī prajñāntakṛd uktaḥ. ataś ca cittāntakṛt– cetayata iti cittaṃ grāhakādyātma, tasyāntakṛt.
ata evāntakṛt– grāhakātmahetutvāt kleśānām aparājitasya ca tadviparītatvāt.
*Hayakandharaḥ ... *paścimadvāre ... | *padmāntakṛd iti …
*... || 88
88.1 Hayakandhara ityādi. Vāgvajravadbhujādyair iti (see 89a) bhujamukhavarṇair Amitābhasadṛśaḥ.
88.2 [ 24a4-5] yathoktāparaviśeṣaṇo Hayakandharaḥ padmāntakṛd ity evaṃ saṃcoditaḥ san paścimadvārastharavau dhyeya iti saṃkṣepārthaḥ.
88.3 saṃcodanam atrāpi prāgvat. arthas tv ayam. vastudharmādhigamopajātā chandādiviṣayā smṛtir viśuddhisūcanāt padmaḥ.
yathā raktam idaṃ padmaṃ rāgadoṣair na lipyate |
vāsadoṣair vividhair evaṃ śuddhaṃ tridhātukam ||
(Sarvabuddhasamāyogaḍākinījālaśaṃvara 5.66)
ity evam antayati ca kleśādikam ity antakṛt. tad uktam–
avācyātmakā dharmā abhāvānāmarūpadhīḥ |
samayaḥ sarvadharmāṇāṃ tena padmāntakṛd vibhuḥ ||
(Guhyasamājatantra 18.55)
iti. pūrvavac chraddhākṣiptavīryaṃ smṛtim ṛddhipādaviṣayām ākṣipati. sā ca kaṭhināyamānā bodhau yogicittasya bandhāya mukhasvabhāvatayā dvāram. etac ca smṛtyā niyamanaṃ samayaḥ. sa cātra yogisamvṛttyā paracittajñānaṃ tattvataḥ svapara doṣāṇāṃ katham avakāśaḥ? tathā ca bodhau cittasya bandhanaṃ syāt. ayam api samayaḥ sarvatra pracārāt.
vikalpakleśānāṃ ca svaparasaṃtānavartināṃ samatayā paracittajñānaṃ tattvato 'jñānam ityādi pūrvavat. tathā cedaṃ pratyavekṣaṇaṃ viśuddhisūcanāt padmam, antayati ca kleśādikam ity antakṛt. vibhuḥ prahāṇasaṃskārair vikṣepanirākaraṇe samarthatvād ādhipatyalābhī.
88.4 athavāvācyātmakā iti vastutattvandādīnām antaṃ karotīti padmāntakṛd bhagavān abhidhīyate.
88.5 athavā mahārāgātmakrodharūpatayā prākṛtarāgasya tat prāgvat taditarasaṃyojanānāṃ dṛṣṭyāsravamṛtyvādiviṣayavikalpam ity ādṛṣṭijanmāvaraṇālayādīnām antakṛt padmāntakṛd ucyate. tad uktam–
rāgo rāgopabhogena kṣayarāgaḥ padmāntakṛt |
vāgāntakṛd bhavet tena samāpattyantakṛt tathā ||
(Guhyasamājatantra 18.60)
iti. samāpattir viṣayaviṣayibhāvalakṣaṇā saṃmatiḥ, tasyāḥ praṇāśaka ity arthaḥ.
*Vāgvajravadbhujādyaiḥ
*[du mas sdang ba 'jigs byed zhal |]
*[gad sgrogs bzhad pas ri rnams g.yo bar byed dang]
*[thub pa'i dbang rnams so] || 89
90.0 Vighnāntakotsargam āha– Akṣobhyavadityādi.
*Akṣobhyavanmukhādyair
*vighnāntakṛd iti [dvārasthe] |
*[pralayānalogra uttara]
āryĀmṛtakuṇḍalir vīraḥ || 90
90.1 mukhabhujavarṇair Akṣobhyasadṛśaḥ.
90.2 [ 24a5] pralayakālānalavad ugraḥ.
90.3 āryĀmṛtakuṇḍaliḥ pāpakebhyo dharmebhya ārād dūraṃ yātatvād āryaḥ. etac ca viśeṣaṇam upalakṣaṇatvena krodhākāratvenāropitaviruddhatvasya nirāsārthaṃ Yamāntakādiṣv api boddhavyam.
90.4 [ 24a5-6] sakalavighnavināśakatvād vīraḥ. vighnāntakṛd ity evaṃ saṃcoditaḥ sann uttaradvārasthabhāsvati dhyeya ity arthaḥ.
90.5 saṃcodanam atrāpi pūrvavat. arthas tv ayam. vikalpāder vighnasyāntakāritvenāntakṛt. tad uktam–
nirvikalpātmakā dharmāḥ prakṛtyā śāntabhāvataḥ | samayaḥ sarvavajrāṇāṃ tena vighnāntakṛt prabhuḥ ||
(Guhyasamājatantra 18.56)
iti. nirvedhabhāgīyalakṣaṇaḥ samādhir indriyādiviṣayaḥ pūrvoktasmṛtyākṣipto ghanāyamānaḥ vikṣepavidviṣṭadevatākārasyāmukhībhāve vikṣepavighnasyānavakāśaḥ. vikṣepavighnopaśamanena ca kāyādiprasrabdheḥ prajñopāyasamādhisatsukhaṃ jāyata iti toṣakaro 'yam. samayaś ca sarvavajrāṇāṃ sarvatra samādhau pracārāt. uktaṃ ca–
śuddhā dhīḥ śaśisaṃkāśā vajram ity abhidhīyate |
(Loc. class. untraced)
iti. tathā hi bhedanimittaṃ grāhyo dharmo na yujyate tadabhāvān na grāhako 'pi śūnyatājñānatvāt. yātrādvayābhāsā (?) cittād anyaṃ na dvayaṃ grāhyagrāhakalakṣaṇam ekānekaviyogādi gaganābjavad. tathā rāgādikleśābhāsāc chraddhādikuśalābhāsād vā cittād anyo na kliṣṭo rāgādiḥ kuśalo vā śraddhādidharmo vidyate. tad uktam–
cittaṃ dvayaprabhāsaṃ
rāgādyābhāsam iṣyate yadvat | śraddhādyābhāsaṃ vā
na tu dharmaḥ kliṣṭakuśalo 'sti || (Mahāyānasūtrālaṃkāra 11.34)
tathā hi dvayapratibhāsavilakṣaṇadvayalakṣaṇābhāvān na tac cittaṃ cetanarūpatvānupatteḥ. tathā cittād anyad ālambanaṃ nāstīty avagamya dhiyā tasyāpi cittamātrasya nāstitvam avagacchati, grāhyābhāve grāhakābhāvāt. asya ca dvayasya jñānajñeyalakṣaṇasya nāstitvaṃ vijñāya saṃtiṣṭhate dvayarahite dharmadhātau. tad uktam–
nāstīti cittāt param etya buddhyā
cittasya nāstitvam upaiti tasmāt |
dvayasya nāstitvam upetya dhīmān
saṃtiṣṭhate 'tadgatidharmadhātau ||
(Mahāyānasūtrālaṃkāra 6.8)
iti. yarūpatvena vipakṣanirākaraṇādhipatye calarūpatvena tannirākaraṇe ca kṛtādhikāratvāt prabhuḥ. ata uktaṃ vīra iti.
athavā kuṇḍaleḥ sārvakarmikatvāt pūrvavan mānādyanyatamaviśuddhirūpatvena prākṛtamānādes tathaiva nāgābharaṇatvena taditarasaṃyojanānāṃ pratipakṣātmadevatākāratvena kāmāsravasya karmajanmāvaraṇayoḥ prākṛtavāsanāyā avidyāsravasya devaputramārādeś ca sakalavighnasyāntakṛd vighnāntakṛd ucyate. tad uktam–
sarvakleśakṣayaṃ yat tat sarvakarmakṣayaṃ tathā |
sarvāvaraṇakṣayaṃ jñānaṃ vighnāntakṛd iti smṛtam ||
(Guhyasamājatantra 18.61)
iti. sarvakleśasyeti kāmāsravāder anantaroktakramānusāreṇa kṣaye karmaṇaś ca kṣayaḥ. sarvāvaraṇeti karmajanmāvaraṇe tayoś caivaṃ kṣayaḥ. upabhogādikaṃ cātrānusṛtya yojanīyam. tata eva sarvavighnavināśanayojanād vighnāntakṛd ucyate. etad uktaṃ bhavati. kleśādipratipakṣarūpaṃ viśuddhakarmaṇaḥ phalaviśodhanātmakaṃ jñānaṃ vighnāntakṛd iti.
athavā sarvakleśetyāder ayam arthaḥ. niḥśeṣakarmakleśāvaraṇakṣayakaraṇasamarthajñānam eva sarvavyāpārakārakaṃ vighnāntakṛd abhihitam ity arthaḥ.

*mitābhaCitteśvarāḥ kramān mukuṭe |

*[ādi kare tathā] || 91
91.1 [ 24a6-7] Vairocanetyādi. eṣām eva Yamāryādīnāṃ vakṣyamāṇe 'bhiṣeke mukuṭe kramaśaḥ kulapatayo VairocanĀkṣobhyĀmitābhĀmoghā draṣṭavyāḥ. mudgarādīni ca catvāri pradhānacihnāni yathākramaṃ kare dhyātavyāni. ādiśabdāt khaḍgādīny api yathāyogaṃ yojyānīty arthaḥ.
91.2 krodhākāratve ca kṛpāvatāṃ sattvavinayanam eva prayojanam. tad uktaṃ Mahāsamayatattve–
aho hi sarvabuddhānāṃ vajrakarma mahādṛḍham |
yac chāntā raudratāṃ yānti raudrāṇāṃ hitahetunā ||
(lost)
iti. tathā Guhyatilake–
aho hi vaśitājñānaṃ buddhānāṃ tattvadarśinām |
yatra hy upāyavinayāḥ krodhatvaṃ yānti nirmalāḥ ||
(untraced)
iti.
*iti parito niṣpannaṃ
*maṇḍalam ālokya sarvabhāvena |

92.1 [ 24b2-5] iti parita ityādi. ity anantaroktakrameṇa paritaḥ sarvato niṣpannaṃ maṇḍalaṃ sarvabhāvena pratyavayavam ālokya jñānasattvahṛnmantrasya mayūkhā evāṅkuśās tais taccodanāj jaḥkāram uccārayato Yamāntakasya hṛnniḥsṛtakrodhair nānāvivarai rakṣāṃ vidhāya samāhṛtān ākṛṣṭān sugatān Mañjuvajrādhipaticakrākārān

93.1 [ 24b5-6] prajñetyādi. prajñopāyamayo vidyāpuruṣasvabhāvo yo 'malaḥ samādhis tatsaṃbhūtaṃ yat satsukhaṃ tenāpūrṇaṃ tac cakram. Antakamathanādyair iti Yamāntakādibhir āhitā vihitā rakṣā jñānacakrasya vighnotsāraṇalakṣaṇā taccakrapraveśavighaṭitasīmādighaṭanalakṣaṇā ca yena sa tathā. evaṃbhūto yogī dhyāyāt.
*svahṛdābhijaptam arghaṃ
*dattvā saccandanādikusumāḍhyam |

94.1 [ 24b6–25a2] svahṛdābhijaptaṃ tryakṣaragarbhitasvamantrādhiṣṭhitam argham ity upalakṣaṇād ācamanādikaṃ ca saccandanādikusumair vakṣyamāṇavidhisādhitapañcāmṛtair āḍhyaṃ sphītībhūtaṃ yuktam iti yāvat.
tam evaṃbhūtam arghaṃ tasmai maṇḍalacakrāya dattvā ḍhaukayitvā tan maṇḍalacakraṃ svahṛdraśmicodanād bhiṣekam iti vakṣyamāṇavidhinā prayataḥ sann abhyarcayet pūjayet.
94.2 [ 25a2-3] atra tu sevādyaṅgacatuṣṭayaṃ tanniḥsyandādiphalaṃ ca paṭūbhūtatvena vipakṣakṣayasamarthatvād adhimātraśabdaviśeṣaṇaṃ, sarvadevatāgatatvena sāmānyaṃ ca.
94.3 upasādhanādikaṃ tu pūrvavad eva, viśeṣas tv atrādhimokṣakṛto boddhavyaḥ.
94.4 [ 25a3-5] abhiṣekānantaraṃ ca buddhānāṃ MāmakīŚabdavajraDharmadhātuvajrāṇāṃ ca maulāv Akṣobhyo bhāvyaḥ. evaṃ LocanāRūpavajrayor Vairocanaḥ. PāṇḍarāRasavajrayor Amitābhaḥ. TārāSparśavajrayor Amoghasiddhiḥ. Gandhavajrāyā Ratnasambhavaḥ.
94.5 Yamāryādīnām uktā evādhipataya iti.
95.0 [ 25a5] tad evam āhitābhiṣekaṃ cakraṃ yena vidhinā pūjayitavyaṃ taṃ vidhiṃ kamalodara ityādināryāṣaṭkena pūjāmantraparyantenāha.

100.1 [ 25b5-6] tad evaṃbhūtābhī Rūpavajrādidevībhis tadvad bāhyaiś ca sarvagandhādyair jinendrān maṇḍalacakrāntarbhūtān oṃ sarvatathāgatapūjāvajrasvabhāvātmako 'ham ity evaṃ mantrādhiṣṭhānena pūjyapūjādibhedavikalpanirmukto mantrī sampūjayed iti.

101.0 [ 25b6] samprati stotropahāram āha– pratiśabdetyādi.
*pratiśabdavat samastaṃ
