101.1 [ 25b6–26a1] tadanv iti pūjāvidher anantaram. pratiśabdavat samastaṃ stutirūpaśabdagrāmaṃ nirūpayann ālocayan, svamana evādhidaivataṃ sveṣṭadevatā tasya stutimukharān stāvakān dharmān pratibhāsārūḍhān itīty Akṣobhyavajretyādigāthābhir (see 102a) bhāvayet.
Akṣobhyavajra mahājñāna vajradhātu mahābudha | trimaṇḍala trivajrāgra ghoṣa vajra namo 'stu te || 102
(Guhyasamājatantra 17.1)
102.1 [ 26a1-7] na kṣubhyata ity Akṣobhyo ttasuvṛtti(?)rūpatvenāpratighātābhedyatayā. kṛtyānuṣṭhānajñānaviśuddhatvam uktam. evaṃbhūtam Akṣobhyaṃ pratipadaṃ sambodhya namo 'stu ta iti namo 'stu tubhyam iti namaskṛtya stutimukharā dharmā āhur ghoṣayeti. iti stotrapūrvakakāyiko namaskāraś caitasiko 'pi sāmarthyalabdho boddhavyaḥ.
Vairocana mahāśuddha vajraśānta mahārate |
prakṛtiprabhāsvarāgrāgra deśa vajra namo 'stu te || 103
(Guhyasamājatantra 17.2)
103.1 [ 26a7–26b3] evaṃ skandhādinairātmyaviśuddhyā virocanād Vairocana. suviśuddhatoktā. mahāśuddheti grāhyā(*dya)bodhicittarūpatvenāgrebhyo 'py agratvāt prakṛtiprabhāsvarāgrāgra. kṛtyaviśuddhiḥ. ity evaṃ sambodhya deśa vajra namo 'stu ta iti pūrvavad āhuḥ.
ratnarāja sugāmbhīrya khavajrākāśanirmala |
svabhāvaśuddhanirlepa kāyavajra namo 'stu te || 104
(Guhyasamājatantra 17.3)
104.1 [ 23b3-7] yathābhāvasvabhāvabodhicittam uttamatvād ratihetutvāc ca ratnaṃ tena rājata iti ratnarāja. suviśuddhatvam uktam. lakṣaṇaviraheṇa suṣṭhu guhyapratibimbasamajagadākāracaryārūpatayā sugāmbhīrya. ādarśaviśuddhiḥ. khasamadharmanairātmyābhedyamāhātmyena n(*i)rmalatvāt khavajrākāśanirmala. samatayā viśeṣaṇam.
kṣaṇikatayā pratītyasamutpādadharmatāviśuddhyā nirlepābhedyatāsūcanāt svabhāvaśuddhanirlepa. pratyavekṣāśuddhatvam. bodhinayarūpeṇa bodhicittarūpābhedyadharmasaṃcayād ekatvena kāyavajra. kṛtyaviśuddhatā. ity evaṃ sambodhya pūrvavad āhuḥ.
vajrāmita mahārāja nirvikalpakhavajradhṛk |
rāgapāramitāprāpta bhāṣa vajra namo 'stu te || 105
(Guhyasamājatantra 17.4)
105.1 [ 26b7–27a3] anutpannacittarūpatayā parimāṇāviṣayatvād vajrāmita. suviśuddhatā. bhāvābhiniveśarahitarāśyā(?) (*pūrvavad ā)huḥ.
amoghavajra sambuddha sarvāśāparipūraka |
śuddhasvabhāvasambhūta vajrasattva namo 'stu te || 106
(Guhyasamājatantra 17.5)
106.1 [ 27a4-7] prakṛtipariśuddhyābhedyakāyāditathāgatarūpatvena sattvārthādiniṣphalatvāyogād amoghavajra. suviśuddhatvaṃ darśitam. ādiśuddhatayā prajñātmatayā nirmalabuddhajñānarūpatvāt sambuddha. ādarśanirdeśaḥ. khasamacaryātmakadeśanayā sarveṣām uttamadharmeṇa sarvāśāparipūraṇāt sarvāśāparipūraka. samatoktiḥ.
niḥsvabhāvabodhicittasvabhāvatayotpannatvāc chuddhasvabhāvasambhūta. pratyavekṣāviśuddhir uktā. śūnyatānayarūpatvenābhedyacittasvabhāvatvād vajrasattva. ity evaṃ sambodhya tu (?) namo 'stu ta iti pūrvavad āhuḥ.
107.0 [ 27a7] stotram abhidhāyedānīm amṛtāsvādam āha– ruciretyādi.
*rucirapraṇavādhiṣṭhitam
*anilānalamaṇḍalastham arciṣmat |
*pañcāmṛtam atha bīja-
*trayajaptāmitamunīndram ujjvālya || 107
107.1 [ 27a7–27b3] yaṃkārajadhūmravarṇadhvajāṅkadhanvābhavāyumaṇḍalam anilamaṇḍalaṃ tatra raṃkārajatryasrāgnimaṇḍalam analaṃ tatrākārajarasādhārapātraṃ tatrasthaṃ pañca dravyaṃ pratibījādhiṣṭhitam antargatabījaraśmyākṛṣṭāmitamunīndraṃ hūṃkāraṃ vajradravaṇajvalanatāpanena ca padmasthaṃ vidhivat pañcāmṛtībhūtaṃ sphuradoṃkārādhiṣṭhitam ujjvālya
dhagiti jvālayitvā, atheti paścād yāvadicchaṃ tryakṣareṇa japtaṃ
*jñānāmṛtasvabhāvaṃ
*hūṃkāranyastavajrasajjihvaḥ |
*hṛccandramadhyagasakala-
*jinacakraṃ prīṇayet tena || 108
108.1 [ 27b3-4] maraṇādiduḥkhaharatvena jñānāmṛtasvabhāvaṃ dhyātvā sitahūṃkārotpāditatriśūkavajrātmikām ātmajihvāṃ vibhāvya tenāmṛtena svabījaraśmicodanāt pratyekakarasthena hṛccandramadhyagatam aśeṣaṃ jinacakram anāsaktyā prīṇayed ity arthaḥ. atra ca śucyaśucikalpanā tyājyā svabhāvaśuddhatvād viśvasyeti.
108.2 [ 27b4-5] iyatā maṇḍalarājāgrir nāma samādhir ukto dvitīyaḥ.
109.0 [ 27b5] adhunā parita ityādy āpūrayan viśvam ityantena (see 129d) binduyogam āha.
*parito nirastavibhramam
*anantahṛdraśminirmitamunīdraiḥ |
*sambuddhīkṛtam akhilaṃ
*jagad antarbhāvya nijamantre || 109
109.1 [ 27b5-7] anantā ye jñānasattvahṛdraśminirmitamunīndrās taiḥ sambuddhīkṛtam ata eva parito nirastavibhramaṃ jagad akhilaṃ nijamantre jñānasattvahṛnmantre 'ntarbhāvya rūpādyākṛtimuktam (see 110a) ityādyartham ākalayya (see 127a) sanmantrabindurūpaṃ (see 127c) svamanaḥ paribhāvayed iti (see 127d) sambandhaḥ.
110.0 [ 27b7–28a2] nanu bāhyārthasadbhāve kathaṃ jñānasattvahṛdayavartikaravālasthitabījatanau sambuddhīkṛtanikhilajagadantarbhāvanam, tadbindau ca viśvasvabhāvamaṇḍalacakravibhāvanam upapattim anubhavatīti manyamāno 'rthasattāsādhakapramāṇābhāvam eva tāvat kathayati– rūpādyākṛtītyādi.
*rūpādyākṛtimuktaṃ
*pratyakṣeṇekṣyate na tadgrāhi |
*grāhyaṃ na cāpi kiṃcit
*sambandhānīkṣanān na cānyena || 110
110.1 [ 28a2–28b6] rūpādyākṛtimuktam iti rūpādyākārarahitaṃ tadgrāhīti tasya rūpāder grāhakaṃ na pratyakṣeṇekṣyate. grāhyaṃ na cāpi kiṃcit pratyakṣeṇekṣyate, kiṃ tu rūpādyākāram eva svasamvidrūpam avikalpakam abhrāntaṃ pratyakṣaṃ prakāśate. grāhyādirūpaṃ tu vikalpaśilpikalpitam eveti bhāvaḥ. yad uktam–
nānyo 'nubhāvyo buddhyāsti tasyā nānubhavo 'paraḥ |
grāhyagrāhakavaidhuryāt svayaṃ saiva prakāśate ||
(Pramāṇaviniścaya 1.38)
tathā–
avibhāgo 'pi buddhyātmā viparyāsitadarśanaiḥ |
grāhyagrāhakasamvittibhedavān iva lakṣyate ||
(Pramāṇavārttika Pra° 354)
iti. nanv arthajñānaṃ samvedanaṃ dharmaḥ, tat kathaṃ samvedakam antareṇa bhavet? dharmā hi dharmitantrā dṛśyante, yathā pākādayaḥ. tasmād avaśyam asyāḥ samvedanakriyāyāḥ kartrā samvedakenāśrayabhūtena dharmiṇā bhāvyam. sa eva ca grāhaka ucyate. tasya rūpādiviṣayatvād grāhyam api siddham.
tato 'numānād arthāpatter vā grāhyagrāhakasiddhir iti, kathaṃ tanniṣedha iti śaṅkāyām āha– sambandhānīkṣaṇān na cānyeneti. rūpādyākṛtimuktaṃ grāhakaṃ grāhyaṃ cekṣyata iti sambandhaḥ.
ayam abhiprāyaḥ. anumānenārthāpattyā paropakalpitapramāṇāntareṇa vā parokṣārthasya na svato 'dhigatiḥ, pratyakṣatāpatteḥ. parato 'pi nāsambaddhāt, sarvataḥ sarvaprati(*nu)palambhayor asambhavaḥ. na hi parokṣe grāhake dharmiṇi tau bhavitum arhataḥ. tatas tadutpattir apy asambhāvinī.
nāpi rūpādijñānānupapattyā cakṣurādivad grāhako 'numeyaḥ, pūrvapūrvakāraṇakalāpamātrād rūpādyākārajñānasyotpatteḥ. tathāpi nimittāntarāṅgīkāre 'navasthāprasaṅgo 'nuttaraḥ. tato na vāstavo grāhyagrāhakasamvittibhedaḥ.
110.2 [ 28b6–29a1] pākādayas tu paramārthata(*s ta)thātathotpadyamānapadārtharūpā eva vyāvṛttimātrakalpitabhedāḥ, na tu tatrāpi vastutaḥ kriyākārakabhedo dharmadharmirūpaḥ.
111.0 nanu mā bhūt samvitter anyo grahaḥ, saiva tu sātādirūpeṇāntaḥsamviditā bahirdeśastharūpādyākāragrāhiṇī samvedyate. tat katham advayasiddhir ity āśaṅkāyām āha– vyāpāra ityādi.
*vyāpāraḥ sātāder
*na kaścid etatparigrahe dṛṣṭaḥ |
*yugapatpratīyamānaṃ
*rūpādivad eva agrāhakam || 111
111.1 [ 29a1–29b1] tasya rūpādeḥ parigrahe sātādisamvedanasya vyāpāro
paricchedo 'ntar anyo 'yaṃ bhāgo bahir iva sthitaḥ |
jñānasyābhedino bhedapratibhāso hy upaplavaḥ ||
(Pramāṇavārttika Pra° 212)
nanu jñānajanakatvena rūpādikaṃ prāk saṃnidhatte paścāt sātādisamvittyutpattiḥ. tat kathaṃ yugapatpratīyamānatā? atrābhidhīyate–
yadi tāvad Vaibhāṣikamatena nirākāraṃ vijñānaṃ tadutpattipratibandhenākāravato 'rthasya grāhakam iṣyate, tadā pratītimātrasya sarvārthasādhāraṇatvena nīlasyeyaṃ pratītiḥ pītasya veti pratītipratiniyamābhāvād rañjanādyarthakriyārthino niyamena nīlādau pravṛttir na syāt. tataḥ sutarāṃ nārthavedanam.
atha Sautrāntikanayenākāravad vijñānaṃ prāgbhāvino janakasyārthakṣaṇasya tadutpattyā grāhakam iṣyate, tadā jñānakāle janakasyārthakṣaṇasya kṣaṇikatvenāsattvād ākāradvayasamvedanābhāvāc ca jñānākāra eva kevalaṃ samvedyata ity artharūpaṃ khapuṣpaṃ nātiśeta iti katham arthāṅgīkāraḥ?
111.2 arthābhāve deśakālākārārthakriyāniyamenopalambho nopapadyata ity arthāpattyā nityaparokṣasyārthasya siddhir iti cet, na, asaty api deśādiniyate bāhye vastuni vāsanāniyamāt svapnādivan niyatadeśādyupalambho bhaviṣyatīti kim anarthakalpanayātmānaṃ vipralabhemahi?
111.3 [ 29b1–30a7] atha syāt svapnādāv api deśāntaradṛṣṭaṃ bāhyam eva nimittam. yad āha–
svapnādipratyaye bāhyaṃ sarvathā na hi neṣyate |
sarvatrālambanaṃ bāhyaṃ deśakālānyathātmakam ||
(Ślokavārtika Nir° 107cd-108ab)
iti. tat kathaṃ svapnādidṛṣṭāntena jāgraddaśābhāvivijñānasya deśādiniyamo varṇyata iti? tad asat, svapnādijñānasyālambanānupapatteḥ. tad dhi grahaṇarūpaṃ vā syāt smaraṇarūpaṃ vā. tatra na tāvad grahaṇam, grahaṇasya hi pūrvagrahaṇānapekṣatvān na pūrvadarśanaviṣayagrāhitāpratiniyamaḥ syāt, darśanāntaravat.
smaraṇarūpatāpy asya nirvikalpatvāt, vartamānaviṣayatvāt, sphuṭābhatvāc ca nopapadyate. tataḥ katham asya deśāntarādidṛṣṭavastuviṣayatvam iti na pratīmaḥ.
athāstu tan nimittam. deśādiniyamas tu tannimitto na śakyate samarthayitum. yatraiva hi deśādau saṃnihitaṃ bāhyaṃ svapne 'pi yadi tatraiva pratibhāseta syārarthavirahe 'py arthakriyā svapne pratīyata eva. yathaiva jāgraddaśāyāṃ vanitānimittāḥ sātādayas tatpratibaddhāś ca visṛṣṭyādayo 'rthakriyāḥ sākṣād anubhūyante tathaiva svapnāvasthāyām api.
tato yathā svapnādau bahirartham antareṇa deśaniyamādayo bhavanti tathā jāgraddaśāyām api bhavantaḥ kenābhibhūyante? yad uktam–
kasyacit kiṃcid evāntarvāsanāyāḥ prabodhakam |
tato dhiyāṃ viniyamo na bāhyārthavyapekṣayā ||
(Pramāṇavārttika Pra° 336)
iti. tat samvedanasya bāhyanimittatvābhāve kathaṃ rūpādisātādyoḥ pūrvāparabhāve yugapatpratīyamānatāvirahadoṣopavarṇanam upapannaṃ bhaviṣyatīti na vidmaḥ.
nanu yugapatpratīyamānatāyām api rūpādigrāhakatvena niścīyamānasya sātāder grāhitvam eva. arthasya tu rūpādeḥ sakalapratipattṛsādhāraṇatvena grāhyatvam eva, pūrvapūrvakāraṇakalāpād eva gamyam, tasya sāmānyaviṣayatvāt. tato 'sādhāraṇarūpādiyugapatpratīyamānaṃ jñānam eva, yogyadeśakair ajñātasya svayaṃ jñānāt.
112.0 [ 30a7–30b1] nanu cākṣaṇikam avayavyādirūpam ekam eva bāhyaṃ vastu, tasya pūrvāparakālasthāyitvāt, sādhāraṇatvam apy anumānena vijñāyata iti nāsambaddham ity āśaṅkāyām āha– dṛṣṭaṃ cetyādi.
*dṛṣṭaṃ ca yat sitādyaṃ
*tad eva kālāntareṣu dṛṣṭaṃ na | *anyo'nyena virodhād
*anayoḥ sātādivan nityam || 112
112.1 [ 30b1-4] yat sitādyaṃ śuklarūpādikam epadgrahaṇaprasaṅgo durvāraḥ syāt.
112.2 uktaṃ cācāryeṇa bāhyārthanirāsārtham Ātmasādhanāvatāre–
nanu grāhyādirūpeṇa pratibhāsamānaḥ kathaṃ vikalpamātrātmakaḥ saṃsāro bhaved iti cet, na, pratītiviṣayasya grāhyāditvenāprasiddheḥ. na hi rūpādipratibhāsavyatirekeṇānyad grāhyaṃ grāhakaṃ vā kiṃcid upalabhyate. na cāpratīyamānasvarūpaṃ sadvyavahāraviṣayaṃ nāmātiprasaṅgāt.
na ca tatsambaddhapratītau tatpratītiḥ, atyantāpratyakṣātmanā kasyacit sambandhāprasiddheḥ.
antaḥpratibhāsamānasya sātāder grāhakatvaṃ nīlādes tu tadbahīrūpatayā pratītalakṣaṇasya grāhyatvam iti cet, na, sukhāder nīlādau vyāpārāsiddheḥ. na cāpratītavyāpāraṃ grāhakaṃ nāma, tathā sati bāhyasyācid grāhakaṃ nāma. tathā cāha–
na hi Subhūte dharmo dharmaṃ jānāti (Aṣṭasāhasrikā Prajñāpāramitā p. xxx)
iti vistaraḥ. yat tūktam
ity api rūpāṇi dvābhyāṃ vijñānābhyāṃ vijñāyante (Loc. class. untraced)
ityādi, tat tadrūpādhyavasāyamātrāpekṣayā kvacit pudgalādideśanāvad ity adoṣaḥ. (Ātmasādhanāvatāra 52b3–53a4)
iti.
112.3 [ 30b4] tad iyatā grāhyagrāhakasādhakapramāṇanirāso darśitaḥ.
113.0 [ 30b4] idānīṃ bādhakapramāṇenārthānām asattvād vijñaptimātratāṃ khyāpayann āha– ekaṃ na cetyādi.
*ekaṃ na ca rūpāder
*ādyantādiprabhedato dṛṣṭeḥ |
*paramāṇuśo 'py adṛṣṭer
*anekarūpaṃ na bhavati tadvac ca || 113
113.1 [ 30b5–31b7] ayam abhiprāyaḥ. bāhyaṃ vastv (see 115d) artho guṇadravyādibhedena bahuprakāraḥ parair upavarṇyate.
tatra guṇānāṃ dharmāṇām āśrayo dravyam, tanniṣedhenaiva te samūlam unmūlitā bhavantīti na pṛthag dūṣaṇam abhidhīyate. na cāsati samavāyini samavāya iti taddūṣaṇam api nākhyāyate. dravyaṃ ca pṛthivy āp tejo vāyur ākāśaṃ kālo dig ātmā mana iti navavidham.
tatrātmānantaraṃ nirākartavyaḥ.
ākāśaṃ tu śabdaguṇam iṣṭam. tac caikam iti samānadeśatvāt sarvaśabdānāṃ bhedena śrutir na syāt, tataś ca samīpadeśa iva vyavahitadeśābhimato 'pi śabdaḥ śrūyeta. na cānyo 'pīty ekāntaḥ. dikkālayoś caikatvāt pūrvāparādipratyayāyogaḥ.
syād etad upādhīnāṃ bhedenāyaṃ pratyayavibhāga iti. kim upādhīnāṃ svataḥ paurvāparyam uta parataḥ? tatrādye pakṣe tata eva tatpratītisiddher niḥprayojanāv etau. dvitīye tu pakṣe yadi dikkālābhyām eva tadā tad evedam ekatvād anayor na saṃgacchati. anyataś cet tadavasthaṃ tayor vaiyarthyam.
nityāś cāmī vyomadikkālāḥ sahakārisāhitye tadvirahe vālabdhasvabhāvabhedāḥ kathaṃ kadācid eva svakāryaṃ janayeyuḥ. tathā hi–
teṣāṃ śaktir aśaktir vā yā svabhāvena saṃsthitā |
nityatvād acikitsyānāṃ kas tāṃ kṣapayituṃ kṣamaḥ ||
(Pramāṇavārttika Pra° 22)
anena mano 'pi nirastam. yugapajjñānānutpattyā ca manovādināṃ mano 'numeyam. anubhūyanta eva ca nartakīdarśanādau yugapadvijñānāni, na caitan manasi sati kadācid yujyate.
tasmān na santy eva vyomādayaḥ.
pṛthivyādayo 'vaśiṣyante. te 'py avayaviparamāṇubhedena dvidhā kalpitāḥ. tatrāpi nāvayavī san nāpi paramāṇavaḥ santaḥ, tadubhayatrāpi bādhakasadbhāvād iti.
tad eva bādhakaṃ yathākramaṃ kathayati– ekam iti paramāṇubhir dvyaṇukādikrameṇārabdham avayavirūpaṃ prasiddhaṃ ghaṭādikaṃ ca naivāsti, rūpāder ādyantamadhyādiprabhedenānyo'nyavirodhinā dharmeṇa sthūlaikarūparahitasyaiva dṛṣṭer hetoḥ. tad anenaikajñānasaṃsargivastūpalambharūpo 'nupalambho hetur uktaḥ.
bhāgā eva hi bhāsante saṃniviṣṭās tathā tathā |
tadvān anyaḥ punar naiva nirvibhāgaḥ prakāśate ||
(Dharmakīrti, untraced/lost)
eśyāmaḥ.
idānīm ekaniṣedhe cānekarūpam avaśiṣyate. tac cānekair anekaprakāram upakalpitam. yathā Vaibhāṣikaiḥ sākṣād adhyakṣagocarāḥ, Vaiśeṣikais tv avayavyārambhakāḥ, Sautrāntikaiś ca nityaparokṣāḥ piśācāyamānā darśane svākārasamarpaṇapravaṇāḥ paramāṇava iṣyante. tatra tāvad Vaibhāṣikakalpitaparamāṇūn nirākartum āha– paramāṇuśo 'pītyādi.
yathā caikaṃ sthūlaṃ nāsti tadvad anekaṃ ca na bhavati. hetum āha– paramāṇuśo 'py adṛṣṭer iti paramāṇurūpasya parasparaviviktasyānupalabdher ity arthaḥ.
114.0 [ 31b7–32a1] atha sthūlānām anyathānupapattyā paramāṇvanumānam ity āha– niravayavatva ityādi.
*niravayavatve cāṇor
*anekaparamāṇuparikarābhāvāt |
*bhūmaṇḍalādirūpa-
*pracayo na hi kaścid atra syāt || 114
114.1 [ 32a1-6] ayam abhiprāyaḥ. sāvayavaṃ tāvat paramāṇavair na draṣṭavyam, yugapad aṃśair viruddhadigvartibhiḥ ṣaṭkayogādinā viruddhadharmasaṃsargiṇaḥ sthūlasyeva nānātvaprasaṅgāt. niravadi so 'pi tatra syāt. pratyāsattyabhāve 'py ābhimukhyamātre 'py ayam eva doṣaḥ.
tataś ca paramāṇumātrapiṇḍarūpaprasaṅgād bhūmaṇḍalādirūpapracayaḥ kaścid atra na hi syāt. yad āha–
saṃyuktaṃ dūradeśasthaṃ nairantaryavyavasthitam |
ekāṇvabhimukhaṃ rūpaṃ yad aṇor madhyavartinaḥ ||
aṇvantarābhimukhyena tad eva yadi kalpyate |
pracayo bhūdharādīnām evaṃ sati na yujyate ||
(Tattvasaṃgraha 1989-1990)
iti.
114.2 tasmād avaśyaṃ tayā tayoḥ svabhāvayor bhedo 'bhyupagantavyaḥ. yathā cānayos tathādharottaradakṣiṇottaraparamāṇupratyāsannānāṃ svabhāvānāṃ bheda iti ṣaḍaṃśataiva paramāṇor balād āpatati. tad āha–
ṣaṭkena yugapad yogāt paramāṇoḥ ṣaḍaṃśatā | ṣaṇṇāṃ samānadeśatvāt piṇḍaḥ syād aṇumātrakaḥ ||
(Viṃśikā 12)
iti.
114.3 [ 32a6] na caikāsiddhāv anekasya siddhir iti na santi paramāṇavaḥ.
114.4
mīmāṃsakaiś ca nāvaśyam iṣyante paramāṇavaḥ |
(Ślokavārtika 5,4.183ab)
iti. na paramāṇunirākaraṇamātreṇa nirākṛtaḥ syād avayavīti nāsya vyarthaṃ dūṣaṇam.
114.5 [ 32b2–33a1] astu tarhi vijñānamātrasvabhāvaṃ jagat. tad apy ekam anekaṃ vā rūpaṃ prāgvan na kṣamata iti, gaganābjam iva na sadvyavahāram arhati. vastusato gatyantarābhāvād ekānekayor anantareṇa tena bhāvyam. na ca tat tathā ghaṭata iti katham idaṃ sadvyavahāraviṣayam iti na jānīmaḥ. athaikānekasvabhāvām aśakyam apagantum.
na hi tad vijñānabahirbhāvanibandhanadūṣaṇaṃ yena tadbhāvena bhavet. mūrtinibandhanabādhakaṃ nāmūrte vijñānātmanīty apy asāram, nīlādyākāratāyāṃ vijñānasyāpi mūrtatvāt. ayam eva hi deśavitānavān ākāro mūrtiḥ, tad asya bāhyatve mūrtisaṃjñānatva iti na vidmaḥ.
114.6 syān mataṃ yeṣāṃ sākāraṃ vijñānam. teṣām ayam astu doṣaḥ. yeṣāṃ tu nirākāraṃ sukhādirūpaṃ tāttvikam iti pakṣas teṣām ayam ekānekaviyogaḥ kathaṃ saṃgaccheta? atrābhidhīyate.
iha prakāśātmakaṃ vijñānaṃ na ca prakāśamānanīlādivyatiriktaḥ teṣāṃ prakāśaḥ svapne 'pi samvidyate na ca sukhādivat prakāśamānasya nīlāder alīkatā yujyate bādhakasadbhāvād iti cet, tad etad bādhakaṃ
114.7 [ 33a1-2] sukhādau kena bādhyate sukhāder amūrtatvāt tadabhāva iti cet, naitad asti, tasyāpi deśavitānena prakāśamānasya śakyāpahnavatvāt. śrāntasya hi yāvanty aṅgāni payasi viśanti tāvatāṃ vyāpakaṃ sukham upajāyamānam anubhūyate.
nāpi tathānubhūyamānam adeśavitānaṃ nāma śarīrasyāpi tadbhāvaprasaṅgāt, deśavitāne ca śarīravad asyāpi mūrtatvāt. tad etad ākāravan nirākāraṃ vā vijñānaṃ bahirbhāvavirahitadūṣaṇaṃ nātikrāmatīti na pṛthag dūṣaṇam ādritam ācāryeṇeti pratipattavyam.
114.8 [ 33a2-7] tat sthitam etat– etad vijñānam api na sadvyavahāram arhatīti. tarhi jñānajñeyayor asattvād asadrūpaṃ tattvam iti syāt. na. samvṛttyā syād vijñānam, samvṛter aśakyāpahnavatvāt. astitvaniṣedhān nāstitvaṃ balād āyātam iti cet, na, astitvapūrvakatvān nāstitvasya, tadasiddhau tasyāpy asiddheḥ. yad āha–
astitvapūrvakaṃ nāsti asti nāstitvapūrvakam | ato nāsti na vaktavyam astitvaṃ na ca kalpayet ||
(Laṅkāvatārasūtra 3.83)
iti. nāpi sadasadātmatayobhayarūpaṃ sado (?) sadasator virodhāt. nāpi sadasadbhyām anyad evānubhayasvabhāvam, ubhayabahirbhūtasya paryudastasyāyogāt. na kiṃcit tarhi tattvam iti prāptam. nanv etad eva tattvaṃ yaduta tattvarahitatvaṃ nāma. tat sthitam etat–
catuḥkoṭivinirmuktaṃ vijñānaṃ pratibhāsate |
iti tattvavidāṃ tattvaṃ na tattvam api tattvataḥ ||
(untraced/original)
iti.
114.9 tad uktaṃ Jñānapādaiḥ–
astu tarhi vijñaptimātrasvabhāvam anasvarātmakaṃ jagad (Ātmasādhanāvatāra 53a4)
ityādy upakramya,
na, vi virodhāt.
nanu sarvathā nirātmakatve bhāvānāṃ kuto 'yam anubhūyamāno 'rthakriyābheda iti cet, avicāritaikātmanaḥ pūrvapūrvakalpanāmātrād eva tadanyasyāpratīter ity āveditam.
astu tarhi bhāvata eva sattvaṃ sarvabhāvānām vastutvasyārthakriyālakṣaṇatvād iti cet, tan na, pratibhāsamātreṇa sattābhyupagame 'virodhāt. yathoktakrameṇa lakṣaṇavivekād eva nairātmyaṃ vyavasthāpyate, na tu pratibhāsamātratayāpy apahnavād iti. yathāha–
na hi Subhūte 'nyad rūpam anyā śūnyatā, api tu rūpam eva śūnyaṃ rūpalakṣaṇena. (Prajñāpāramitā, untraced)
iti vistaraḥ. ata eva pūrvāparabhedena samastasāmvṛtavastupratibhāsamānatvān na nityatvam āśaṅkanīyam. yat tu
vijñaptimātraṃ bho jinaputrā yaduta traidhātukam ( Daśabhūmikasūtra p. 49)
ity uktam, tad atyantabhāvābhiniveśināṃ bahirarthādhyavasāyanirāsārtham. tasmin sati sukhenaiva cittamātrādhyavasāyasyāpi nirākaraṇasambhavāt. tad evāha–
cittamātraṃ samāśritya jñeyā bāhyā svabhāvatā |
tathatālambane sthitvā cittamātraṃ vyatikramet ,||
( Laṅkāvatārasūtra 10.256)
iti. (Ātmasādhanāvatāra 53a4–53b5)
114.7 [ 32a7] tad iyatā bādhakapramāṇasadbhāvo darśitaḥ.
115.0 [ 32a7–32b1] tataś ca bādhakapramāṇābhāvād bādhakasadbhāvāc ca jñeyādisamastavastunairātmyāj jagad idaṃ kalpanāmātranirmitasvabhāvam ity upasaṃharann āha– tad grāhakādītyādi.
*tad grāhakādivirahāt
*svatantrarūpādikalpanārūpam |
gandharvanagarasaṃnibham
akhilaṃ sacarācaraṃ vastu || 115
115.1 [ 32b1-2] tad ity anantaroktopapattisāmarthyato grāhakādivirahād gandharvanagarasaṃnibhaṃ paramārthasannimittarahitatvena ca kalpanāsantatirūpatayā svatantrarūpādikalpanārūpaṃ sthāvarajaṅgamaṃ vastu.
116.0 [ 33a7–33b1] nanu yady evaṃ tattvaṃ tadā śuddham eveti kathaṃ saṃsāraḥ, tadabhāvād vyavadānaṃ ca kathaṃ bhaved iti śaṅkāyām āha– ātmetyādi.
*ātmātmīyāropād *vikalpasantāna eṣa saṃsāraḥ |
*śuddhis tadviparīta-
*svarūpamātrā tu vijñeyā || 116
116.1 [ 33b2-4] tatraiva catuḥkoṭirahite cetasy ātmādy āropya vikalpasantānākāraḥ saṃsāraḥ. śuddhis tu mokṣaḥ punar ātmādyāropaviparītanairātmyasvarūpamātraniṣṭhatvena vijñeyā. ata evoktam–
kalpanāśuddhau yatitavyam. (Loc. class. untraced)
tathā hi bahirarthādhyavasāyī vikalpo bhrāntatvād bhāvānukūlaḥ prākṛtaḥ. svarūpamātraniṣṭhatvenābhrāntatvenāvikalpatvāt pratyakṣarūpaḥ pariśuddhaḥ. tad uktam–
kalpanāpi svasamvittāv iṣṭā nārthe vikalpanāt |
(Pramāṇasamuccaya 1.7ab)
iti.
116.2 rāgādayo 'py āropayanto dṛṣṭacittasvarūpaniṣṭāḥ. tad uktaṃ śrīParamādye–
rāgo dveṣaś ca mohaś ca traya ete viṣatāṃ gatāḥ |
viṣatvam upayānty ete viṣameṇa tu sevitāḥ |
amṛtatvaṃ punar yānti amṛtatvāya sevitāḥ ||
(Paramādya 220bX)
iti. tato vikalpaṃ bahirarthādhyavasāyadoṣād vyāvartya svarūpe 'vasthāpayituṃ yogibhir yatitavyam.
117.0 [ 33b4] tasmād vikalpo 'pi svarūpe nirvikalpa eveti svabhāvaśuddhāḥ sarvadharmāḥ. tad āha– na ca kaścid ityādi.
*na ca kaścid dharmatayā
*bhedaḥ –/.. śamabhavasvabhāvānām | *grāhyādyākṛtivirahaḥ
*sarvatra yato sadā siddhaḥ || 117
117.1 [ 33b4-6] kaścid iti nirvāṇam idaṃ saṃsāras tv ayam iyaṃ samalāvastheyaṃ tu nirmalāvasthā heyam idam upādeyam idam ityādir bhedo viśeṣo na ca naiva yukto dharmatayeti tattvataḥ. atropapattim āha– grāhyetyādi. ata evāryaNāgārjunapādair apy uktam–
nirvāṇaṃ ca bhavaś caiva dvayam etan na vidyate |
parijñānaṃ bhavasyaiva nirvāṇam iti kathyate ||
(Yuktiṣaṣṭikā 6)
na saṃsārasya nirvāṇāt kiṃcid asti viśeṣaṇam |
na nirvāṇasya saṃsārāt kiṃcid asti viśeṣaṇam ||
(Mūlamadhyamakakārikā 25.19)
iti.
117.2 bhagavatāpy uktam–
yā Subhūte pṛthagjanatathatā yā ca tathāgatatathataikaiva. (Prajñāpāramitā, untraced)
ityādi vistaraḥ.
118.0 [ 33b6-7] atha yadi tattvataḥ śamabhavasvabhāvānāṃ na bhedaḥ (see 117ab), kathaṃ tarhi grāhyādyākṛtivirahaḥ sarvatrety (see 117cd) abhidhīyate? sarvaśabdaḥ kilānekārthaviṣayaḥ, sa ca bhedābhāve prayogaṃ nārhatīty āśaṅkyāha– sarvaṃ na cetyādi.
*sarvaṃ na cāpi kiṃcit *tatpratibhāsasvabhāvasaṃkalpāt |
*na ca cittād anyaḥ syād
*aham iti kaścid ata ātmādiḥ || 118
118.1 [ 33b7–34a1] samvṛttyā tu śamabhavasvabhāvānāṃ bhedo (see 117b) 'sty eva, tataḥ sāmvṛtavastupratibhāsasvabhāvo yaḥ saṃkalpas tato 'nyan na kiṃcit sarvaṃ nāma. vikalpāropitabhedaviṣayaḥ sarvaśabdaḥ. tad evaṃbhūte sarvatra (see 117d) tāttvikabhedaniṣedhaḥ pratipādyata ity arthaḥ. evaṃ carācaram api na tatpratibhāsasvabhāvasaṃkalpād anyat.
118.2 [ 34a1–34b1] nanv ātmadarśanasya satyārthatve nairātmyābhāvena bhavasadbhāvād bhavaśamasvabhāvānāṃ (see 117b) kathaṃ bhedābhāva iti cet, tad asat. ātmasiddhatvāt, svabhāvaliṅgāyogāt. na cānyad vidhisādhanaṃ liṅgaṃ sambhavati yenānumānam asya syāt. anyena ca liṅgena bhavatā sādhyavyāptena bhāvyam.
sādhyasyātmanaś ca kvacid asiddhe vyāpakatvāniścayān na tadvyāptitvaṃ kasyacin niścetuṃ śakyata iti nāsya kiṃcil liṅgam upapadyate.
118.3 [ 34b1-4] nanu mā bhūc cakṣurādivijñānam anumānaṃ vā sādhakaṃ mānasam ahaṃpratyayarūpam artasya mūrtadravyānuvidhāyinī gamanakriyopapannā.
118.4 na cāyaṃ māṇavake siṃhapratyaya iva bhākto yuktaḥ, skhalanābhāvāt. na cāsati skhalane bhāktaparikalpanā yuktā, mukhyavyavahārāstaṅgamaprasaṅgāt.
118.5 [ 34b4-6] tataḥ sthitam etat– ātmanaḥ sādhakaṃ pramāṇaṃ nāstīti. sadvyavahārayogyatāstaṃgataiva. bādhakapramāṇasadbhāvena tv asyāsattvam eva yuktam. ātmā hi bhavan bodharūpo vā syād abodharūpo vā. tatrādye pakṣe cakṣurādivaiphalyaprasaṅgo 'nivāryaḥ. na hi nitye rūpādyupalambhātmany ātmani cakṣurādīnām asti sāphalyam.
abodharūpasyāpy ātmanaḥ sadbhāvam anupalambho bādhate.
118.6 tathā hi yasya yatropalabdhilakṣaṇaprāptasyānupalambhaḥ, tasya tatrābhāvo vyavahartavyaḥ, yathāśvaśirasi viśāṇasyopalabdhilakṣaṇaprāptasya tv ātmano 'nupalambhaḥ śarīrādāv iti.
118.7 nanu śarīrādyadhikaraṇatvam ātmanas tadvādinām aniṣṭam eva. yad āha–
kasya vā śarīra ātmā? (untraced)
iti. tathābhūtasya ca tasya niṣedhe na kiṃcid aniṣṭam. satyam. nāsmābhir apy atrādheyasya niṣedhaḥ kriyate, kiṃ tu saṃsargeṇa. evaṃ hi niṣedhaḥ sarvatrānupalambhena kriyate, ghaṭāder api niṣedhasya ca saṃsargitvāt. na cādheya eva saṃsargī bhavati, anyathā bāhyātmanānādheyena saṃsargābhāvāt. sātmakatvam api śarīrāder na syāt.
na ceha niṣedhyasya śarīrādikam adhikaraṇatvenopāttam, api tv anupalambhaniṣedhavyavahārayor iti na doṣaḥ.
nanv ayam anupalambho na tāvad upalambhābhāvaḥ, tasya sādhanatvāyogāt. nāpy ekajñānasaṃsargivastvantaropalambhasvabhāvadikam ālambamānaḥ sphuṭataram anubhūyate. ataḥ śarīrādikam evātmanā sahaikajñānasaṃsargi bhavati na ca tadvyatiriktam aparam ahaṃpratyayaviṣayabhūtam anubhūtam astīti tadupalambha eva tadupalambhaḥ.
nanu yadi śarīrādivyatirikto 'smady arthapratītiviṣayo na syāt, mama śarīram iti na syāt. asti caiṣa pratyayaḥ. tasmād asmadarthaḥ śarīrādivyatiriktaḥ pratīyamāna eva draṣṭatrakaśarīravyatirekapratyayapakṣe nikṣipati mama śarīrādikam ity evam ātmānam api nikṣiped ity alaṃ bahunā.
tasmād avasthitam etat– ādhārātmānam ātmanam anupalambho na sahata iti.
118.8 [ 34b6-7] tato 'sattvam evāsya yuktam iti sthitam.
118.9 [ 34b7–35a3] atha sahajasatkāyadarśanaṃ sakalalokasiddhaṃ katham apahnetuṃ śakyam ity āśaṅkyāha– na ca cittād ityādi. ayam abhiprāyaḥ. yathoktakrameṇa jñātṛjñeyādirūpasakalabhāvābhāvān na ca cittād yathoktarūpād anyaḥ kaścit sthiragrāhakādirūpo 'ham ity ahaṃkāraviṣayaḥ syāt sambhavet. ataḥ kāraṇāt prāguktacittam evāhaṃkāraviṣayaḥ.
tasmāt sarvathātmano 'bhāvāt keśādivijñānavad anyasya cātmadarśanasyāsatyārthatā. tato nāpi nairātmyābhāve bhavasadbhāvād bhavaśamasvabhāvānāṃ (see 117b) bhedaniṣedhābhāva iti.
119.0 [ 35a3] abhedam eva yathoktam upapādayann āha– yad rūpam ityādi.
*yad rūpaṃ pratipannaṃ
*tasya nijaṃ [yat] tad eva rūpaṃ kim | *tac[ ]cittābhedamater
*abhinnatā sarvabhāvānām || 119
119.1 tena ca cittena yad rūpaṃ yad vastu pratipannaṃ tac cittād anantarayukter bahir asiddham. atas tasya nijaṃ rūpaṃ kiṃ bhavatu cittapratītarūpād anyābhāvāt? tad eva rūpaṃ yac cittena pratipannam.
tataś ca vicāraṇam– bhedāprasiddhau yac cittam abhedaṃ manyate taccittasyābhedamatir yā mananakriyā tato hetoḥ sarvabhāvānām abhedena cetasā pratipannānām abhinnam eva nijaṃ rūpam. ity abhinnatvam eva teṣāṃ yuktaṃ siddhaṃ bhavati.
119.2 [ 35a3-5] athavā– yat sāmvṛtaṃ bālaiḥ pratipannaṃ tasya yan nijaṃ tāttvikaṃ rūpam abhrāntair dhīmadbhiḥ pratipannaṃ tasya tad eva nijam eva rūpam. yad yasmād evaṃ tat tasmāt sarveṣāṃ śamabhavasvabhāvānām abhedasya śūnyataikarūpatvena bhedābhāvasya mater avabodhād abhinnatā sarvabhāvānām.
119.3 tad uktam–
eko bhāvaḥ sarvabhāvasvabhāvaḥ
sarve bhāvā ekabhāvasvabhāvāḥ |
eko bhāvas tattvato yena dṛṣṭaḥ
sarve bhāvās tattvatas tena dṛṣṭāḥ ||
(Loc. class. untraced)
iti. Nāgārjunapādair apy uktam–
samvṛtivyatirekeṇa na tattvam upalabhyate |
samvṛtiḥ śūnyatā proktā śūnyataiva hi samvṛtiḥ ||
(ab = Bodhicittavivaraṇa 68ab; cd = Loc. class. untraced)
iti.
120.0 [ 35a5] sarvaṃ na cāpi kiṃcid (see 118a) ity etat sphuṭayann āha– ābhātītyādi.
*ābhāti yaś ca viṣayo
*yaś cānyaḥ kalpanāspadaṃ kaścit |
*tattadviyogavirahād
*ataś ca no sarvam anyo'nyaḥ || 120
120.1 [ 35a5-7] ābhātīti grāhyatayā pratibhāsate yaś ca rūpādirūpo viṣayaḥ, yaś cānyaḥ kaścid ahaṃkāraviṣayo grāhakarūpaḥ kalpanāyā āspadam āśrayaḥ, tasya tasya grāhyasya grāhakasya ca yo viyogo viyuktatvaṃ nānātvaṃ tasya virahān no sarvam anyo'nyo vibhidyate. ataś ca no sarvaṃ sarvaṃ nāma na kiṃcit paramārthataḥ śūnyatāmātrarūpatvāt.
nānātvaṃ tu kālpanikam eveti bhāvaḥ.
120.2 ata evācāryaDignāgenoktam–
pṛthagjanānāṃ yaj jñānaṃ prakṛtivyavadānikam |
uktaṃ tad buddhaśabdena bodhisattvo yathā jinaḥ ||
(Prajñāpāramitāpiṇḍārthasaṃgraha 37)
iti. ata eva ca Paramādye 'pi
sarvasattvās tathāgatagarbhāḥ samantabhadramahābodhisattvasarvātmatayā. (Adhyarthaśatikā Prajñāpāramitā 137ax)
ity uktam.
120.3 [ 35a7–35b3] tasmāt tattvato buddhābuddhatvasamatvād buddhān na bhedaḥ sattvānām, samvṛttyaiva bhedāt. evaṃ ca yat sambuddhīkṛtam akhilaṃ jagad (see 109cd) ity uktaṃ tad yuktam iti boddhavyam, svabhāvaśuddhatvāj jagataḥ. paramārthābhyāsāt tu vikalpo viśodhyaḥ, kalpanāvaśād buddhādibhedādhyāropadoṣāt.
evam eva sambuddhīkṛtanikhilajagadantarbhāvanaṃ nijamantre, tadbindau ca viśvasvabhāvamaṇḍalacakravibhāvanam upapannam eveti vijñeyam, jñeyādyabhāve svapratibhāsasyaiva tathotpādād iti sarvaṃ sustham.
121.0 [ 35b3-4] adhunā yathoktadharmatāṃ prastute yojayan devatānāṃ maṇḍalasya cābhedam eva dharmatayā darśayan sarveṣāṃ sarvaviśuddhisvabhāvatve 'pi vineyāśayena śraddhādiviśuddhipratiniyamena Yamāntakādirūpamaṇḍalacakrākāraṃ cittam āha– śraddhetyādi.
*śraddhāvīryaviśuddhyā
*tathā smṛtisamādhayor viśuddhyāpi
*saddvārapālarūpa-
*prajñāśuddhyā samastaviṣayābham || 121
121.1 [ 35b4-6] śraddhādiśabdair iha tadaṃśa eva kalpitabhedāpekṣayā prāgvad vaktavyaḥ. tatas tadviśuddhyā yathākramaṃ Yamāryādisaddvārapālacatuṣṭayarūparūpādīnāṃ prāgvat kalpanāmātratājñānaṃ prajñā, tacchuddhī Rūpavajrādidevatāropaḥ, tayā samastaviṣayābhaṃ rūpa(?)samastaviṣayākāraṃ ca cetaḥ.
ity ākalayya (see 127a) svamanaḥ paribhāvayed (see 127d) iti sambandhaḥ.
122.0 [ 35b6] punas tad eva cittaṃ viśinaṣṭi– animittetyādi.
*animittarativiśuddheḥ
*samastadevīgaṇasvabhāvaṃ tat |
*sattvārthātmavikalpa-
*pravṛttivijñānaśuddhes tat || 122
122.1 [ 35b6–36a2] animittaratir mohādipratyavekṣaṇāsaṅgaḥ. yad vā vastusannimittarahitatvenānimittyā mahāsukhasvabhāvā yā ratis tadviśuddheḥ samastaLocanādidevīgaṇasvabhāvaṃ tan maṇḍalacakrākāraṃ cetaḥ. sattvaḥ sattvavyavahārakāraṇatvāc cakṣurādipañcakam. artho rūpādiviṣayasyārthaḥ. ātmā kliṣṭamanaḥsvabhāvā satkāyadṛṣṭiḥ.
vikalpaḥ śabdasaṃsṛṣṭaṃ vijñānam. pravṛttivijñānāni pañca cakṣurādibuddhayaḥ. eṣāṃ śuddhiḥ samalatathatāvinivṛttiḥ,
*ādarśādimano'mala-
*rūpatvāt sarvabuddharūpaṃ ca |
*tathatāviśuddhiyogāt
*cakreśatayā vyavasthitaṃ caitat || 123
123.1 tato yathākramam ādarśādimanāṃsy evāmalāni viśuddhāni tadrūpatvād Vairocanādisarvabuddharūpaṃ tac (see 122d) cetaḥ. caḥ samuccaye.
123.2 tenāyam arthaḥ. sattvaviśuddher amalādarśajñānarūpam. tac ca
ādarśajñānam acalam. trīṇi samatādijñānāni tadāśritāni calāni ca. (Mahāyānasūtrālaṃkārabhāṣya ad 9.67)
ahaṃkārādiviparyāsarahitatvenāmamaṃ sarvadeśānugataṃ sarvakālānugataṃ ca tat.
sarvajñeyeṣv asammūḍhaṃ sadāvaraṇavigamāt, na ca teṣv āmukham anākāratvāt, samatādisarvajñānānāṃ sarvaprakārāṇāṃ hetutvāt sarvajñānākaropamam, sambhogādinimittaṃ ca. ( Mahāyānasūtrālaṃkārabhāṣya ad 9.68-69)
tad uktaṃ Sūtrālaṅkāre–
ādarśajñānam acalaṃ jñānatrayaṃ tadāśritam |
samatā pratyavekṣākhyaṃ kṛtyānuṣṭhānam eva ca ||
ādarśajñānam amamam aparicchinnaṃ sadānugam |
sarvajñeyeṣv asammūḍhaṃ na ca teṣv āmukhaṃ sadā ||
sarvajñānanimittatvān mahājñānākaropamam |
sambhogabuddhatājñānapratibaddhodayāc ca tat ||
(Mahāyānasūtrālaṃkāra 9.67-69)
iti. sarvatrāgantukavibandhamalānāṃ nirākaraṇe tathatānairmalyena prabhāsvaratvāc ca tad ādarśajñānaṃ jagatpratibimbodayāt. tad uktam Ātmasādhanāvatāre–
āgantukamalāpagame prabhāsvaratvāt (Ātmasādhanāvatāra 57a5)
iti. daśabalatve bhāvaṃ ca tat, yatas tathatākaraṇamukhena dharmāṇāṃ svarūpaṃ pravicinoti. tataś ca daśabalasvabhāvam.
tāni ca balāni– sthānāsthānajñānabalam, vipākajñānabalam, adhimuktijñānabalam, cyutyupapattijñānabalam, sarvatragapratipattijñānabalam, indriyaparāparajñānabalam, samādhisamāptijñānabalam, divyacakṣurjñānabalam, pūrvanivāsānusmṛtijñānabalam, āsravakṣayajñānabalaṃ ceti. tad uktaṃ Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaye–
yatas tathatākaraṇamukhena dharmāṇāṃ svarūpam avatarati, tataś ca balabhāvitam ādarśajñānam. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya 305a3-4, 149c28–150a3)
iti. tadrūpatvād Vairocanarūpaṃ tac cetaḥ.
arthaviśuddheś cāmalasamatājñānasvabhāvam. tac ca
yad bodhisattvasyābhisamayakāle samatājñānaṃ pratilabdhaṃ tad bhāvanāviśuddhito bodhiprāptasyāpratiṣṭhitanirvāṇaniṣṭhaṃ samatājñānam iṣyate, mahāmaitrīmahākaruṇābhyāṃ sarvakālānugataṃ ca yathādhimokṣaṃ ca sattvānāṃ buddhabimbanidarśakam, yataḥ kecit sattvās tathāgataṃ nīlavarṇaṃ paśyanti kecit pītavarṇam ityādi.
(Mahāyānasūtrālaṃkārabhāṣya ad 9.70-71)
tad uktaṃ Sūtrālaṅkāre–
sattveṣu samatājñānaṃ bhāvanāśuddhito matam |
apratiṣṭhitaśamāviṣṭaṃ samatājñānam iṣyate ||
mahāmaitrīkṛpābhyāṃ ca sarvakālānugaṃ matam |
yathādhimokṣaṃ sattvānāṃ buddhabimbanidarśakam ||
(Mahāyānasūtrālaṃkāra 9.70-71)
iti. anābhogavāhimaitryādisvabhāvatayā gaganavadanantasattvasantatau mṛdvādibhedabhinnāyāsūtaś ca (?) bhedānupalambhāt samatājñānam iṣyate. tad uktam Ātmasādhanāvatāre–
anālambanakṛpādyātmatayā santānāntare bhedāparāmarśāc ca samatājñānam. (Ātmasādhanāvatāra 57a5-6)
iti. bhūtakoṭyā tadadhigamotkarṣeṇa sarvatra niḥśaṅkatayā caturvaiśāradyalakṣaṇaṃ samatājñānam. tāni ca vaiśāradyāni– dharmārohaṇavaiśāradyam, dharmadeśanāvaiśāradyam, niryāṇamārgāvatāraṇavaiśāradyaṃ ceti. tad uktaṃ Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaye–
bhūtakoṭyā tadadhigamotkarṣam ato vaiśāradyalakṣaṇaṃ samatājñānam. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya 305a4, 150a3-4)
iti. etadrūpatvāc ca Ratnasambhavarūpaṃ tac cetaḥ.
ātmaviśuddhitaś ca nirmalapratyavekṣaṇājñānarūpam. pratyavekṣaṇājñānaṃ ca sadā sarvākārasarvajñeyavisarasākṣātkāri, sakaladhāraṇīsamādhyākaropamam, HariHaraHiraṇyagarbhādīnāṃ sarvaśrāvakapratyekabuddhānāṃ bodhisattvānāṃ cāgocararddhipradarśakam, sarveṣāṃ sarvasaṃśayavicchedakam, yathādhimokṣaṃ mahādharmāmṛtapravarṣakaṃ ca. yad uktaṃ Sūtrālaṅkāre–
pratyavekṣakaṃ jñānaṃ jñeyeṣv avyāhataṃ sadā | dhāraṇīnāṃ samādhīnāṃ nidhānopamam eva ca ||
parṣanmaṇḍaleṣu sarveṣu sarvavibhūtīnāṃ nidarśakam | sarvasaṃśayavicchedi mahādharmapravarṣakam ||
(Mahāyānasūtrālaṃkāra 9.72-73)
iti. Ātmasādhanāvatāre 'py uktam–
sarvajñeyāviparyāsāt pratyavekṣaṇājñānam. (Ātmasādhanāvatāra 57a6)
iti. animittyena sūkṣmakalpanāviśodhanāc catuḥpratisamvitsvarūpaṃ pratyavekṣaṇājñānam. pratisamvidaś ca– granthapratisamvit, arthapratisamvit, niruktipratisamvit, pratibhāsapratisamvic ceti. tad uktaṃ Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaye–
animittena sūkṣmakalpanāviśuddhim ataḥ pratisamvitsvabhāvaṃ pratyavekṣaṇājñānam. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya 305a4, 150a4-5)
iti. etadātmatvāc cĀmitābharūpaṃ tac cetaḥ.
vikalpaviśuddheś cāmalakṛtyānuṣṭhānajñānarūpam. kṛtyānuṣṭhānajñānaṃ tu
sarvalokadhātuṣu nirmāṇair nānāprakārair aprameyair acintyaiś ca sarvasattvārthakārakam. tac ca buddhanirmāṇaṃ sarvadā sarvathācintyaṃ veditavyam, kāryakriyābhedataḥ saṃkhyātaḥ kṣetrataś ca. (Mahāyānasūtrālaṃkārabhāṣya ad 9.74-75)
tad uktaṃ Sūtrālaṅkāre–
kṛtyānuṣṭhānatājñānaṃ nirmāṇaiḥ sarvadhātuṣu |
vicitrāprameyācintyaiś ca sarvasattvārthakārakam ||
kṛtyaniṣpattibhir bhedaiḥ saṃkhyākṣetraiś ca sarvadā | acintyaṃ buddhanirmāṇaṃ vijñeyaṃ tac ca sarvathā ||
(Mahāyānasūtrālaṃkāra 9.74-75)
iti. Ātmasādhanāvatāre coktam–
samastajagaddhitādvāra(?)hetutayā kṛtyānuṣṭhānajñānātmakam. (Ātmasādhanāvatāra 57a6)
iti. dharmadhātunā sarvadharmasāratvādhigamāśrayatvaniścayān mahāmaitrīmahākaruṇākāraṃ sarvasya jagato duḥkhataddhetuviyogahitasukhādhānahetutayā. tad uktaṃ Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaye–
dharmadhātunā sarvadharmasāratādhigamāśrayatvaṃ ca tato mahāmaitrīkaruṇākāraṃ kṛtyānuṣṭhānajñānam. (Mahāyānalakṣaṇasamuccaya 305a4-5, 150a5-6)
iti.
etac ca jñānacatuṣṭayaṃ dhāraṇāder utpadyate. tatra dhāraṇāc chrutānāṃ dharmāṇām ādarśajñānasamudbhavaḥ. samacittāt sarvasattveṣu mahāmaitrīmahākaruṇābhyām ātmasamatayā samatājñānasya samudāgamaḥ. samyagdharmaprakāśanahetukaṃ pratyavekṣaṇājñānam. sarvasattvakṛtyānuṣṭhānahetukaṃ kṛtyānuṣṭhānajñānam. tad uktam–
dhāraṇāt samacittāc ca samyagdharmaprakāśanāt |
kṛtyānuṣṭhānataś caiva caturjñānasamudbhavaḥ ||
(Mahāyānasūtrālaṃkāra 9.76)
iti. kṛtyānuṣṭhānasvabhāvatvāc cĀmoghasiddhirūpaṃ ca tac cetaḥ.
pravṛttivijñānaviśuddheś cāmalasuviśuddhadharmadhātujñānarūpam. suviśuddhadharmadhātujñānaṃ tu śrāvakādya nāsti vimuktijñānadarśanahāniḥ, trividhaṃ kāyavākcittakarma jñānapūrvakaṃ jñānānuvarti, traiyadhvikam asaṅgam apratihataṃ jñānaṃ ceti.
etadrūpatvāc cĀkṣobhyākāraṃ ca tac cetaḥ.
123.3 tathatāviśuddhir mārgāśrayaparāvṛttinirmalatathatāsarvākārajñatālakṣaṇā, acintyopalakṣyapratipakṣākārā, sthitasthāpitabādhakarūpā puṣpākārajanmaprakhyā, tadyogāt tadrūpatayā cakreśatayā vyavasthitaṃ caitac cetaḥ.
123.4 sā ca mārgāśrayaparāvṛttir viśeṣāśrayaprayogādibhedād bhidyate.
tatra viśeṣaḥ kṣetradarśanadeśanāvibhutvapratilambhaviśeṣataś caturdhā. tatra tūlopamamāṇikyamayādikṣetraviśuddhiḥ kṣetraviśeṣaḥ. darśanapratilambhaviśeṣaḥ sarvākārajñatājñeyapratyakṣībhāvataḥ. deśanāpratilambhaviśeṣo gāmbhīryodāryavicitrānekamukhāprameyadeśanataḥ.
vibhutvapratilambhaviśeṣaḥ sattvakṛtyānuṣṭhānāśrayāprameyāvyāhatābhijñādiguṇapratilambhaviśeṣaḥ.
āśrayaś caturdhā, ālambanaprayogānuśaṃsaparijñānabhedāt. tatrālambanaṃ tantrādideśanā, tadālambanam āśrityotpatteḥ, tadadhimuktim, tanniścayam, sambhārapūriṃ ca.
prayogataḥ– animittāprameyāsaṃkhyeyavaśitākāralakṣaṇatvenācintyadūrīkaraṇam, anuttarasukhādhānam, kleśajñeyāvaraṇaviśuddhitatpṛṣṭhalabdhajñānasarvākārajñeyapraveśaḥ, buddhakṣetrapariśuddhiḥ, sattvaparipākasarvajñatārpaṇam.
teṣām āśrayaṇe sthirīkaraṇe tena ca prayogaḥ prayuktiś caturdhā– ādikalpitaḥ, kiṃcijjñānavaśī, prāptajñānavaśī, samyagjñānavaśī ceti. tena bhidyate.
vastu caturvidham, tadbhedād api bhidyapāyābhāvāt.
arthaḥ punar eka eva.
prayojanaṃ trividham, praṇidhānālambanaprayogabhedāt. tadyathā– sattvārthaṃ pratyāsannasamyaksambodhipraṇidhānataḥ, deśanayā sattvārthālambanataḥ, samādhibhedapratiṣṭhānaprayogataś ca.
svabhāvaviśeṣaḥ mahāsukhasvabhāvatvāt.
guṇabhedas tu– parārthatvena prakṛṣṭatvam, , saṃkleśahetor ālayavijñānasyāvartanam, saṃkleśān nivartanam, āyatatvam ā saṃsāraṃ buddhānāṃ pravṛtteḥ, abhisambodhiparinirvāṇadarśanād dvayarūpatvam, saṃsāranirvāṇayor apratiṣṭhitatvena saṃskṛtāsaṃskṛtarahitatvenādvayatvam, kleśavimuktisāmyena śrāvakādisamatvam, balavaiśāradyādibhir buddhadharmair
asamatvād viśiṣṭatvam, sarvayānopadeśagatatvena sarvagamitvaṃ ceti. tad uktam–
pravṛttir udvṛttir avṛttir āśrayo
nirvṛttir āvṛttir atho dvayādvayā | samā viśiṣṭāpi sarvagāmikā
tathāgatānāṃ parivṛttir iṣyate ||
(Mahāyānasūtrālaṃkāra 9.14)
iti.
*rūpādibhramavirahād
*amalaskandhādibhāvasaṃsiddham |
*mātsaryādinivṛtteḥ
*pāramitābhūmiśukladharmamayam || 124
124.1 [ 36a4–36b2] rūpādiṣu yo bhrama ekatvādyāropas tasya virahād vicāreṇābhāvād amalāḥ kalpanārahitā ye skandhāyatanadhātavaḥ, teṣāṃ bhāvaḥ pāramārthikaḥ svabhāvaḥ, teṣāṃ saṃsiddhaṃ tadrūpaniṣpannaṃ tac cakreśatayā vyavasthitaṃ (see 123d) cetaḥ. mātsaryam atyāgitvaṃ tad ādi śuddhatvāc chuklā dharmāḥ saṃkleśamalarahitāḥ.
tanmayaṃ pāramitābhūmiśukladharmamayaṃ tac cetaḥ.
*avikalpasvaparodaya-
*nibandhanatvena [tasya ca] kramataḥ |
*gambhīrodāratayā
*prajñopāyātmakaṃ cetaḥ || 125
125.1 [ 36b2-3] avikalpo 'nābhogaḥ svarasavāhitvaṃ tena yaḥ svaparayor udayo laukikalokottaraphalaṃ tannibandhanatvena kramato 'bhyāsakramād gambhīrodāratayā prajñopāyātmakaṃ tac cetaḥ.
125.2 [ 36b3-6] etenaitad uktam. jñeyāvaraṇādiprahāṇalakṣaṇānābhogavāhisvodayanimittatvena gambhīratayā bhāvābhāvayor asthitatvenānavagāhatayā prajñāsvabhāvaṃ svarasavāhilaukikalokottaraparodayakāraṇatvenodāratayopāyarūpaṃ dayāsvabhāvam. etad ādhārādheyarūpaṃ saccakrākāraṃ ceta iti.
etac ca prajñopāyarūpatvam atra tatsvabhāvavidyāpuruṣarūpatvaṃ vijñeyam. tad uktam–
yā niḥsvabhāvatā prajñā upāyo bhāvalakṣaṇaḥ |
(Guhyasamājatantra 18.33cd)
iti. tad īdṛśam eva maṇḍalacakrākāraṃ cittaṃ sākṣāt sambodhimārgasatyam. uktaṃ ca–
śūnyatākaruṇābhinnaṃ bodhicittam iti smṛtam |
(Guhyasamājatantra 18.38cd)
iti.
126.0 [ 36b6] adhunā ca na ca kaścid ityādinā (see 117a) yo bhedābhāvo bhavaśamasvabhāvānām abhedo vā kathitaḥ, so 'pi na vāstava ity āha– paramārthatas tv iti.
*paramārthatas tv abhinnaṃ
*nābhinnaṃ kiṃcid iṣyate 'nyasmāt |
*anyagrahavinivṛttes
*tathāpratīter na citratvam || 126
126.1 [ 36b6–37a2] tattvatas tu vastu kiṃcid anyasmād abhinnam iti bhedarahitam, nābhinnam ity abhedara 'pratīteḥ.
126.2 tad etatpramāṇena sakalavāstavarūparahitaṃ pratītimātraṃ sāmvṛtaṃ tattvam iti sthitam. tathā ca sati yad etad bhedābhedādi vyavahartṛbhir vyavasthitaṃ tat sarvaṃ vikalpaśilpiracanāmātrād ity arthaḥ.
*ity ākalayya nijahṛdi
*nirmalacandrasthacihnamadhyastham |
*sanmantrabindurūpaṃ
*svamanaḥ paribhāvayed bhāsvat || 127
127.1 [ 37a3-5] itir evamarthe. evam anantaranirdiṣṭam artham ākalayyālocya nijahṛdi jñānasattvahṛdaye yo nirmalaś candras tatrasthacihnamadhyastham, sanmantro maṃkāraḥ sa eva svaraśmijajinaiḥ kṛtajinasvabhāvajagadantarbhāve saraśmikatvena bindvākāratvād binduḥ svarūpaṃ svabhāvo yasya svamanasas tatsampannabindurūpaṃ bhāsvat svamanaḥ paribhāvayet.
*taccittaraśmimālāṃ
*prabodhya [vākkāyavajratanuṃ] śreṣṭhām |
*punar atraiva niviṣṭāṃ
*jñānāmṛtavāhinīṃ dhyāyāt || 128
128.1 [ 37a6–37b2] tasya bindvākāramaṃkārākārasya cittasya raśmimālāṃ punar atraiva niviṣṭāṃ dhyāyād iti sambandhaḥ. kiṃ kṛtvety āha– prabodhyeti, tasyaiva sphāritaraśmimālayā cihnagarbhādikrameṇāvabhāsya vākkāyavajratanuṃ tathāgatakāyavajrādyabhinnatvena śreṣṭhām. anena ca raśmimeṇa jhaṭiti prāgvat samādhitrayarūpiṇaṃ svādhipam adhimuñced iti.
129.0 [ 37b2] idānīṃ vyomaparyantaspharaṇapūrvakasaṃharaṇam āha– antastanum ityādi.
*antastanum atha sarvām
*avabhāsya tayā samantato 'nantaiḥ |
*pratiromaprabhavābhā-
*vyūhair āpūrayan viśvam || 129
129.1 [ 37b2-5] atheti bindāv ādiyogamaṇḍalarājāgryādiniṣpādanānantaraṃ Mañjuvajrasyāntastanuṃ tayā praviṣṭaraśmimālayā cihnagarbhādikrameṇāvabhāsya pratiromaprabhavābhāvyūhair anantaiḥ samantato viśvam āpūrayaṃs tatraiva raśmimālāṃ krameṇa niviṣṭāṃ (see 128c) dhyāyād (see 128cd) iti pūrveṇaiva sambandhanīyam. ayam arthaḥ.
bindumadhye 'pi cakraṃ cintayan yāvadicchaṃ punaḥ punar evaṃ spharaṇasaṃharaṇe kuryād iti.
129.2 [ 37b5-6] iyatā binduyogaḥ karmarājāgrisaṃjñakas tṛtīyaḥ samādhir uktaḥ.
130.0 [ 37b6-7] binduyogopasaṃhārānantaraṃ sūkṣmayogabhāvanāvidhānam āha– sūkṣmam ityādi.
*sūkṣmaṃ svacihnam athavā
*bhāsvan nāsāgrasaṃsthitaṃ dhyāyāt |
*antargatamunicakraṃ
*kṛpayāniśam āntasatprajñaḥ || 130
130.1 [ 37b7–38a3] sūkṣmaṃ yavaphalamātrapramāṇaṃ svacihnaṃ kṛpāṇam, athaveti sthairya vidyānāsāgrasaṃsthāpitaṃ bhāvayed iti. kṛpayety upāyena.
130.2 [ 37b7] āntā gṛhītā satprajñā yena sa bhavaty āntasatprajñaḥ. prajñopāyamūrtir ity arthaḥ.
*sthairyanimittaṃ dṛṣṭvā
*spharaṇaṃ tadraśminirgatamunīndraiḥ |
*nānāvidhaiś ca cihnair
*vidadhīta punaḥ punar dhīmān || 131
131.1 [ 38a3-5] sthairyanimittam–
prathamaṃ marīcikākāraṃ dhūmākāraṃ dvitīyakam ||
tṛtīyaṃ khadyotakākāraṃ caturthaṃ dīpavaj jvalam |
pañcamaṃ tu sadālokaṃ nirabhragaganasaṃnibham ||
(Guhyasamājatantra 18.150cd-151)
iti. īdṛśaṃ sthairyanimittaṃ tasya sūkṣmayogasya dṛṣṭvā tad iti sūkṣmacihnaṃ tasya ye raśmayas tebhyo ye nirgatā munīndrās tair nānāvidhaiś ca cihnaiḥ padmakośavarāyudhair bodhisattvamahāmeghaiś ca spharaṇaṃ punaḥ punar vidadhīta dhīmān.
131.2 [ 38a5] iyatā sūkṣmayogo nirdiṣṭaḥ.
131.3 [ 38a5-6] binduyogasūkṣmayogayor api Mañjuvajrasya tadvidyāyā māṇḍaleyānāṃ ca sevopasādhanasādhanamahāsādhanāni yathoktaniḥsyandādiphalāni yathākramaṃ mṛdumadhyādhimātrāṇi prāgvad vijñeyāni.
131.4 [ 38a6–38b1] yat tu kaiścid uktam– binduyogasūkṣmayogayor api catvāri catvāry aṅgāny adhimātraniḥsyandādiphalānīti, tan na yuktam. yato bhagavato mṛdūny aṅgāni tatphalāni ca sambhavanti prajñāyāś ca madhyānīti, tato mṛdvādiniḥsyandādiphalānīti yujyate vaktum, nādhimātraniḥsyandādiphalānīti.
131.5 [ 38b1-5] atha tṛtīye samādhau sakalavipakṣakṣayasamarthatvād bhagavato 'py anicakram ity uktaṃ (see 130c) yasmād avaśyaṃ vipakṣakṣayatāratamyādiviśeṣārthāni taditarāṇi saniḥsyandāny aṅgāny eṣṭavyāni. tataś ca balād āpatitam ihāpy apekṣayā mṛdvādibhedena kiṃcit.
niḥsyandādyadhimokṣaiś ca bindusūkṣmatriguhyajāḥ |
dhyeyā mṛdvādibhir bhedair yogāḥ sevādivat tathā ||
(Guhyasamājamaṇḍalavidhi 113)
iti yad uktaṃ Bhadrapādaiḥ, tad apy evaṃvādibhir mahato 'pi mahīyobhir nirastaṃ bhavatīty alam aticintitena.
131.6 [ 38b5-6] tat sthitam etat– bindusūkṣmayor api mṛdvādibhedabhinnāni sevādyaṅgāni tanniḥsyandādiphalāni ceti. etac ca sarvaṃ pūrvamaṇḍalasthenaiva yoginā pratibhāsāntareṇādhimokṣato dhyeyam iti.
132.0 [ 38b7] japaṃ vaktum āha– japam ityādi.
*japam [anv adhi]kṛtacittaḥ
*samastabhāvātmani svacitteśe |
*[kṛtvā] citramayūkhaṃ *nijamantraṃ bhāvayan dhīmān || 132
132.1 [ 38b7–39a2] samastabhāvātmani viśvātmake maṇḍalacakrarūpe svacitteśe cittarāje viśuddhāhaṃkārādhimokṣeṇa kṛtacitto maṇḍalacakrākāracittātmety arthaḥ. nijamantraṃ svajñānasattvahṛdayasthamaṃkāraṃ svakāyavajrādihṛdayasthacihnāntargatoṃkārādibhir vidarbhitatvāc citramayūkhaṃ bhāvayan mantrī japaṃ kuryāt.
133.0 [ 39a2] katham ity āha– prāṇetyādi.
*prāṇāyāmasuyantritam
*[uccārya ... spaṣṭam ... mantrarājam] |
*kāyādīnāṃ spharaṇaṃ
*kramaśo yugapac ca kurvīta || 133
133.1 [ 39a2-4] prāṇāyāmābhyāṃ preraṇākarṣaṇābhyāṃ suṣṭhu yantritaṃ drutādirahitaṃ spaṣṭam uccāryeti spharaṇoccāraṇayoḥ samānakālatve 'py uccāraṇārambhasya prāgbhāvitvaṃ darśayituṃ ktvāpratyayena nirdeśaḥ. uccārayan sphārayen na ca sphārayann uccārayed iti. uktaṃ ca Śrīsamāje–
uccārayan sphared vajrān samāptau saṃhāram ādiśet |
(Guhyasamājatantra 13.13ab)
iti.
133.2 [ 39a4-6] tad evam uccārya kāyādīnāṃ spharaṇaṃ kurvīta kramaśo yugapac ceti kāyādyakṣarebhyo yathākramaṃ tebhyaḥ, yugapad vā pratyekaṃ daśadevītathāgatasahitānāṃ Kāyavajrādīnāṃ krameṇa [vā] spharaṇaṃ bhāvyaṃ kāyādīnāṃ daśadevītathāgatasahitānām eva spharaṇaṃ kāryam.
jñānasattvabījāt tu devītathāgatān eva, na tu jñānasattvam ubhayato 'pi sphārayed iti.
*viyadudaraṃ kāyādi-
*[mayūkha – – prabhābhir] āpūrya |
*prāgvad vicitra[pūjā-] *[vyūhair] niścārya [...] || 134
134.1 [ 39a6–39b1] tato mayūkhamukhabhāsibhir viyadudaraṃ gaganodaram āpūrya prāgvad iti dvitīyasamādhau kamalodare svabījaprabhābhir (95ab) ityādyuktavidhinā vicitraRūpavajrādi
*[satprajñāsaṅgamahā-]
*[sukhena – – vaśīkṛtaṃ dṛṣṭvā |]
*[vistīrṇamunīndraughaṃ]
*[svātmany ante] [niveśayet] || 135
135.1 [ 39b1-4] (*etenaitad uktam. sūkṣmayogaṃ saṃhārya cakrādi)cihnamadhyasthoṃkārādibhyo devītathāgatān Kāyavajrādīn pratyuccāraṇakālaṃ krameṇa yaugapadyena vā niścārya (see 134d?) sapūjaṃ pratisaṃhāraṃ praveśayan jāpaṃ kuryād iti mantrajapaḥ.
136.0 [ 39b4] āpyāyanaṃ vaktum āha–
*nijahṛttattvanirūpaṇa-
*parikhedaparīttacittasaṃcāraḥ |
*[sad]vidyāsaṃgītaiḥ
*prabodhito 'bhyarcayet cakram || 136
136.1 [ 39b4-5] nijahṛd iti jñānasattvahṛdayasthacihnāntargatamaṃkāras tad evādiyogādisamādhirūpatvāt tattvaṃ tasya nirūpaṇaṃ dhyānaṃ tena parikhedaḥ śramas tena parīttaḥ saṃkucitaś cittasya saṃcāraḥ pravṛttir yasya sa tathā. itthaṃ khinnaḥ san pūrvaval Locanādividyāgaṇasaṃgītaiḥ prabodhitaś coditaḥ sann abhyarcayec cakram.
137.0 [ 39b5] āpyāyanam āha–
*saccittavāridhārāṃ
*prāṇāpānaprabhāṅkuśākṛṣṭām |
*khedāpanude vidhivan
*nipātayet Kāyavajrādau || 137
137.1 [ 39b6] saccittaṃ bodhicittam. tadrūpavāridhārāṃ prāṇāpānau niśvāsapraśvāsau tad eva prabhā saivāṅkuśas tenākṛṣṭām,
137.2 athavā prāṇāyāmavāyusahagataprabhāyojitām,
137.3 [ 39b6–40a1] khedāpanude svabījaraśmisparśād dravībhūtapraṇavasparśataḥ khedaśamanāya kāyādau nipātayet patantīṃ cintayet.
vidhivad iti mūrdhana upari vitastimātram atikramya vitastimātre śaśini praṇavaṃ pratibījādhiṣṭhitapañcadravyaparivṛtaṃ tadbījaraśmyākṛṣṭatathāgatair ekīkṛtaṃ jñānasattvabījaraśmisaṃcodanād dravīkṛtapraṇavasparśāt sarvam amṛtībhūtaṃ vicintya tena bodhicittavāridhārārūpeṇa kāyādīn sakalanāḍīsaṃcāreṇa saṃtarpayed ity arthaḥ.
*iti viśramya muhūrtaṃ
*pūrvoktavidhānataḥ [tu] tadvijñaḥ |
*sanmantrajāpam aniśaṃ
*kuryān mantre 'py asaṃsaktaḥ || 138
138.1 [ 40a1] itīty anantaroktavidhinā muhūrtaṃ viśramya pūrvoktavidhānata iti citramayūkhanijamantrabhāvanāvidhānena tadvijñaḥ sanmantrajāpam aniśaṃ kuryān mantre 'py asaṃsaktaḥ.
139.0 [ 40a1-2] samprati visarjanam āha– vihitetyādi.
*vihitastutipūjādir
*visarjane jātasambhramo mantrī |
*praṇavādyāhitacakraḥ
*kalpitakāyādikaṃ dhyāyāt || 139
139.1 [ 40a2-3] cakrabhāvanādivisarjane jātasambhramo jātādaro mantrī prāgvad vihitastutipūjādiḥ praṇavādinā tryakṣareṇa jñānasattvacakre bahiścakram āhitam antarbhāvitaṃ yena sa praṇavādyāhitacakraḥ kalpanāyattakāyādikaṃ yathāvat kalpitaṃ karmarājāgrisamīpatvāt tatraiva cāsya sūkṣmayogādeś cakropasaṃhāraparyantasyāntarbhāvaḥ, dhyāyāt paribhāvayet.
*evaṃvidhatattvadṛśā
*nirmuktaṃ sattvadhātum avalambya |
*kṛpayā jagaddhitārthaṃ
*praṇidhānāny āmukhīkuryāt || 140
140.1 [ 40a3-4] evaṃvidhā yā tattvadṛg devatācakrādyākārā tayā nirmuktaṃ sattvadhātum avalambya buddhvā kṛpayā jagaddhitārthaṃ satataṃ sarvathā jagadarthaṃ kuryām ityādi pūrvavat praṇidhānāny āmukhīkuryāt.
*utthāya śuddhasattvaḥ
*svadevatākāram udvahañ cetaḥ |
*[sakalaviṣayavyavasthān]
*prāg iva paribhāvayan viṣayān || 141
141.1 [ 40a4-6] tato dhyānād utthāya svadevatāhaṃkāraṃ ceta udvahan, ata eva śuddhasattvo devatākāratvena prākṛtarūpaśūnyatvāt, viṣayān paribhāvayan samastaṃ kṛtyaṃ sampādayed (see 143ab) iti sambandhaḥ. kiṃbhūtān viṣayān ity āha– prāg iveti grāhyagrāhakādivirahān māyopamān.
141.2 [ 40a6–40b1] ata eva sakalarūpādiviṣayavyavastheti. (?)
*kāyādivajrabhūtān
*yathākramaṃ svasvabhāvasaṃśuddhyā |
*dharmatayā tu śrīmad- *[vajradharākārān parasparābhinnān] || 142
142.1 [ 40b1-2] punar viśinaṣṭi– svasvabhāvasaṃśuddhyā yathākramaṃ kāyādivajrabhūtān. tathā hi– rūpaskandhasya rūpāyatanasya vā śuddhyā Kāyavajraḥ. vedanāskandhasya gandhasya vā viśuddhyā Ratnasambhavaḥ. saṃjñāskandhasya rasasya vā śuddhyĀmitābhaḥ. saṃskāraskandhasya sparśasya vā śuddhyĀmogheśaḥ.
vijñānaskandhasya śabdāyatanasya vā viśuddhyā Cittavajraḥ.
142.2 [ 40b2-4] īdṛśī tu viśuddhir nānādhimuktasattvānurodhena draṣṭavyā, paramārthatas tu sarvaviśuddhisvabhāvā eva buddhā boddhavyāḥ. tad evaṃ Kāyavajrādisvabhāvabhūtān viṣayān dharmatayeti tattvena tu parasparābhedena śrīvajradharākārāṃs tathatātmakavajradharadevatārūpān paribhāvayan (see 141d),
*sampādayet samastaṃ
*kṛtyaṃ viparītakalpanāmuktaḥ |
*[sakala/sarva]municakrapūjā
*parameyaṃ sarvadā tasya || 143
143.1 [ 40b4] ata eva viparītakalpanayā grāhyādirūpayā prākṛtāhaṃkārātmikayā ca muktaḥ parityaktaḥ samastaṃ saddharmavyākhyānabhramaṇādikaṃ kāmopabhogādikaṃ vā kṛtyaṃ sampādayed anutiṣṭhet.
143.2 [ 40b4-5] evaṃ ca yogino yathoktavidhinā devatāhaṃkārayuktasya sakalakṛtyasampādanam iyaṃ sarvadaiva paramā municakrapūjā, satatasamāhitatvāt tasya yoginaḥ.
143.3 [ 40b5] tad iyatā prātaḥsandhyā kathitā.
144.0 [ 40b5-6] adhunā bhojanavidhānam āha– hṛccandra ityādi.
*hṛccandre nijabījair
*bhojanasamaye 'valambya cakreśam |
*sampannabodhicitta-
*svabhāvam ābhāsvaraprabhaṃ dhyāyāt || 144
144.1 [ 40b6-7] nijabījair vuṃkārādibhiḥ samāṇḍaleyaṃ cakreśaṃ samayasattvasya hṛccandre bhojanasamaye 'valambya vicintya prāgvad amṛtāsvādavidhinā sampannabodhicittasvabhāvaṃ svabhojyam (see 145b) oṃkāreṇābhāsvaraprabhaṃ
*sarvasamatāviśuddhaṃ
*svabhojyam abhimantritaṃ tribhis tattvaiḥ |
*kāyādidevatānāṃ
*tena pariprīṇanam kuryāt || 145
145.1 [ 40b7–41a1] tribhis tattvair oṃ-āḥ-hūṃ-kārair yāvadiccham abhimantritaṃ sarvasamatāviśuddhaṃ dhyāyāt (see 144d). kāyavajrādidevatānāṃ tena pariprīṇanaṃ
hūṃnyastavajrasajjihvaḥ
(Guhyasamājamaṇḍalavidhi 101a)
kuryāt. tad evaṃ mantriṇā hṛdyantargatasakalamunicakrāpyāyanādhimokṣeṇa bhoktavyam.
146.0 [ 41a1-2] yogibhojanasya homarūpatvāt tatprastāve homavidhim āha– unneya iti.
*unneyo bāhyo 'yaṃ
*homavidhir mānasas tu manasaiva |
*skandhādīndhanadahanāj
*jñānajvalane tv anuttaraḥ khyātaḥ || 146
146.1
Vajrasattvasamādhisthaḥ kalpayan dṛḍhabuddhimān ||
homaṃ kurvīta mantrajñaḥ
(Guhyasamājatantra 16.32cd-33a)
ityādi ṣoḍaśapaṭalahomopayikānirdiṣṭavidhinā bāhyo homavidhir ghṛtādyāhutirūpa unneya ity anenaiva vidhinotkarṣeṇa neyo yojayitavyaḥ.
146.2 [ 41a2-3] mānasas tu homavidhir yo 'yaṃ yogibhojanasvabhāvaḥ sa manasaiva tadahaṃkārādhimuktyaiveti boddhavyam. jñānajvalane tv iti sarvabhāvasamatājñānāgnau punar anuttaro homaḥ, skandhādīny evendhanāni teṣāṃ dahanāt khyātaḥ. tad evaṃ bāhyo mānaso 'nuttaraś ceti homatrayam uktam.
*evaṃ samastakāmair
*ātmānaṃ svaṃ svadevatārūpam |
*sampūjayet paraṃ vā
*samayaḥ sugatair mataḥ samyak || 147
147.1 [ 41a3-6] evam ity uktakrameṇa samastakāmai rūpādibhir ātmānaṃ svam ātmīyaṃ paraṃ vā svadevatārūpaṃ sampūjayet. evaṃ ca svaparayor avasāyamātrāropitabhedayos tattvatas tu yathoktasvapratītirūpayor uktadevatākārayor viśuddharūpādīnāṃ pūjanam anullaṅghanīyatvāt samayaḥ sugataiḥ samyaṅ mataḥ. tad uktam–
sarvakāmopabhogais tu sevyamānair yathecchataḥ |
svādhidaivatayogena svaṃ parāṃś caiva pūjayet ||
duṣkarair niyamaiḥ kaṣṭaiḥ sevyamāno na sidhyati |
sarvakāmopabhogais tu sevayann āśu sidhyati ||
(Guhyasamājatantra 7.2-3)
iti.
147.2 nanu katham ātmana eva sarvakāmopabhogādikaṃ pūjārūpaṃ śreyasāṃ kāraṇaṃ śrāvakādīnāṃ rūpādyu Ātmasādhanāvatāra 59a2-3)
147.3 na tarhi pratimāder apy abhimatadevatāsvarūpasyātmavyatiriktasya pūjyatvaṃ syād iti cet, tan na, Ātmasādhanāvatāra 59a3-4)
147.4 yad apy uktaṃ śrāvakādīnām upabhogasya pratiṣedhād iti tad apy anupapannam, sāmānyena pravṛttasya pratiṣedhavākyasya viśeṣavidhinā bādhyamānatvāt. ekaviṣayatve hi vidhipratiṣedhavākyayor virodhaḥ syāt, na nānāviṣayatve. ( Ātmasādhanāvatāra 59a4-5)
147.5 tathā hi śrāvakasya bahirarthābhiniviṣṭasyānupāyakuśalasyāviditavastutattvasya bhikṣoś
cakṣuṣā samvaraḥ sādhuḥ sādhuḥ śrotreṇa samvaraḥ |
ghrāṇena samvaraḥ sādhuḥ sādhur jihvayā samvaraḥ |
kāyena samvaraḥ sādhur manasā sādhuḥ samvaraḥ | sarvatra samvṛto bhikṣuḥ sarvaduḥkhād vimucyate ||
(Mahāsāṅghikaprātimokṣasūtra p. xx)
ityādyāgamavākyasya sāmānyena kāmopabhoganiṣedhaparasya. pratipannavastutattvānāṃ nipuṇataradṛṣṭīnāṃ dṛḍhadevatāhaṃkārāṇām upāyakuśalānāṃ yogināṃ tu
rūpaṃ vijñāya trividhaṃ devatānāṃ nivedayet |
(Guhyasamājatantra 7.8a & 9b)
sarvakāmopabhogais tu sevayann āśu sidhyati ||
(Guhyasamājatantra 7.3cd)
kāyavākcittasausthityaṃ prāpya bodhiṃ samaśnute |
anyathākālamaraṇaṃ pacyate narake dhruvam ||
(Guhyasamājatantra 7.5)
147.6 [ 41a6-7]
duṣkarair niyamais tīvrair mūrtiḥ śuṣyati duḥkhitā | duḥkhād vikṣipyate cittaṃ vikṣepāt siddhir anyathā ||
(Paramādya 242bx)
147.7
nāsti kiṃcid akartavyaṃ prajñopāyena cetasā |
nirviśaṅkāḥ sadā bhūtvā bhuñjatha kāmapañcakam ||
(Guhyendutilaka 271ax)
ityādinā
pramuditasya prītir jāyate prītimanasaḥ kāyaṃ praśrabhyate praśrabdhakāyaḥ sukhaṃ vedayati sukhinaś cittaṃ samādhīyate samāhitacitto yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti. (Śrāvakabhūmi p. xx)
ityādyāgamoktena viśeṣeṇa kāmopabhogavidhāyakena bhinnaviṣayatve bādhitatvāt.
147.8
kartavyasvarūpāviśeṣān nānāviṣayatvānupapattir iti cet, na, ḍitotpādanavat, pṛthivīsaṃjñayā jalade nimagnāyā api krūroddharaṇavac ca. bodhisattvānāṃ tu yāvat kṛpādiśubhamanaskāraprabhāvitaṃ karma, tāvad anujñāviṣayaṃ, bodhisattvamārgasya tallakṣaṇatvāt.
nāpattiḥ śubhacittasya snehāc caiva dayāvataḥ |
(Bodhisattvasamvaraviṃśikā 20cd)
iti. tathā–
niṣiddham apy anujñātaṃ kṛpālor arthadarśinaḥ |
(Bodhicaryāvatāra 5.84cd)
punaś coktam–
saṃkalpād bodhisattvānāṃ
śubhaṃ vā yadi vāśubham |
sarvaṃ kalyāṇatām eti
teṣāṃ vaśyaṃ yato manaḥ ||
(Catuḥśataka 5.5)
iti. upāyakauśalyavyatirekeṇa prāṇātipātādinivṛttivyavasthāpanāt, upāyakuśalasya tu tadabhyanujñānāt. yad āha Trayastriṃśe Vairocanābhisambodhau ca–
iha bodhisattvaḥ sarveṇa sarvaṃ prāṇātipātāt prativirato bhavati yāvad anyān sattvān paśyati ratnatrayāpakāriṇa iti. evam adattādānād virato bhavati yāvad anyān sattvān paśyati matsariṇaḥ. tathā paradārebhyaḥ paraparigṛhītebhyo 'bhidhyācittam api notpādayati kiṃ punar āliṅganena dvīndriyasamāpattyā vā pravartate. (Vairocanābhisambodhi, untraced)
anyatra tathārūpeṣv akaraṇīyeṣv iti vistareṇa sarvaśikṣāvibhāgo draṣṭavyaḥ. lābhādyāśayasamutthāpitaṃ hi kāyavākkarmāniṣṭaphalatvād akuśalaṃ na tu svarūpeṇa tasyāvyākṛtasvabhāvatvāt. yat tu paropakārādhyāśayaprabhāvitaṃ tad anuttaraphalāvāhakatvād bodhisattvaśikṣāsvabhāvam. na cātmīyam evaṃ vṛttaṃ paripālayan bodhisattvaḥ sāvadyaḥ syāt.
yad āhĀryākṣayamatinirdeśe–
avijugupsamānā sarvopāyakauśaleṣu (Akṣayamatinirdeśa, untraced)
iti. tulye 'pi hi viṣayādibhoge 'dhyāśayavaśena sāvadyānavadyavyavasthāpanāt kliṣṭādhyavasāyino hi kliṣṭo viṣayopabhogaḥ, akliṣṭāśayasya tv akliṣṭa iti sarvaṃ sustham. (Ātmasādhanāvatāra 59a5–60a5)
147.9 ata eva vakṣyati– evaṃvidhe cetyādi (see 149a), ity astasakalakaluṣe cetasi kṛṣṇaṃ kutaḥ karmeti (see 151cd) ca. yac cedaṃ samastakāmaiḥ svaparapūjanaṃ so 'yam anullaṅghanīyatvāt samayam.
147.10 [ 41a7–41b2] etac ca nipuṇadṛḍhadevatāhaṃkārān eva yogino 'dhikṛtyoktam. anyathā punar apāyapatanaṃ syāt. tad uktam–
sunirūpya susaṃcintya praveśaṃ kārayed budhaḥ |
anyathāgnipraveśo 'sya kalāṃ nārghati ṣoḍaśīm ||
tattvaṃ vijñāya yatnena yo 'dhimuktiṃ niṣevate |
sa sidhyaty anyathā tasya mahānirayapātanam ||
(Loc. class. untraced)
iti.
148.0 [ 41b2-3] idānīm amum evārthaṃ yaś cotthāya śuddhasattva (see 141a) ityādyukto 'rthas taṃ ca saprapañcaṃ sopapattikaṃ catasṛbhir āryābhir āha.
*anurūpaṃ jagadarthaṃ
*[samasta/samagra]sugatārcanāṃ ca paritulayan |
*kāyādikarma tat tat
*vidadhīta samāhito nityam || 148
148.1 [ 41b3-4] tat tat kāyādikarmānurūpaṃ jagadarthaṃ jagatāṃ laukikalokottaraprayojanaṃ paritulayann anurūpāṃ sugatārcanāṃ ca bāhyapuṣpādipūjāṃ paritulayan nityaṃ samāhito bāhyādhyātmikadevatāhaṃkāraśuddhātmā kāyādikarma bhramaṇādikaṃ tat tad vidadhīta.
evaṃvidhe ca cetasi
yat kiṃcit kāyavāṅmayaṃ karma |
mudrāmantrākāraṃ
gaditaṃ tat sarvasambuddhaiḥ || 149
149.1 [ 41b4-5] evaṃvidhe ca devatāhaṃkārapūrite cetasy evam adhimuktaṃ yat kiṃcit kāyavāṅmayaṃ karma mudrāmantrākāram eveti sarvasambuddhair gaditam.
150.0 [ 41b5-6] kutaḥ punar anyathāpratīyamānaṃ kāyādikarma mudrādyākāraṃ bhavatīty āśaṅkāyām āha– ekatraiva tv iti.
*ekatraiva tu vastuni
*nānāsaṃkalpasambhṛtākārāḥ |
*duḥkhasukhamuktijanakāḥ
*karmaviśeṣā bhavanti bahavo yat || 150
150.1 [ 41b6–42a3] pramadākāyādivastuni viraktaraktatattvavidāṃ yathākramaṃ ye kuṇapaḥ pramadā māyeti nānāsaṃkalpās taiḥ sambhṛtākārā utpāditasvabhāvāḥ karmaviśeṣā uttarottarānuṣṭhānādayo duḥkhasukhamuktijanakā bhavanti. evaṃ sarvavastuni yathā yathā saṃkalpas tathā tathā tadabhyāsāt pratibhāsaviśeṣā draṣṭavyāḥ. tad āha bahava iti.
tathā ca bahirarthanaye 'py abhihitam–
aviśeṣe 'pi bāhyasya viśeṣāt prītitāpayoḥ |
bhāvanāyā viśeṣeṇa nārtharūpāḥ sukhādayaḥ ||
(Pramāṇaviniścaya 1.23)
tathā–
parivrāṭkāmukaśunām ekasyāṃ pramadātanau |
kuṇapaḥ kāminī bhakṣyam iti tisro vikalpanāḥ ||
(Bodhicittavivaraṇa 20)
iti. bahirarthavirahe tu pratītimātraṃ kāmaṃ svāyattatvād yathābhyasyate tathā bhavatīti na doṣaḥ. yad iti yasmād evam anubhavasiddhaṃ
*kuśalākuśalākāraṃ
*kāyādyaṃ cetanāvaśād uktam |
*ity astasakalakaluṣe
*cetasi kṛṣṇaṃ kutaḥ karma || 151
151.1 [ 42a3-6] tasmāc cetanāvaśāt kāyādyaṃ karma kuśalākuśalākāraṃ bhavati na tu vastuta iti sugatair uktam. tasmāt saṃkalpavaśāt kāyādikarmaṇāṃ mudrādirūpatvaṃ saṃkalpapariśuddhyā viśuddher iti yuktam eva. tad uktam–
yena yena hi bhāvena manaḥ saṃyujyate nṛṇāṃ | tena tanmayatāṃ yāti viśvarūpo maṇir yathā ||
(Loc. class. untraced)
iti. itīty anantaroktanyāyena śubhamayatvād astasakalakaluṣe cetasi kṛṣṇaṃ karma kutaḥ sambhavet?
nāpattiḥ śubhacittasya
(Bodhisattvasamvaraviṃśikā 20c)
ityādyuktakrameṇa naiva sambhaved iti bhāvaḥ.
152.0 [ 42a6] idānīṃ khaṇḍitasamayotthāpanavidhim āha– samayetyādi.
*samayakṣatau yathāvad
*dhṛccandranyastaviśvahṛdvajraḥ |
*śrīsamayavajrayogī
*prakṛtiviśuddhaṃ jagad dhyātvā || 152
152.1 [ 42a6–42b2] samayo rakṣyādiḥ, tasya kṣatau taddoṣanāśārtham abhiṣekam ādadhyād (see 153b) iti sambandhaḥ. katham ity āha– yathāvad iti, yathopadeśakramam. viśvadalakamalamadhye candrasthaviśvavajrasthitacandre śrīsamayavajro 'moghasiddhiḥ, tasya yoga ādiyogādibhāvanā, sa yasyāsti sa śrīsamayavajrayogī.
hṛccandre nyastaviśvahṛdā viśvavajrabījena sahitaṃ vajraṃ viśvavajraṃ yena sa hṛccandranyastaviśvahṛdvajraḥ. hṛdayacandravinirmitakhaṃkārajakhaṃkārādhiṣṭhitaviśvavajra ity arthaḥ. prakṛtiviśuddhaṃ sarvaṃ jagad iti dhyātvā
*viśvadalakamalamadhye
*pūrvakramato 'bhiṣekam ādadhyāt |
*bāhye 'pi snānavidhau
*kramo 'yam eva [tu/ca] viśeṣato dhyeyaḥ || 153
153.1 [ 42b2-4] pūrvakramata iti munivaretyādyu(see 61a)ktakrameṇābhiṣekam ādadhyād ātmani bhāvayet. gurvavajñādike tu doṣe vidhim evam anuṣṭhāya sadakṣiṇaṃ bhojyam aparakāryam iti boddhavyam. ayam evābhiṣekakramo bāhye 'pi snānavidhau dhyātavya ity atidiśann āha– bāhye 'pītyādi. viśeṣata iti yathāsvadevatayā.
154.0 [ 42b4] idānīṃ sandhyāntarānuṣṭheyaṃ yogam āha– sandhyāntare 'pīti.
*sandhyāntare 'pi tadvat
*pūjādipuraḥsaraṃ japaṃ kṛtvā | *hṛdyantargatacakraḥ
*kṛpayā prajñānvitaḥ supyāt || 154
154.1 na kevalaṃ prāk pūjādipuraḥsaraṃ japaṃ kṛtavān api tu sandhyāntare'pīty apiśabdaḥ. ayam arthaḥ. dvividho hi yogī, vyagra ekāgraś ca.
154.2 [ 42b4-7] tatra vyagro yogī madhyāhnādisandhyāyām api sukhāsane svadevatācihnādīn ākalayya tryakṣareṇa maṇḍalaṃ visphāryābhiṣekam ādāya tadvat prāgvat pūjādipuraḥsaraṃ japaṃ kṛtvā visarjane jātasambhramaḥ pūjāstutyamṛtāsvādaṃ kṛtvā tryakṣareṇa hṛdyantargatacakraḥ pūrvavad aparam anutiṣṭhed iti śeṣaḥ.
ardharātre tu muḥkārāntatryakṣareṇa jñānacakraṃ muktvā bahiścakram antaścakre praveśya kṛpāprajñānvitaḥ svayaṃ ca supyāt.
154.3 [ 42b7–43a2] ekāgras tu yogī tadvad ādiyogādikrameṇa pūjādipuraḥsaraṃ japaṃ kṛtvā visarjayitukāmaḥ pūjāstutyamṛtāsvādaṃ kṛtvā tryakṣareṇa hṛdyantargatacakraḥ pūrvavad aparam anutiṣṭhet. ardharātre tu tathaiva supyād iti.
155.0 [ 43a2] śayanād utthānasamaye dhyeyam āha–
*utthānakālasamaye
*devīsaṃgīticoditaḥ śrīmān |
*kurvītotthāya munir
*japādikaṃ pūrvavidhinaiva || 155
155.1 [ 43a2-4] utthānakālasya yaḥ samayaḥ prātaḥsandhyā tatra samaye Māmakyādidevīsaṃgīticoditaḥ śrīmān hṛccandrasthabījamātradhārī prākṛtarūpī munir bhāṣyārāmatādikam aprastutam akurvan ādikalpito yogī japādikaṃ kurvīta. pūrvavidhinaiveti pūrvavad ādiyogādividhinā japam athavetyādyuktavidhinā japādikaṃ sampādayed ity arthaḥ.
155.2 [ 43a4-5] kiṃcijjñānavaśī tu devīsaṃgīticoditaḥ prātar utthāya Mañjuvajrarūpī oṃ dharmadhātusvabhāvātmako 'ham iti (see 54') mantram uccārya tataḥ param aparaṃ sarvaṃ vibhāvya tathaiva sandhyāniyamena pūrvavidhinaiva ca japādikaṃ kurvīta.
155.3 [ 43a5-6] prāptajñānavaśī punaḥ sudṛḍhatarābhyāsasundaraḥ sandhyādiniyamakalpanāmuktaḥ sarvavastuparamāṇugataṃ sarvākārasuniṣpannaṃ spharaṇasaṃharaṇātmakaṃ cakraṃ pratikṣaṇaṃ cintayann aniśam eva yogam āśrayet, taṃ prati sandhyādiniyamasya kalpitatvāt.
155.4 [ 43a6-7] samyagjñānavaśī tu sudṛḍhatamābhyāsād anābhogavāhitrisaccakram ātmānaṃ sarvagataṃ kṣaṇapratītimuktaṃ paramasukhākāraṃ dhyāyañ jagatām arthaṃ kurute, atropanatasya pratikṣaṇayogāśrayaṇasya kalpitatvāt.
156.0 [ 43a7–43b1] siddhisādhanavidhānaṃ sūcayann āha– siddhāv iti.
*siddhau labdhanimittas
*tritattvapūjāṃ vidhāya nijacitte |
*pūrvoditacakrasthaḥ
*prasādhayed īhitāṃ siddhim || 156
156.1 [ 43b1-2] kṣudrasiddhau vā mahāsiddhau vā yathāyathaṃ labdhanimittaḥ pañcadaśapaṭalādyuktaṃ praśastadevatāsvapnādinimittaṃ prāptavān tritattvapūjāṃ Kāyavajrādibhāvanāpūjāṃ yathoktāṃ vidhāya nijacitte svacetasi pūrvoditacakrasthaḥ sann īhitām iṣṭāṃ khaḍgādisiddhim ekādaśapaṭalādyuktavidhinā prasādhayet.
156.2 tad anenābhilaṣitasiddhinimittalābhe sambhārapūraṇaṃ tattvapūjādibhiḥ pratipāditam iti boddhavyam.
157.0 [ 43b2-3] siddhisādhanamaṇḍalalikhanādikāle yadi vighno bhavet tadā tannivāraṇavidhim āha– vighnam ityādi.
*vighnam amṛtākhyacakre
*gṛhītaseko nihanty ayatnena |
cakraṃ tu tasya tat syād
yas tanmadhyasthito bhūyāt || 157
157.1 [ 43b3-4] amṛtākhyasyĀmṛtakuṇḍaleś cakre prāgvad gṛhītābhiṣeko vighnaṃ sātmībhūtadevatāyogatvād ayatnena nihanti. kiṃ tad amṛtākhyacakram ity āha– cakraṃ tv iti. maṇḍaladevatācakraṃ tat tasya syāt, yas tasya maṇḍalacakrasya madhye sthito bhūyād iti sādhāraṇo nyāyaḥ.
tasmān madhyasthitatvenādhipatībhūtatvād Vighnāntakasyaiva tac cakraṃ bhavati. tatrādhipadhi(?)vighnārībhūya vighnotsāraṇārtham abhiṣeko vidhivad grāhya ity arthaḥ.
158.0 [ 43b6-7] samprati prakaraṇārtham upasaṃhartukāmo 'syācintyākāradevatācakrākārasya cittasya bhavaduḥkhapratipakṣatvaṃ darśayann āha– prākṛtetyādi.
prākṛtavikalpavṛtter
aparaṃ na hi kiṃcid asti bhavaduḥkham |
tasya viruddhaṃ caitat
sākṣād avagamyate cetaḥ || 158
158.1 [ 43b7–44a1] prākṛtavikalpa ātmātmīyādikalpanā, tasya vṛttiḥ santānenotpattiḥ, tasyā aparam anyad bhavaduḥkhaṃ na kiṃcid asti. saiva bhavaduḥkham iti yāvat. tasya ca prākṛtavikalpasya sākṣād viruddham eva tan maṇḍalacakrākāraṃ gambhīrodārarūpaṃ ceto 'vagamyate, vidhipratiṣedhāyāḥ sākṣādviruddhatvāt.
159.0 [ 44a1-2] viruddhatvam evopapādayann āha– gambhīretyādi.
*gambhīrodārātmani
*tasminn ābhāti yad vikalpo na |
*samvittivyatiriktaṃ
*na cāsti kiṃcit prasādhitaṃ purataḥ || 159
159.1 [ 44a2-3] yad yasmāt tasmin gambhīrodārātmani prajñopāyarūpe maṇḍalacakrākāre mārgasatyātmani cetasi nābhāti na prakāśate bhavaduḥkhasvabhāvaḥ prākṛtavikalpaḥ. anubhava eva pramāṇam ity arthaḥ.
159.2 [ 44a3-5] nanu pratibhāsatvaṃ nāma tathāpi sthāsyatīti ced atrāha– samvittīti. samvitteḥ pratīteḥ samvṛttirūpāyā vyatiriktam anyad ātmādi na ca kiṃcid astīti purato rūpādyākṛtimuktam (see 110a) ityādinā prasādhitam. catuḥkoṭivinirmuktaṃ pratītimātraṃ viśvam iti pratipāditam ity arthaḥ.
159.3 tataś ca samvittivyatiriktasyātmāder abhāve 'samviditasya ca samvedanasyāyogān niyamena gambhīrodārākāracetasi na tadvipakṣasya prākṛtavikalpasya kathaṃcit sambhava iti sthitam etat.
160.0 [ 44a5-6] nanu yady api prākṛtavikalpasya virodhi gambhīrodārākāraṃ cittaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ tathāpi tan nātyantaṃ nivartayiṣyati dveṣādikaṃ maitryādir iveti śaṅkāyām āha– yac cetyādi.
*yac ca viruddhaṃ yena hi
*sakṛd api syāt tad gataprayāsaṃ sat |
*dṛṣṭyabhyāsavivṛddhyā
*svaviruddhātyantasaṃrodhi || 160
160.1 [ 44a6–44b1] yac ca vastu yena saha sakṛd api viruddhaṃ syāt tat sarvaṃ dṛṣṭyā pramāṇaniścitayābhyāsena vivṛddhyā prakarṣagamanena gataprayāsam aprāptaprakarṣaṃ sac chrotriyakāpālikanairghṛṇyādivad ghṛṇādeḥ svaviruddhasyātyantasaṃrodhi. tasmād idaṃ gambhīrodārākāracittaṃ prākṛtavikalpasyātyantanivartakam eveti yuktam.
sattvālambanā maitryādayas tu tāvatkālam uparodhe 'pi dveṣādīn nātyantaṃ nivartayanti, ubhayeṣām api satkāyadṛṣṭimūlatvāt. tāvatkālayāpanam eva maitryādibhir dveṣādīnām analādibhir iva kaphādīnām ādhīyata iti yuktam uktam.
160.2 tad uktam–
ātmagrahaikayonitvāt kāryakāraṇabhāvataḥ ||
rāgapratighayor bādhā bhede 'pi na parasparam |
mohāvirodhān maitryāder nātyantaṃ doṣanigrahaḥ ||
tanmūlāś ca malāḥ sarve sa ca satkāyadarśanam |
(Pramāṇavārttika Pramā° 213cd-215ab)
iti.
161.0 [ 44b1-2] nanu dānādividher eva bhavaduḥkhavirodhī mārgaḥ śreyān asti, tat kiṃ maṇḍalacakrarūpacetasety āśaṅkyāha– prākṛtetyādi.
prākṛtanijarūpebhyo
nairātmyasamanvito 'pi dānādiḥ |
na virudhyate yato 'smin
saty api tan naiti vinivṛttim || 161
161.1 [ 44b2-4] prākṛtasya pṛthagjanasya nijāni svakīyāni rūpāṇi tebhyo hetubhyo dānādir bhavan bhavaduḥkhena saha na virudhyate, nairātmyasamanvito 'pi tathaiva. yato 'smin dānādau saty api tat prākṛtanijarūpabhavaṃ bhavaduḥkhaṃ vinivṛttiṃ na yāti. tad ayam arthaḥ.
upalambhadṛṣṭyānupalambhadṛṣṭyā vā kriyamāṇo dānādir na prākṛtavikalpavṛttivirodhīti.
161.2 athavā pṛthagjanarūpebhyo na virudhyate tāvad dānādir nairātmyasamanvito 'pi ca na bhavati, nairātmyasyaiva sākṣān na virodhas tasyaiva nyāyato nairātmyarūpatvāt. tadabhāve 'bhāvāt, anupalabdhirūpatve 'pi dānādikāryasya niyatadeyāditvāt.
161.3 praṇidhānena vā syād iti cet, tan na, sāpy asākṣād iti. tataś ca pāramitānayo dānādipāramitātmako na samyaksambodheḥ sākṣāt kāraṇam iti na saṃkṣipto nāpy aduṣkaraḥ, tasmāc chīghram eva sadā sarvathā niḥśeṣajagadarthakaraṇakāmaiḥ prajñātiśayaśālibhir upāyakuśalaiḥ kleśeṣv avirodhadarśibhir nipuṇair yuktisātmyena sādhyābheda nudirbhir etad eva
mārgasatyaṃ śīlanīyam iti.
161.4 tathā hi–
na tāvad deyādyupalambhaprabhāvitaḥ kāruṇyaprabhavo 'pi dānādikaḥ kuśalaprabandhaḥ parātmanor avikalpapadaniṣpattaye manāg api kṣamaḥ, viparītādhyavasāyasya svānurūpakalpanodayanibandhanatvāt. tathā cāha–
vinu prajña pañca imu pāramitā acakṣuḥ |
avināyakā na ca bhonti spṛhetu bodhim ||
(Ratnaguṇasaṃcayagāthā 7.1cd)
iti. samastavastunairātmyādhyavasāyas tu svayaṃ vikalpātmakatve 'pi svaviṣayādhyavasāyasvabhāvatayā sakalakalpanābhiḥ pratītinirodha eva. yac ca yenaikadā dṛṣṭavirodhaṃ tad atyantābhyāse svaviruddhasya sarvātmanā nivartakam, jvalanādivac chitādeḥ, anyathā viparītaśaṅkayā sarvatra vyavahārānupapatteḥ. āha cātra–
nirātmadharmān yadi pratyavekṣate
tān pratyavekṣya yadi bhāvayīta |
sa hetu nirvāṇaphalasya prāptaye
yo 'nyahetū na sa bhoti muktaye ||
(Samādhirāja p. xx)
iti. nairātmyābhyāsaś copāyaviśeṣavikalaḥ sakalakalpanākalaṅkavikalam aśeṣajagadupakāri sarvajñajñānam utpādayitum asamartho yatas tasmād upāyātiśayasammukhībhāve prayatitavyam. aśeṣajagadupakāraprabhāvitaṃ hi sarvajñajñānam upakāraś ca niratiśayaudāryaprabhavas taddhetuś copāyābhyāsa eva, nairātmyābhyāsasya vikalpamātraprahāṇaphalatvāt. yad āha–
audāryād api gāmbhīryāt paripāko 'vikalpanā |
deśanāto dvayasyāsmin sa copāyo niruttaraḥ ||
(Mahāyānasūtrālaṃkāra 1.13)
iti.
tad astv aviparyastasya dānādicetanāvṛttir upāyaḥ, sarvajñatāpariṇāme sati tasyāḥ sambodhihetubhāvāt. tathā coktam–
dānaṃ ca śīlam api kṣānti tathaivaṃ vīryaṃ dhyānāni prajñā pariṇāmayitāgrabodhau | na ca bodhiskandha nivimitta (!) parāmṛṣeyā ye ādikarmika na deśayitavya evam ||
(Prajñāpāramitāsaṃcayagāthā 131/15.3)
iti. naitad evam, visadṛśahetvabhyāsasya dhiḥ sambuddhapadaprāptihetuḥ. yad āha–
kathaṃ tvaṃ kulaputrānuttarāṃ samyaksambodhim abhisambhotsyase, yas tvaṃ sarvatathāgatatattvānabhijñatayā sarvaduṣkarān utsahasi. (Sarvatathāgatatattvasaṃgraha 1,23,18)
iti. tasmān nairātmyavad audāryam api svasvabhāvāvyatibhinnam eva bhāvayet.
nanv avidyamānam audāryaṃ bhāvyamānam api kathaṃ samīhitaphalasampattaye prabhavatīti cet, tan na, abhūtasyāpi bhāvanābhyāsena pṛthivīkṛtsnādivat parisphuṭapratibhāsahetutvāt.
kathaṃ cāsyābhūtatvaṃ buddhavigrahākārarahite svātmani tadrūpāropapravṛttyādhimuktimanaskāratvād iti cet, tattvamanaskārasyāpi tarhi na bhūtatvam, yathātattvam apravṛtteḥ. nirvikalpaikalakṣaṇaṃ hi tattvaṃ na ca tathā tanmanaskāraḥ, tasyādikarmikāvasthāyāṃ vikalparūpatayā pravṛtteḥ.
bhavatu nāma savikakṛtasvabhāvo na ca tadrūpāvabhāsī prabhā cātiśayaśālisaugatākāragocaro manaskāraḥ.
yady anudārātmani svarūpe na kiṃcid ākārāntaram āropayati, tat katham asau tasya pratipakṣa iti cet, saugatarūpāvabhāsino manaskārasya svayam aprākṛtasvabhāvatayotpatteḥ, bhāvanāvaśena ca nirantaraparisphuṭākārasajātīyapratyayotpattihetubhāvāc ca. na codārapratyayodayakāle tatrāparaḥ prākṛtākāragocaraḥ pratyayo 'nubhūyate, na cāpratīyamānam api.
tad asty eva prākṛtaṃ svarūpam iti śakyam avasātum, tasya pratītimātrasvabhāvatvād iti nirṇītaprāyam.
tasmān nirastasaṃkalpaṃ
samantaspharaṇatviṣam | Samantabhadram ātmānaṃ
bhāvayann eva bodhibhāk ||
yad āha–
yena mudrāvidhānena
kalpāsaṃkhyeyakoṭibhiḥ |
aprāpyaṃ sarvabuddhatvaṃ
prāpyatehaiva janmani ||
tad ahaṃ kalpayiṣyāmi
(Sarvabuddhasamāyogaḍākinījālaśaṃvara 1.18-19a)
yāvat
svādhidaivatayogena tasmād ātmaiva bhāvayet ||
(Sarvabuddhasamāyogaḍākinījālaśaṃvara 1.24cd)
iti. evaṃbhūtamanaskārāpekṣayaivaṃ niṣpannajñānātiśayair bhagavadbhir aniṣpannasyāpi tattvayoginaḥ sāmyam upavarṇyate, sambuddhasvabhāvādhimukter niṣpannayogibhir bhedāparāmarśāt. tatparāmarśe hi na sugatātmamanaskāraḥ syāt, bhagavatāṃ praṇa(?)bhedādhyavasāyatvāt. na cānyākāreṣu bhagavatsu tadvilakṣaṇasvarūpādhyavasāyī vikalpo niravadyaphalaḥ syāt.
tan niveśya jinamūrtim anantām
ātmanīva manasāmaladehaḥ | sarvabuddhasadṛśottamabuddhiḥ
syād aśeṣajanavāñchitasiddhyai ||
taṃ ca sphuradamitamayūkhamukhamālinam anantasvaparārthasampadāśrayatvena paramopāyabhūtam ātmānaṃ prajñājñānāvirahakātaram āvaprajñapter iti.
prajñāṅganopagūḍhaṃ
paramaratāsvādasampadaṃ samyak | tadupāyabhūtam aniśaṃ
svaṃ bhāvaṃ bhāvayed dhīmān ||
tadanu–
prajñopāyaprabhavair
abhedam ālambya nirmitaprasaraiḥ |
ābhāsasthitiviṣayair
viṣayaiḥ sampūjayet svatanum ||
yathoktakrameṇa prajñopāyātmakaṃ svarūpam āmukhīkurvan yāvanti nirmāṇāni tadvaśitvaniṣpattaye 'bhimukhīkuryāt, tāvatām ātmasvarūpeṇābhinnarūpatvam anujñeyam, aviparyastasvarūpotpattiyogyatve prajñopāyātmakatvenaikalakṣaṇatvāt, abhinnasantatisvabhāvatvāc ca.
pūrvapūrvasaṃkalpaprabhūtakalpanāprabandho hi santānaḥ, na ca nirmāṇaṃ nāmānyad eva tatsaṃkalpamātrāt.
varṇādibhedena nānāsvabhāvatvāt katham ekatvam iti cet, evaṃ tarhi na kvacid ekatvaṃ syāt, ekadevatāmūrter apy anekasvabhāvatayā nānātmakatvāt, niravayavasya cānupalabdher iti. ekaikas tu yogināṃ svacittodayaḥ samastavipakṣavyāvṛttarūpatayā sakalamāṇḍaleyadevatāsvabhāvaḥ pratyavekṣaṇīyaḥ, yathāsvaṃ samādhīnāṃ tattadviparyayānupalambhasūcakatvāt.
(Ātmasādhanāvatāra 53b6–57a4)
tat sthitam etad idam evāsādhāraṇasamyaksambodhikāraṇam iti.
161.5 [ 44b7–45a2] yad vā prākṛtāni prakṛtibhavanāni, ata eva nijāni yāni cittasya rūpāṇi prakṛtiprabhāsvaraśūnyatādilakṣaṇā incipit pravartamāno 'pi dānādir devatācakrākāracetasā saha na virudhyate, nairātmyasamanvita eveti kṛtvā na virudhyata ity arthaḥ.
161.6 ayam abhiprāyaḥ. nairātmyasamanvitatve hi kalpanāmātrasvabhāvatvaṃ tataś cecchāmātrādhīnatvena Rūpavajrādidevīrūpatvāt tadātmataiveti kathaṃ tadvirodhaḥ? tato niḥśeṣavyavadānasaṃketadarśitam iti.
161.7 [ 45a2-3] idam eva spaṣṭayati. yato 'smin maṇḍalacakrākāre prajñopāyasamāpattirūpe cetasi saty api tad dānaśīlādivrataṃ vinivṛttiṃ naiti.
162.0 [ 45a3] itītyādinā sāmpratam upasaṃharati.
*ity avagamya dayāvāñ
*jhaṭitijñānaprasādhitaṃ cetaḥ | *śrīmatsamantabhadraṃ
*nijarūpaṃ ko na vai dhyāyāt || 162
162.1 [ 45a3-6] ity anantaroktam artham avagamya jhaṭiti saṃkṣepeṇa prajñopāyajñānena prasādhitaṃ siddhavadbhūtanirdeśenānāyāsasādhyatāṃ bodhayati. śīghram anāyāsena sādhayituṃ śakyam ity arthaḥ.
niḥśeṣasvārthasampadādhāratvena śrīmantaṃ samantaṃ niḥśeṣaṃ bhadraṃ kalyāṇaṃ yathāyogaṃ vineyānām asmād bhavatīti parārthasampattyāśrayatvena samantabhadraṃ sarveṣāṃ svapratibhāsarūpatvena nijarūpaṃ ko dayāvāñ janahitakāmo na dhyāyāt. vaiśabdo 'kṣāntau. na kṣamyata etad yat kaścid dhīmān na dhyāyād iti.
163.0 [ 45a6] sampraty ācāryaḥ svakīyam auddhatyaṃ parijihīrṣur āha– amaletyādi.
*amalamatimayūkhāpāstasarvāndhakārair
*viracitum alam etan mādṛśaḥ ko 'vakāśaḥ |
*gurujanamatam āttaṃ yat tu kiṃcit tad ādau
*ślathayati bhavabandhaṃ yat tato 'yaṃ prayāsaḥ || 163
163.1 [ 45a6–45b1] amalamataya eva mayūkhās tair apāstasarvamohāndhakārair mahadbhir etan Mañjuvajrasya sādhanaṃ viracituṃ kartum alaṃ śakyam.
mādṛśas tu ko 'trāvakāśaḥ? tathāpi yat tu kiṃcid gurujanamatam āttaṃ gṛhītaṃ tat kila sādhanakaraṇadvāreṇābhyasyamānam ādau prathamaṃ gāḍhabhavabandhaṃ ślathayati yad yasmāt tato 'yaṃ sādhanaracanāprayāso na tu jñānābhimāneneti śeṣaḥ.
164.0 [ 45b1-2] idānīṃ sādhanaracanāsambhūtapuṇyapariṇāmanāyāha– itīti.
*iti racanāsambhūtaṃ
*śubham amalaṃ yaj jaganmanas tena |
*buddhajñānasanāthaṃ
*tannimnam adaḥ sadā dhyāyāt || 164
164.1 [ 45b2-3] etatsādhanaracanāsambhūtaṃ yad amalaṃ śubhaṃ tena śubhena jagatāṃ mano yathoktaṃ buddhajñānasanāthaṃ tannimnaṃ tatpravaṇaṃ sad ado devatācakraṃ sadā dhyāyād iti.
Closing verse
śrīmanMañjugiro niruttaramahākāruṇyaratnākara-
prajñāyās tu samastakalpavisarajñānasya + + jikam ,|
pūrṇasādhanam āvibhajya vipulaṃ prāptaṃ mayā yac chubhaṃ bhūyāt tena jano nirantararatir viśvākṛtir mārajit ||
Colophon [ 45b3] Sāramañjarī nāma Caturaṅgasādhanasya ṭīkā samāptā. kṛtir iyam ācāryaśrīSamantabhadrapādānām iti. explicit

ye dharmā hetuprabhavā hetuṃ teṣān tathāgato hy avadat teṣāṃ ca yo nirodha evamvādī mahāśramaṇaḥ + + doṣa iti || ||
